## `app/gui/main_window.py`

```python
"""
app/gui/main_window.py
=======================
FAMILYFIN — Main Application Shell

Layout:
  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │  TOP BAR  (logo | breadcrumb | user | theme toggle) │
  ├──────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │          │                                          │
  │ SIDEBAR  │           CONTENT AREA                  │
  │ (nav     │     (swapped by nav selection)          │
  │  tree)   │                                          │
  │          │                                          │
  └──────────┴──────────────────────────────────────────┘
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from typing import Optional

from PySide6.QtCore    import Qt, QSettings, QSize, QTimer
from PySide6.QtGui     import QFont, QIcon
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget, QHBoxLayout, QVBoxLayout, QLabel,
    QPushButton, QFrame, QStackedWidget, QSizePolicy,
    QScrollArea, QApplication,
)
import qtawesome as qta

from app.config      import APP_NAME, APP_VERSION, THEMES, NAV_ITEMS, NAV_ITEMS_TOP, NAV_ITEMS_BOTTOM
from app.db.models   import User
from app.settings    import SettingsManager
from sqlalchemy.orm  import Session


class _NavButton(QPushButton):
    """Sidebar navigation button."""

    def __init__(self, label: str, icon_name: str,
                 view_key: str, theme: dict) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.view_key  = view_key
        self._label    = label
        self._icon_name = icon_name
        self._active   = False

        self.setFixedHeight(44)
        self.setCheckable(False)
        self.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding,
                           QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)
        self.setFlat(True)
        self._refresh(theme)

    def set_active(self, active: bool, theme: dict) -> None:
        self._active = active
        self._refresh(theme)

    def _refresh(self, theme: dict) -> None:
        colour = (theme["text_sidebar_active"]
                  if self._active else theme["text_sidebar"])
        bg     = (f"background-color: {theme['bg_sidebar_hover']};"
                  if self._active else "background-color: transparent;")
        border = (f"border-left: 3px solid {theme['accent']};"
                  if self._active else "border-left: 3px solid transparent;")

        self.setIcon(qta.icon(self._icon_name, color=colour))
        self.setIconSize(QSize(20, 20))
        self.setText(f"  {self._label}")
        self.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QPushButton {{
                {bg}
                {border}
                color: {colour};
                text-align: left;
                padding: 0 16px;
                font-size: 13px;
                font-weight: {"600" if self._active else "400"};
                border-radius: 0px;
            }}
            QPushButton:hover {{
                background-color: {theme["bg_sidebar_hover"]};
            }}
        """)


class MainWindow(QWidget):
    """
    Main application window — fullscreen shell with sidebar navigation.
    """

    SETTINGS_ORG = "GEVernova"
    SETTINGS_APP = "FAMILYFIN"
    KEY_THEME    = "ui/theme"
    KEY_NAV      = "ui/last_nav"
    AUTO_BACKUP_CHECK_MS = 30 * 60 * 1000

    def __init__(self, user: User, session: Session,
                 theme: str = "light") -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self._user    = user
        self._session = session
        stored_settings = SettingsManager().load()
        default_theme = stored_settings.theme if stored_settings.save_appearance_on_start else "light"
        selected_theme = theme if theme != "light" else default_theme
        self._theme   = selected_theme if selected_theme in THEMES else "light"
        self._t       = THEMES[self._theme]
        self._settings = QSettings(self.SETTINGS_ORG, self.SETTINGS_APP)
        self._nav_btns: dict[str, _NavButton] = {}
        self._current_view = ""

        self.setWindowTitle(f"{APP_NAME}  v{APP_VERSION}")
        self.setWindowFlag(Qt.WindowType.FramelessWindowHint)
        self.showMaximized()

        self._build_ui()
        self._apply_theme()

        # Navigate to last view or default dashboard
        last_nav = self._settings.value(self.KEY_NAV, "dashboard")
        self._navigate(last_nav)

        # Automatic backups: check now, then periodically while the app runs.
        self._auto_backup_timer = QTimer(self)
        self._auto_backup_timer.setInterval(self.AUTO_BACKUP_CHECK_MS)
        self._auto_backup_timer.timeout.connect(self._run_auto_backup)
        self._auto_backup_timer.start()
        QTimer.singleShot(0, self._run_auto_backup)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # UI construction
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        root = QVBoxLayout(self)
        root.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        root.setSpacing(0)

        # ── Top bar ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        self._top_bar = self._make_top_bar()
        root.addWidget(self._top_bar)

        # ── Body (sidebar + content) ───────────────────────────────────
        body = QWidget()
        body_layout = QHBoxLayout(body)
        body_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        body_layout.setSpacing(0)

        self._sidebar   = self._make_sidebar()
        self._stack     = QStackedWidget()
        self._stack.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding,
                                   QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding)

        body_layout.addWidget(self._sidebar)
        body_layout.addWidget(self._stack)
        root.addWidget(body)

        # ── Populate stacked views ─────────────────────────────────────
        self._views: dict[str, QWidget] = {}
        self._register_views()

    def _make_top_bar(self) -> QFrame:
        """
        Minimal modern top bar:
        [☰] [● FAMILYFIN  v0.1.x] ── [breadcrumb] ──────── [Pedro ▾] [🌙] [⏻]
        Height: 48px. Single accent-colored bottom border. No visual clutter.
        """
        bar = QFrame()
        bar.setFixedHeight(48)
        bar.setObjectName("topBar")
        layout = QHBoxLayout(bar)
        layout.setContentsMargins(4, 0, 12, 0)
        layout.setSpacing(0)

        # ── Sidebar toggle ────────────────────────────────────────────
        self._btn_menu = QPushButton()
        self._btn_menu.setFixedSize(48, 48)
        self._btn_menu.setFlat(True)
        self._btn_menu.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self._btn_menu.setToolTip("Mostrar / ocultar menú lateral")
        self._btn_menu.clicked.connect(self._toggle_sidebar)
        layout.addWidget(self._btn_menu)

        layout.addSpacing(4)

        # ── Logo dot + brand + version (single compact group) ────────
        lbl_logo = QLabel()
        lbl_logo.setObjectName("topBarLogo")
        lbl_logo.setFixedSize(18, 18)
        self._lbl_topbar_logo = lbl_logo
        layout.addWidget(lbl_logo)

        layout.addSpacing(6)

        lbl_brand = QLabel(f"{APP_NAME}  ")
        lbl_brand.setObjectName("topBarBrand")
        layout.addWidget(lbl_brand)

        lbl_ver = QLabel(f"v{APP_VERSION}")
        lbl_ver.setObjectName("topBarVersion")
        layout.addWidget(lbl_ver)

        layout.addSpacing(20)

        # ── Breadcrumb (current view name) ────────────────────────────
        self._lbl_breadcrumb = QLabel("Posición Global")
        self._lbl_breadcrumb.setObjectName("breadcrumb")
        layout.addWidget(self._lbl_breadcrumb)

        layout.addStretch()

        # ── User chip (avatar dot + name, no extra separator) ─────────
        initials = self._user.username[:2].upper()
        lbl_av = QLabel(initials)
        lbl_av.setObjectName("topBarAvatar")
        lbl_av.setFixedSize(26, 26)
        lbl_av.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        layout.addWidget(lbl_av)

        layout.addSpacing(5)

        self._lbl_user = QLabel(self._user.username.capitalize())
        self._lbl_user.setObjectName("topBarUsername")
        layout.addWidget(self._lbl_user)

        layout.addSpacing(12)

        # ── Theme toggle ──────────────────────────────────────────────
        self._btn_theme = QPushButton()
        self._btn_theme.setFixedSize(32, 32)
        self._btn_theme.setFlat(True)
        self._btn_theme.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self._btn_theme.setToolTip("Cambiar tema claro / oscuro")
        self._btn_theme.clicked.connect(self._toggle_theme)
        layout.addWidget(self._btn_theme)

        layout.addSpacing(2)

        # ── Logout ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        self._btn_logout = QPushButton()
        self._btn_logout.setFixedSize(32, 32)
        self._btn_logout.setFlat(True)
        self._btn_logout.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self._btn_logout.setToolTip("Cerrar sesión")
        self._btn_logout.clicked.connect(self._on_logout)
        layout.addWidget(self._btn_logout)

        layout.addSpacing(16)

        # ── Window controls (frameless) ───────────────────────────────
        sep_wc = QFrame()
        sep_wc.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.VLine)
        sep_wc.setFixedHeight(20)
        sep_wc.setObjectName("topBarSepV")
        layout.addWidget(sep_wc)

        layout.addSpacing(8)

        btn_min = QPushButton()
        btn_min.setFixedSize(28, 28)
        btn_min.setFlat(True)
        btn_min.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_min.setToolTip("Minimizar")
        btn_min.setObjectName("wctrlMin")
        btn_min.setIcon(qta.icon("mdi.minus", color=self._t["text_secondary"]))
        btn_min.clicked.connect(self.showMinimized)
        layout.addWidget(btn_min)

        self._btn_maximize = QPushButton()
        self._btn_maximize.setFixedSize(28, 28)
        self._btn_maximize.setFlat(True)
        self._btn_maximize.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self._btn_maximize.setToolTip("Restaurar / Maximizar")
        self._btn_maximize.setObjectName("wctrlMax")
        self._btn_maximize.setIcon(qta.icon("mdi.crop-square", color=self._t["text_secondary"]))
        self._btn_maximize.clicked.connect(self._toggle_maximize)
        layout.addWidget(self._btn_maximize)

        btn_close = QPushButton()
        btn_close.setFixedSize(28, 28)
        btn_close.setFlat(True)
        btn_close.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_close.setToolTip("Cerrar aplicación")
        btn_close.setObjectName("wctrlClose")
        btn_close.setIcon(qta.icon("mdi.close", color=self._t["text_secondary"]))
        btn_close.clicked.connect(self.close)
        layout.addWidget(btn_close)

        layout.addSpacing(4)

        # ── Drag support for frameless window ─────────────────────────
        bar._drag_pos = None

        def _bar_mouse_press(e):
            if e.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
                bar._drag_pos = (
                    e.globalPosition().toPoint() - self.frameGeometry().topLeft()
                )
        def _bar_mouse_move(e):
            if bar._drag_pos and e.buttons() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
                if self.isMaximized():
                    self.showNormal()
                    bar._drag_pos = (
                        e.globalPosition().toPoint() - self.frameGeometry().topLeft()
                    )
                self.move(e.globalPosition().toPoint() - bar._drag_pos)
        def _bar_mouse_release(e):
            bar._drag_pos = None
        def _bar_double_click(e):
            self._toggle_maximize()

        bar.mousePressEvent   = _bar_mouse_press
        bar.mouseMoveEvent    = _bar_mouse_move
        bar.mouseReleaseEvent = _bar_mouse_release
        bar.mouseDoubleClickEvent = _bar_double_click

        return bar

    def _make_sidebar(self) -> QFrame:
        sidebar = QFrame()
        sidebar.setObjectName("sidebar")
        sidebar.setFixedWidth(220)

        layout = QVBoxLayout(sidebar)
        layout.setContentsMargins(0, 8, 0, 8)
        layout.setSpacing(2)

        # User avatar area
        avatar_area = QFrame()
        avatar_area.setObjectName("sidebarUserCard")
        avatar_area.setFixedHeight(72)
        avatar_layout = QHBoxLayout(avatar_area)
        avatar_layout.setContentsMargins(16, 8, 16, 8)

        lbl_avatar = QLabel()
        lbl_avatar.setFixedSize(36, 36)
        lbl_avatar.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        lbl_avatar.setObjectName("avatarCircle")
        initials = self._user.username[:2].upper()
        lbl_avatar.setText(initials)
        avatar_layout.addWidget(lbl_avatar)

        avatar_layout.addSpacing(8)

        user_info = QVBoxLayout()
        user_info.setSpacing(1)
        lbl_name = QLabel(self._user.username.capitalize())
        lbl_name.setObjectName("sidebarUsername")
        lbl_role = QLabel("Personal")
        lbl_role.setObjectName("sidebarRole")
        user_info.addWidget(lbl_name)
        user_info.addWidget(lbl_role)
        avatar_layout.addLayout(user_info)

        avatar_layout.addStretch()

        # Account settings button
        self._btn_account = QPushButton()
        self._btn_account.setFixedSize(28, 28)
        self._btn_account.setFlat(True)
        self._btn_account.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self._btn_account.setToolTip("Configuración de cuenta")
        self._btn_account.clicked.connect(self._on_account_settings)
        avatar_layout.addWidget(self._btn_account)

        layout.addWidget(avatar_area)

        # Separator
        sep = QFrame()
        sep.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.HLine)
        sep.setObjectName("sidebarSep")
        layout.addWidget(sep)
        layout.addSpacing(4)

        # ── Top nav section ───────────────────────────────────────────
        for label, icon_name, view_key in NAV_ITEMS_TOP:
            btn = _NavButton(label, icon_name, view_key, self._t)
            btn.clicked.connect(
                lambda checked=False, k=view_key: self._navigate(k)
            )
            layout.addWidget(btn)
            self._nav_btns[view_key] = btn

        # ── Flexible spacer pushes bottom section down ────────────────
        layout.addStretch()

        # ── Bottom section separator ──────────────────────────────────
        sep2 = QFrame()
        sep2.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.HLine)
        sep2.setObjectName("sidebarSep")
        layout.addWidget(sep2)
        layout.addSpacing(4)

        # ── Bottom nav section (tools) ────────────────────────────────
        for label, icon_name, view_key in NAV_ITEMS_BOTTOM:
            btn = _NavButton(label, icon_name, view_key, self._t)
            btn.clicked.connect(
                lambda checked=False, k=view_key: self._navigate(k)
            )
            layout.addWidget(btn)
            self._nav_btns[view_key] = btn

        layout.addSpacing(8)

        # ── Version at very bottom ────────────────────────────────────
        lbl_ver = QLabel(f"v{APP_VERSION}")
        lbl_ver.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        lbl_ver.setObjectName("sidebarVersion")
        layout.addWidget(lbl_ver)

        return sidebar

    def _register_views(self) -> None:
        """Register a factory per view; each widget is built on first visit.

        Building every view at login ran all of their DB queries up front.
        Views are now created (with the theme current at that moment) the
        first time the user navigates to them, then kept in the stack.
        """
        from app.gui.dashboard         import DashboardView
        from app.gui.import_view       import ImportView
        from app.gui.transactions_view import TransactionsView
        from app.gui.categories_view   import CategoriesView
        from app.gui.rules_view        import RulesView
        from app.gui.expenses_view     import ExpensesView
        from app.gui.incomes_window    import IncomesWindow
        from app.gui.settings_view      import SettingsView

        self._view_factories = {
            "dashboard":    lambda: DashboardView(
                                self._user, self._session, self._theme
                            ),
            "expenses":     lambda: ExpensesView(
                                self._session, self._user, self._theme
                            ),
            "income":       lambda: IncomesWindow(
                                self._user, self._session, self._theme
                            ),
            "investments":  lambda: _PlaceholderView(
                                "Inversiones", "mdi.chart-line", self._t
                            ),
            "transactions": lambda: TransactionsView(
                                self._user, self._session, self._theme
                            ),
            "import":       lambda: ImportView(
                                self._user, self._session, self._theme
                            ),
            "categories":   lambda: CategoriesView(
                                self._user, self._session, self._theme
                            ),
            "rules":        lambda: RulesView(
                                self._user, self._session, self._theme
                            ),
            "forecast":     lambda: _PlaceholderView(
                                "Previsiones",
                                "mdi.calendar-clock-outline", self._t
                            ),
            "export":       lambda: _PlaceholderView(
                                "Exportar", "mdi.download-outline", self._t
                            ),
            "settings":     lambda: SettingsView(
                                self._user, self._session, self._theme
                            ),
        }


    def _ensure_view(self, view_key: str) -> QWidget:
        """Return the view for view_key, building it on first use."""
        widget = self._views.get(view_key)
        if widget is None:
            widget = self._view_factories[view_key]()
            self._stack.addWidget(widget)
            self._views[view_key] = widget
        return widget

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Navigation
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _navigate(self, view_key: str) -> None:
        if view_key not in self._view_factories:
            view_key = "dashboard"

        # Update sidebar active state
        if self._current_view and self._current_view in self._nav_btns:
            self._nav_btns[self._current_view].set_active(False, self._t)
        if view_key in self._nav_btns:
            self._nav_btns[view_key].set_active(True, self._t)

        # Update breadcrumb
        label = next(
            (lbl for lbl, _, k in NAV_ITEMS if k == view_key),
            view_key.capitalize()
        )
        self._lbl_breadcrumb.setText(label)

        self._stack.setCurrentWidget(self._ensure_view(view_key))
        self._current_view = view_key
        self._settings.setValue(self.KEY_NAV, view_key)

    def _toggle_maximize(self) -> None:
        if self.isMaximized():
            self.showNormal()
            self._btn_maximize.setIcon(
                qta.icon("mdi.crop-square", color=self._t["text_secondary"])
            )
            self._btn_maximize.setToolTip("Maximizar")
        else:
            self.showMaximized()
            self._btn_maximize.setIcon(
                qta.icon("mdi.window-restore", color=self._t["text_secondary"])
            )
            self._btn_maximize.setToolTip("Restaurar")

    def _toggle_sidebar(self) -> None:
        visible = self._sidebar.isVisible()
        self._sidebar.setVisible(not visible)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Theme
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _toggle_theme(self) -> None:
        self._theme = "dark" if self._theme == "light" else "light"
        self._t     = THEMES[self._theme]
        self._settings.setValue(self.KEY_THEME, self._theme)
        self._apply_theme()

        # Propagate theme to ALL registered views
        for key, view in self._views.items():
            # DashboardView uses refresh_theme(str)
            if hasattr(view, "refresh_theme"):
                view.refresh_theme(self._theme)
            # TransactionsView, ExpensesView, CategoriesView,
            # RulesView, ImportView use apply_theme(str)
            elif hasattr(view, "apply_theme"):
                view.apply_theme(self._theme)

    def _apply_theme(self) -> None:
        t = self._t
        is_dark = self._theme == "dark"

        icon_color = t["text_primary"]
        self._btn_menu.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.menu", color=t["text_sidebar_active"])
        )
        self._btn_menu.setIconSize(QSize(22, 22))

        theme_icon = ("mdi.weather-night"
                      if self._theme == "light" else "mdi.weather-sunny")
        self._btn_theme.setIcon(qta.icon(theme_icon, color=icon_color))
        self._btn_theme.setIconSize(QSize(20, 20))

        self._btn_logout.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.logout", color=icon_color)
        )
        self._btn_logout.setIconSize(QSize(20, 20))

        self._btn_account.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.dots-vertical", color=self._t["text_sidebar"])
        )
        self._btn_account.setIconSize(QSize(16, 16))

        # Refresh nav buttons
        for key, btn in self._nav_btns.items():
            btn._refresh(t)

        # Refresh logo icon (accent color changes with theme)
        self._lbl_topbar_logo.setPixmap(
            qta.icon("mdi.chart-areaspline",
                     color=t["accent"]).pixmap(26, 26)
        )

        self.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QWidget {{
                background-color: {t["bg_primary"]};
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-family: "Segoe UI";
                font-size: 13px;
            }}
                        QPushButton#wctrlMin:hover,
            QPushButton#wctrlMax:hover {{
                background-color: {t["bg_hover"]};
                border-radius: 6px;
            }}
            QPushButton#wctrlClose:hover {{
                background-color: #EF4444;
                border-radius: 6px;
            }}
            QPushButton#wctrlClose:hover {{
                /* icon color via setIcon no cambia con CSS — ok */
            }}
            QFrame#topBarSepV {{
                color: {t["border"]};
                background-color: {t["border"]};
                max-width: 1px;
            }}
            QFrame#topBar {{
                background: qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:1, y2:0,
                    stop:0 {t["bg_secondary"]},
                    stop:1 {t["bg_primary"]});
                border-bottom: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-top: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.25);
            }}
            QLabel#topBarBrand {{
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 13px;
                font-weight: 700;
            }}
            QLabel#topBarVersion {{
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 11px;
                font-weight: 400;
            }}
            QLabel#breadcrumb {{
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 12px;
            }}
            QLabel#topBarAvatar {{
                background-color: {t["accent"]};
                color: #FFFFFF;
                border-radius: 13px;
                font-size: 10px;
                font-weight: 700;
            }}
            QLabel#topBarUsername {{
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 12px;
                font-weight: 600;
            }}
            QFrame#sidebar {{
                background: qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:1, y2:1,
                    stop:0 {t["bg_sidebar"]},
                    stop:1 {t["bg_sidebar"]});
                border-right: 1px solid {t["border"]};
            }}
            QFrame#sidebarUserCard {{
                background-color: {t["bg_sidebar_card"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["bg_sidebar_hover"]};
                border-radius: 12px;
            }}
            QLabel#avatarCircle {{
                background-color: {t["accent"]};
                color: #FFFFFF;
                border-radius: 18px;
                font-size: 13px;
                font-weight: 700;
            }}
            QLabel#sidebarUsername {{
                color: {t["text_sidebar_active"]};
                font-size: 13px;
                font-weight: 700;
            }}
            QLabel#sidebarRole {{
                color: {t["text_sidebar_muted"]};
                font-size: 11px;
            }}
            QFrame#sidebarSep {{
                color: {t["border"]};
                background-color: {t["border"]};
                max-height: 1px;
            }}
            QLabel#sidebarVersion {{
                color: {t["text_sidebar_muted"]};
                font-size: 11px;
            }}
        """)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Slots
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _run_auto_backup(self) -> None:
        """Create an automatic backup if one is due. Never interrupts the user."""
        try:
            SettingsManager().run_auto_backup_if_due(user_name=self._user.username)
        except Exception:
            logging.getLogger(__name__).exception(
                "No se pudo crear la copia de seguridad automática."
            )

    def _on_logout(self) -> None:
        self._session.close()
        from app.gui.login_window import LoginWindow
        self._login = LoginWindow()
        self._login.show()
        self.close()

    def _on_account_settings(self) -> None:
        """Open account settings dialog with delete account option."""
        from PySide6.QtWidgets import QDialog, QDialogButtonBox

        t = self._t

        dlg = QDialog(self)
        dlg.setWindowTitle("Configuración de cuenta")
        dlg.setFixedWidth(380)
        dlg.setModal(True)

        layout = QVBoxLayout(dlg)
        layout.setContentsMargins(28, 28, 28, 28)
        layout.setSpacing(16)

        # Header — avatar + username
        header = QHBoxLayout()
        lbl_av = QLabel(self._user.username[:2].upper())
        lbl_av.setFixedSize(48, 48)
        lbl_av.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        lbl_av.setStyleSheet(f"""
            background-color: {t["accent"]};
            color: #FFFFFF;
            border-radius: 24px;
            font-size: 16px;
            font-weight: 700;
        """)
        header.addWidget(lbl_av)
        header.addSpacing(12)

        info = QVBoxLayout()
        lbl_uname = QLabel(self._user.username.capitalize())
        lbl_uname.setStyleSheet(
            f"font-size: 15px; font-weight: 700; color: {t['text_primary']};"
        )
        lbl_joined = QLabel(
            f"Cuenta creada: "
            f"{self._user.created_at.strftime('%d %b %Y')}"
            if self._user.created_at else "Cuenta activa"
        )
        lbl_joined.setStyleSheet(
            f"font-size: 11px; color: {t['text_secondary']};"
        )
        info.addWidget(lbl_uname)
        info.addWidget(lbl_joined)
        header.addLayout(info)
        header.addStretch()
        layout.addLayout(header)

        # Separator
        sep = QFrame()
        sep.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.HLine)
        sep.setStyleSheet(f"color: {t['border']};")
        layout.addWidget(sep)

        # Delete account button
        btn_delete = QPushButton(
            "  Eliminar cuenta permanentemente"
        )
        btn_delete.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.account-remove-outline", color="#FFFFFF")
        )
        btn_delete.setFixedHeight(42)
        btn_delete.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_delete.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QPushButton {{
                background-color: {t["expense"]};
                color: #FFFFFF;
                border: none;
                border-radius: 8px;
                font-size: 13px;
                font-weight: 600;
                padding: 0 16px;
                text-align: left;
            }}
            QPushButton:hover {{
                background-color: #B91C1C;
            }}
        """)
        layout.addWidget(btn_delete)

        lbl_warn = QLabel(
            "Se eliminarán todos tus datos: transacciones, cuentas,\n"
            "categorías y reglas. Esta acción no se puede deshacer."
        )
        lbl_warn.setStyleSheet(
            f"font-size: 11px; color: {t['text_secondary']};"
        )
        lbl_warn.setWordWrap(True)
        layout.addWidget(lbl_warn)

        # Close button
        sep2 = QFrame()
        sep2.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.HLine)
        sep2.setStyleSheet(f"color: {t['border']};")
        layout.addWidget(sep2)

        btn_close = QPushButton("Cerrar")
        btn_close.setFixedHeight(38)
        btn_close.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_close.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QPushButton {{
                background-color: transparent;
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 8px;
                font-size: 13px;
            }}
            QPushButton:hover {{
                background-color: {t["bg_hover"]};
            }}
        """)
        btn_close.clicked.connect(dlg.reject)
        layout.addWidget(btn_close)

        dlg.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QDialog {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
            }}
        """)

        def _on_delete_clicked() -> None:
            dlg.accept()
            self._confirm_delete_account()

        btn_delete.clicked.connect(_on_delete_clicked)
        dlg.exec()

    def _confirm_delete_account(self) -> None:
        """
        Two-step confirmation dialog before permanently deleting the account.
        Step 1: Warning + confirm button.
        Step 2: Type username to confirm.
        """
        from PySide6.QtWidgets import QDialog, QLineEdit

        t = self._t
        username = self._user.username

        dlg = QDialog(self)
        dlg.setWindowTitle("Confirmar eliminación de cuenta")
        dlg.setFixedWidth(420)
        dlg.setModal(True)

        layout = QVBoxLayout(dlg)
        layout.setContentsMargins(28, 28, 28, 28)
        layout.setSpacing(14)

        # Warning icon + title
        lbl_icon = QLabel()
        lbl_icon.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        lbl_icon.setPixmap(
            qta.icon("mdi.alert-circle-outline",
                     color=t["expense"]).pixmap(48, 48)
        )
        layout.addWidget(lbl_icon)

        lbl_title = QLabel("¿Eliminar cuenta definitivamente?")
        lbl_title.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        lbl_title.setStyleSheet(
            f"font-size: 15px; font-weight: 700; color: {t['text_primary']};"
        )
        layout.addWidget(lbl_title)

        lbl_msg = QLabel(
            f"Vas a eliminar la cuenta <b>{username}</b> y "
            f"<b>todos sus datos</b>.\n\n"
            f"Para confirmar, escribe tu nombre de usuario:"
        )
        lbl_msg.setWordWrap(True)
        lbl_msg.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        lbl_msg.setStyleSheet(
            f"font-size: 12px; color: {t['text_secondary']};"
        )
        layout.addWidget(lbl_msg)

        inp_confirm = QLineEdit()
        inp_confirm.setPlaceholderText(f"Escribe «{username}» para confirmar")
        inp_confirm.setFixedHeight(42)
        inp_confirm.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        inp_confirm.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QLineEdit {{
                background-color: {t["bg_primary"]};
                border: 2px solid {t["expense"]};
                border-radius: 8px;
                padding: 8px 12px;
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 13px;
            }}
            QLineEdit:focus {{
                border: 2px solid #B91C1C;
            }}
        """)
        layout.addWidget(inp_confirm)

        # Error label
        lbl_err = QLabel("")
        lbl_err.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        lbl_err.setStyleSheet(
            f"color: {t['expense']}; font-size: 11px;"
        )
        lbl_err.setVisible(False)
        layout.addWidget(lbl_err)

        # Buttons row
        btn_row = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_row.setSpacing(10)

        btn_cancel = QPushButton("Cancelar")
        btn_cancel.setFixedHeight(40)
        btn_cancel.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_cancel.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QPushButton {{
                background-color: transparent;
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 8px;
                font-size: 13px;
            }}
            QPushButton:hover {{ background-color: {t["bg_hover"]}; }}
        """)
        btn_cancel.clicked.connect(dlg.reject)

        btn_confirm = QPushButton("  Eliminar cuenta")
        btn_confirm.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.delete-forever-outline", color="#FFFFFF")
        )
        btn_confirm.setFixedHeight(40)
        btn_confirm.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_confirm.setEnabled(False)   # disabled until username matches
        btn_confirm.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QPushButton {{
                background-color: {t["expense"]};
                color: #FFFFFF;
                border: none;
                border-radius: 8px;
                font-size: 13px;
                font-weight: 600;
                padding: 0 16px;
            }}
            QPushButton:hover {{ background-color: #B91C1C; }}
            QPushButton:disabled {{
                background-color: {t["border"]};
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
            }}
        """)

        btn_row.addWidget(btn_cancel)
        btn_row.addWidget(btn_confirm)
        layout.addLayout(btn_row)

        dlg.setStyleSheet(f"QDialog {{ background-color: {t['bg_secondary']}; }}")

        # Enable confirm button only when username matches exactly
        def _on_text_changed(text: str) -> None:
            btn_confirm.setEnabled(text.strip() == username)
            lbl_err.setVisible(False)

        inp_confirm.textChanged.connect(_on_text_changed)
        inp_confirm.returnPressed.connect(
            lambda: btn_confirm.click() if btn_confirm.isEnabled() else None
        )

        def _do_delete() -> None:
            if inp_confirm.text().strip() != username:
                lbl_err.setText("El nombre de usuario no coincide.")
                lbl_err.setVisible(True)
                return
            try:
                # Expire all cached objects to avoid stale lazy-load
                # on columns added after the DB was first created
                self._session.expire_all()

                # Re-fetch user within this session to ensure clean state
                from app.db.models import User as UserModel
                user_to_delete = self._session.get(
                    UserModel, self._user.id
                )
                if user_to_delete is None:
                    lbl_err.setText("Usuario no encontrado en la base de datos.")
                    lbl_err.setVisible(True)
                    return

                self._session.delete(user_to_delete)
                self._session.commit()

            except Exception as exc:
                self._session.rollback()
                lbl_err.setText(f"Error al eliminar: {exc}")
                lbl_err.setVisible(True)
                return

            dlg.accept()

            # Clear last user from settings and return to login
            from PySide6.QtCore import QSettings
            settings = QSettings("GEVernova", "FAMILYFIN")
            settings.remove("auth/last_username")

            self._session.close()

            from app.gui.login_window import LoginWindow
            self._login = LoginWindow()
            self._login.show()
            self.close()

        btn_confirm.clicked.connect(_do_delete)
        dlg.exec()


class _PlaceholderView(QWidget):
    """Temporary placeholder for views not yet implemented."""
```

## `app/gui/dashboard.py`

```python
"""
app/gui/dashboard.py
=====================
FAMILYFIN — Dashboard View (Posición Global)

KPI cards:
  - Saldo actual / Ingresos / Gastos / Inversiones / Tasa ahorro / Sin categorizar

Charts (Phase 5 — implemented):
  - Gastos por categoría: Donut (QPainter) + Barras horizontales, toggle button
  - Evolución mensual ingresos vs gastos: Barras agrupadas 12 meses (QPainter)
  - Últimas 5 transacciones: mini-tabla embebida

Range selector: Mes actual | Año actual | Todo
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import math
from collections import defaultdict
from datetime    import date, datetime
from typing      import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from PySide6.QtCore    import Qt, QRectF, QPointF
from PySide6.QtGui     import (
    QFont, QColor, QPainter, QPen, QBrush,
    QPainterPath, QFontMetrics,
)
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel,
    QFrame, QScrollArea, QComboBox, QPushButton,
    QSizePolicy, QGridLayout, QTableWidget,
    QTableWidgetItem, QHeaderView, QAbstractItemView,
)
import qtawesome as qta

from sqlalchemy     import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.config    import THEMES
from app.db.models import Transaction, TransactionType, Category, User
from app.gui._style_utils import apply_card_shadow

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Palette helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

_CHART_PALETTE = [
    "#2563EB", "#16A34A", "#DC2626", "#D97706",
    "#7C3AED", "#0891B2", "#DB2777", "#65A30D",
    "#EA580C", "#6366F1",
]

# Colour override for special labels (prefix-matched)
_CHART_COLOR_OVERRIDES: dict[str, str] = {
    "Sin categorizar": "#94A3B8",   # slate-400 — neutral grey
}


def _chart_color(index: int, label: str) -> QColor:
    """Return palette colour, with overrides for special labels."""
    for prefix, hex_color in _CHART_COLOR_OVERRIDES.items():
        if label.startswith(prefix):
            return QColor(hex_color)
    return QColor(_CHART_PALETTE[index % len(_CHART_PALETTE)])


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# KPI Card widget
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class _KPICard(QFrame):
    """
    A single KPI metric card.

    ┌────────────────────────────┐
    │  🔵  Label          ↑+5%  │
    │      €1.234,56             │
    │      sub-label             │
    └────────────────────────────┘
    """

    def __init__(self, label: str, icon_name: str,
                 accent_color: str, theme: dict) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self._theme   = theme
        self._accent  = accent_color
        self.setObjectName("kpiCard")
        self.setMinimumWidth(200)
        self.setMinimumHeight(110)

        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(20, 16, 20, 16)
        layout.setSpacing(6)

        # Header row: icon + label + badge
        header = QHBoxLayout()
        header.setSpacing(8)

        lbl_icon = QLabel()
        lbl_icon.setPixmap(
            qta.icon(icon_name, color=accent_color).pixmap(20, 20)
        )
        header.addWidget(lbl_icon)

        self._lbl_label = QLabel(label)
        self._lbl_label.setObjectName("kpiLabel")
        header.addWidget(self._lbl_label)

        header.addStretch()

        self._lbl_badge = QLabel("")
        self._lbl_badge.setObjectName("kpiBadge")
        self._lbl_badge.setVisible(False)
        header.addWidget(self._lbl_badge)

        layout.addLayout(header)

        # Value
        self._lbl_value = QLabel("—")
        self._lbl_value.setObjectName("kpiValue")
        layout.addWidget(self._lbl_value)

        # Sub-label
        self._lbl_sub = QLabel("")
        self._lbl_sub.setObjectName("kpiSub")
        layout.addWidget(self._lbl_sub)

    def set_value(self, value: str, sub: str = "",
                  badge: str = "", badge_positive: bool = True) -> None:
        self._lbl_value.setText(value)
        self._lbl_sub.setText(sub)
        if badge:
            self._lbl_badge.setText(badge)
            color = (self._theme["income"]
                     if badge_positive else self._theme["expense"])
            self._lbl_badge.setStyleSheet(
                f"color: {color}; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 600;"
            )
            self._lbl_badge.setVisible(True)
        else:
            self._lbl_badge.setVisible(False)

    def apply_style(self, theme: dict) -> None:
        self._theme = theme
        t = theme
        self.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QFrame#kpiCard {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border-radius: 12px;
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
            }}
            QLabel#kpiLabel {{
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 12px;
                font-weight: 500;
            }}
            QLabel#kpiValue {{
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 22px;
                font-weight: 700;
            }}
            QLabel#kpiSub {{
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 11px;
            }}
        """)
        apply_card_shadow(self, theme)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Section header
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Chart widget — Donut / Barras horizontales con toggle
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class _CategoryChartWidget(QFrame):
    """
    Gastos por categoría con toggle Donut ↔ Barras horizontales.
    Recibe datos via set_data(). Se repinta al cambiar de modo.
    """

    MODE_DONUT = "donut"
    MODE_HBAR  = "hbar"

    def __init__(self, theme: dict) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self._t      = theme
        self._mode   = self.MODE_DONUT
        self._data:  List[Tuple[str, float]] = []   # (label, value)
        self._total: float = 0.0

        self.setObjectName("chartCard")
        self.setMinimumHeight(260)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding,
                           QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)

        # ── Header row ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        root = QVBoxLayout(self)
        root.setContentsMargins(20, 14, 20, 14)
        root.setSpacing(8)

        hdr = QHBoxLayout()
        self._lbl_title = QLabel("Gastos por categoría")
        self._lbl_title.setObjectName("chartTitle")
        hdr.addWidget(self._lbl_title)
        hdr.addStretch()

        self._btn_toggle = QPushButton()
        self._btn_toggle.setFixedSize(28, 28)
        self._btn_toggle.setFlat(True)
        self._btn_toggle.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self._btn_toggle.clicked.connect(self._toggle_mode)
        hdr.addWidget(self._btn_toggle)
        root.addLayout(hdr)

        # Keep the card size unchanged while allowing the bars to grow
        # inside a vertical scroll area when there are more categories.
        self._canvas = _PaintCanvas(self._paint_chart)
        self._canvas.setMinimumHeight(200)
        self._scroll = QScrollArea()
        self._scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        self._scroll.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.NoFrame)
        self._scroll.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(
            Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAlwaysOff
        )
        self._scroll.setVerticalScrollBarPolicy(
            Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAsNeeded
        )
        self._scroll.setWidget(self._canvas)
        root.addWidget(self._scroll)

        self._update_toggle_icon()
        apply_card_shadow(self, self._t)

    def _toggle_mode(self) -> None:
        self._mode = (self.MODE_HBAR
                      if self._mode == self.MODE_DONUT
                      else self.MODE_DONUT)
        self._update_canvas_height()
        self._update_toggle_icon()
        self._canvas.update()

    def _update_toggle_icon(self) -> None:
        icon_name = ("mdi.chart-bar" if self._mode == self.MODE_DONUT
                     else "mdi.chart-donut")
        self._btn_toggle.setIcon(
            qta.icon(icon_name, color=self._t.get("text_secondary", "#888"))
        )
        tip = ("Ver como barras" if self._mode == self.MODE_DONUT
               else "Ver como donut")
        self._btn_toggle.setToolTip(tip)

    def set_data(self, data: List[Tuple[str, float]]) -> None:
        """data = [(category_name, abs_amount), ...] sorted desc."""
        self._data  = data
        self._total = sum(v for _, v in self._data)
        self._update_canvas_height()
        self._canvas.update()

    def _update_canvas_height(self) -> None:
        if self._mode == self.MODE_HBAR:
            # Keep one readable row per category; the card size remains
            # unchanged and the scroll area exposes the remaining rows.
            height = max(200, len(self._data) * 28 + 20)
        else:
            height = 200
        self._canvas.setMinimumHeight(height)

    def apply_theme(self, theme: dict) -> None:
        self._t = theme
        self._update_toggle_icon()
        self._canvas.update()
        apply_card_shadow(self, theme)

    def _paint_chart(self, painter: QPainter,
                     rect: QRectF) -> None:
        if not self._data or self._total == 0:
            self._paint_empty(painter, rect)
            return
        if self._mode == self.MODE_DONUT:
            self._paint_donut(painter, rect)
        else:
            self._paint_hbar(painter, rect)

    def _paint_empty(self, painter: QPainter, rect: QRectF) -> None:
        painter.setPen(QColor(self._t.get("text_secondary", "#888")))
        painter.drawText(rect, Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter,
                         "Sin datos para el período")

    def _paint_donut(self, painter: QPainter, rect: QRectF) -> None:
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing)

        # ── Geometry ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
        legend_w   = max(160, int(rect.width() * 0.38))
        donut_rect = QRectF(rect.x(), rect.y(),
                            rect.width() - legend_w - 8, rect.height())
        cx = donut_rect.center().x()
        cy = donut_rect.center().y()
        r  = min(donut_rect.width(), donut_rect.height()) / 2 - 6
        r  = max(r, 20)
        hole = r * 0.55

        arc_rect = QRectF(cx - r, cy - r, r * 2, r * 2)

        # ── Sectors ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
        start_angle = 90 * 16   # QPainter angles are in 1/16 degree
        for i, (label, value) in enumerate(self._data):
            color  = _chart_color(i, label)
            span   = int(round(value / self._total * 360 * 16))

            painter.setBrush(QBrush(color))
            painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
            painter.drawPie(arc_rect, start_angle, span)
            start_angle -= span   # clockwise

        # ── Donut hole ───────────────────────────────────────────────
        bg = QColor(self._t.get("bg_secondary", "#FFFFFF"))
        painter.setBrush(QBrush(bg))
        painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
        painter.drawEllipse(QPointF(cx, cy), hole, hole)

        # ── Centre label ─────────────────────────────────────────────
        painter.setPen(QColor(self._t.get("text_primary", "#000")))
        f = QFont("Segoe UI", 9, QFont.Weight.Bold)
        painter.setFont(f)
        painter.drawText(
            QRectF(cx - hole, cy - hole, hole * 2, hole * 2),
            Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter,
            _fmt_currency_short(self._total),
        )

        # ── Legend ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
        lx = donut_rect.right() + 12
        ly = rect.y() + 8
        line_h = 18
        painter.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 8))
        for i, (label, value) in enumerate(self._data):
            color = _chart_color(i, label)
            pct   = value / self._total * 100
            # Colour dot
            painter.setBrush(QBrush(color))
            painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
            painter.drawEllipse(
                QRectF(lx, ly + i * line_h + 4, 8, 8)
            )
            # Text — elide dynamically to the space actually available
            painter.setPen(QColor(self._t.get("text_secondary", "#666")))
            fm = painter.fontMetrics()
            pct_str  = f"  {pct:.1f}%"
            avail_w  = max(0.0, legend_w - 14 - fm.horizontalAdvance(pct_str))
            elided   = fm.elidedText(label, Qt.TextElideMode.ElideRight,
                                     int(avail_w))
            painter.drawText(
                QRectF(lx + 13, ly + i * line_h, legend_w - 14, line_h),
                Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft,
                f"{elided}{pct_str}",
            )

    def _paint_hbar(self, painter: QPainter, rect: QRectF) -> None:
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing)

        n        = len(self._data)
        bar_h    = min(22, max(12, int((rect.height() - 20) / n) - 4))
        label_w  = 130
        val_w    = 70
        bar_area = rect.width() - label_w - val_w - 8
        max_val  = self._data[0][1] if self._data else 1

        painter.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 8))

        for i, (label, value) in enumerate(self._data):
            y      = rect.y() + i * (bar_h + 6)
            color  = _chart_color(i, label)

            # Label — elide dynamically based on available column width
            painter.setPen(QColor(self._t.get("text_secondary", "#666")))
            fm = painter.fontMetrics()
            elided = fm.elidedText(label, Qt.TextElideMode.ElideRight,
                                   int(label_w))
            painter.drawText(
                QRectF(rect.x(), y, label_w, bar_h),
                Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft,
                elided,
            )

            # Bar background
            bx = rect.x() + label_w + 4
            bg_color = QColor(self._t.get("border", "#ddd"))
            painter.setBrush(QBrush(bg_color))
            painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
            painter.drawRoundedRect(
                QRectF(bx, y + 4, bar_area, bar_h - 8), 3, 3
            )

            # Bar fill
            fill_w = max(4.0, bar_area * value / max_val)
            painter.setBrush(QBrush(color))
            painter.drawRoundedRect(
                QRectF(bx, y + 4, fill_w, bar_h - 8), 3, 3
            )

            # Value
            painter.setPen(QColor(self._t.get("text_primary", "#000")))
            painter.drawText(
                QRectF(bx + bar_area + 6, y, val_w, bar_h),
                Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft,
                _fmt_currency_short(value),
            )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Chart widget — Evolución mensual (barras agrupadas)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class _MonthlyChartWidget(QFrame):
    """
    Ingresos vs Gastos por mes — barras agrupadas, últimos 12 meses.
    """

    def __init__(self, theme: dict) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self._t = theme
        # List of (month_label, income, expense)
        self._data: List[Tuple[str, float, float]] = []

        self.setObjectName("chartCard")
        self.setMinimumHeight(260)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding,
                           QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)

        root = QVBoxLayout(self)
        root.setContentsMargins(20, 14, 20, 14)
        root.setSpacing(8)

        hdr = QHBoxLayout()
        lbl = QLabel("Ingresos vs Gastos — últimos 12 meses")
        lbl.setObjectName("chartTitle")
        hdr.addWidget(lbl)
        hdr.addStretch()

        # Legend
        for color, text in [
            ("#16A34A", "Ingresos"),
            ("#DC2626", "Gastos"),
        ]:
            dot = QLabel("●")
            dot.setStyleSheet(f"color: {color}; font-size: 11px;")
            hdr.addWidget(dot)
            lbl2 = QLabel(text)
            lbl2.setObjectName("chartLegendLabel")
            hdr.addWidget(lbl2)
            hdr.addSpacing(8)

        root.addLayout(hdr)

        self._canvas = _PaintCanvas(self._paint_chart)
        self._canvas.setMinimumHeight(200)
        root.addWidget(self._canvas)
        apply_card_shadow(self, self._t)

    def set_data(self,
                 data: List[Tuple[str, float, float]]) -> None:
        """data = [(month_label, income, expense), ...] oldest→newest."""
        self._data = data[-12:]
        self._canvas.update()

    def apply_theme(self, theme: dict) -> None:
        self._t = theme
        self._canvas.update()
        apply_card_shadow(self, theme)

    def _paint_chart(self, painter: QPainter,
                     rect: QRectF) -> None:
        if not self._data:
            painter.setPen(QColor(self._t.get("text_secondary", "#888")))
            painter.drawText(rect, Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter,
                             "Sin datos para el período")
            return

        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing)

        n        = len(self._data)
        pad_l    = 44       # space for Y-axis value labels
        pad_r    = 8
        pad_top  = 8
        pad_bot  = 24       # space for month labels
        usable_w = rect.width()  - pad_l - pad_r
        usable_h = rect.height() - pad_top - pad_bot

        group_w  = usable_w / n
        bar_w    = max(4.0, group_w * 0.32)
        gap      = max(2.0, group_w * 0.06)

        max_val  = max(
            (max(inc, exp) for _, inc, exp in self._data),
            default=1.0
        ) or 1.0

        color_inc = QColor("#16A34A")
        color_exp = QColor("#DC2626")
        color_lbl = QColor(self._t.get("text_secondary", "#888"))
        color_grid = QColor(self._t.get("border", "#ddd"))
        color_axis = QColor(self._t.get("border_strong", "#9098A3"))

        # ── Gridlines + Y-axis value labels ────────────────────────────
        painter.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 7))
        for step in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
            gy = rect.y() + pad_top + usable_h * (1 - step)
            painter.setPen(color_lbl)
            painter.drawText(
                QRectF(rect.x(), gy - 8, pad_l - 10, 16),
                Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter,
                _fmt_currency_short(max_val * step),
            )
            painter.setPen(QPen(color_grid, 0.5, Qt.PenStyle.DashLine))
            painter.drawLine(
                QPointF(rect.x() + pad_l, gy),
                QPointF(rect.right() - pad_r, gy),
            )

        # ── Left axis line ──────────────────────────────────────────────
        painter.setPen(QPen(color_axis, 1.0, Qt.PenStyle.SolidLine))
        painter.drawLine(
            QPointF(rect.x() + pad_l, rect.y() + pad_top),
            QPointF(rect.x() + pad_l, rect.y() + pad_top + usable_h),
        )

        # ── Bars ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        painter.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 7))

        for i, (label, income, expense) in enumerate(self._data):
            gx     = rect.x() + pad_l + i * group_w
            center = gx + group_w / 2

            # Income bar
            h_inc = usable_h * income / max_val
            bx_inc = center - bar_w - gap / 2
            painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
            painter.setBrush(QBrush(color_inc))
            painter.drawRoundedRect(
                QRectF(bx_inc,
                       rect.y() + pad_top + usable_h - h_inc,
                       bar_w, h_inc),
                2, 2,
            )

            # Expense bar
            h_exp = usable_h * expense / max_val
            bx_exp = center + gap / 2
            painter.setBrush(QBrush(color_exp))
            painter.drawRoundedRect(
                QRectF(bx_exp,
                       rect.y() + pad_top + usable_h - h_exp,
                       bar_w, h_exp),
                2, 2,
            )

            # Month label
            painter.setPen(color_lbl)
            painter.drawText(
                QRectF(gx, rect.bottom() - pad_bot, group_w, pad_bot),
                Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignHCenter | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter,
                label,
            )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Paint canvas helper — delegates paintEvent to a callable
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class _PaintCanvas(QWidget):
    """Minimal widget that delegates painting to an external callable."""

    def __init__(self, paint_fn) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self._paint_fn = paint_fn
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding,
                           QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding)

    def paintEvent(self, event) -> None:
        painter = QPainter(self)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing)
        rect = QRectF(self.rect())
        self._paint_fn(painter, rect)
        painter.end()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Recent transactions mini-table
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class _RecentTransactionsWidget(QFrame):
    """Last 5 transactions — compact table, no pagination."""

    COLS = ["Fecha", "Emisor / Concepto", "Importe", "Categoría"]

    def __init__(self, theme: dict) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self._t = theme
        self.setObjectName("chartCard")
        self.setMinimumHeight(200)

        root = QVBoxLayout(self)
        root.setContentsMargins(20, 14, 20, 14)
        root.setSpacing(8)

        lbl = QLabel("Últimas transacciones")
        lbl.setObjectName("chartTitle")
        root.addWidget(lbl)

        self._table = QTableWidget(0, len(self.COLS))
        self._table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(self.COLS)
        self._table.setEditTriggers(
            QAbstractItemView.EditTrigger.NoEditTriggers
        )
        self._table.setSelectionBehavior(
            QAbstractItemView.SelectionBehavior.SelectRows
        )
        self._table.setShowGrid(False)
        self._table.verticalHeader().setVisible(False)
        self._table.setAlternatingRowColors(True)
        self._table.setObjectName("recentTable")

        hdr = self._table.horizontalHeader()
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(1, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(2, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(3, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)

        root.addWidget(self._table)
        apply_card_shadow(self, self._t)

    def set_data(self, rows: List[dict]) -> None:
        """
        rows: list of dicts with keys:
          transaction_date, issuer_name, concept, amount, category_name
        """
        t = self._t
        self._table.setRowCount(0)
        for row in rows[:5]:
            r = self._table.rowCount()
            self._table.insertRow(r)

            date_str = str(row.get("transaction_date", ""))[:10]
            issuer   = (row.get("issuer_name") or
                        row.get("concept") or "—")[:40]
            amount   = float(row.get("amount") or 0.0)
            cat      = row.get("category_name") or "—"

            amt_str  = _fmt_amount(amount)
            color    = t["income"] if amount >= 0 else t["expense"]

            for col, (text, align) in enumerate([
                (date_str,  Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft),
                (issuer,    Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft),
                (amt_str,   Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight),
                (cat,       Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft),
            ]):
                item = QTableWidgetItem(text)
                item.setTextAlignment(
                    align | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter
                )
                if col == 2:
                    item.setForeground(QColor(color))
                item.setFlags(Qt.ItemFlag.ItemIsEnabled |
                              Qt.ItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable)
                self._table.setItem(r, col, item)

        self._table.resizeRowsToContents()

    def apply_theme(self, theme: dict) -> None:
        self._t = theme
        apply_card_shadow(self, theme)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Formatting helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _fmt_currency_short(value: float) -> str:
    """€1.234 / €12,3k / €1,2M"""
    av = abs(value)
    if av >= 1_000_000:
        return f"€{av/1_000_000:.1f}M"
    if av >= 1_000:
        return f"€{av/1_000:.1f}k"
    return f"€{av:,.0f}".replace(",", ".")


def _fmt_amount(value: float) -> str:
    sign = "+" if value >= 0 else ""
    return (
        f"{sign}{abs(value):,.2f} €"
        .replace(",", "X").replace(".", ",").replace("X", ".")
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class _SectionHeader(QLabel):
    def __init__(self, text: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(text)
        self.setObjectName("sectionHeader")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Dashboard view
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class DashboardView(QWidget):

    RANGES = [
        ("Mes actual",    "month"),
        ("Año actual",    "year"),
        ("Todo",          "all"),
    ]

    def __init__(self, user: User, session: Session,
                 theme: str = "light") -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self._user    = user
        self._session = session
        self._theme   = theme
        self._t       = THEMES[theme]
        self._range   = "month"

        self._build_ui()
        self._apply_theme()
        self.refresh_data()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # UI construction
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        root = QVBoxLayout(self)
        root.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        root.setSpacing(0)

        # ── Dashboard top bar ──────────────────────────────────────────
        topbar = QFrame()
        topbar.setObjectName("dashTopBar")
        topbar.setFixedHeight(56)
        tbl = QHBoxLayout(topbar)
        tbl.setContentsMargins(24, 0, 24, 0)

        lbl_title = QLabel("Posición Global")
        lbl_title.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 15, QFont.Weight.Bold))
        lbl_title.setObjectName("dashTitle")
        tbl.addWidget(lbl_title)

        tbl.addStretch()

        # Range selector
        lbl_range = QLabel("Periodo:")
        lbl_range.setObjectName("dashRangeLabel")
        tbl.addWidget(lbl_range)

        tbl.addSpacing(8)

        self._cmb_range = QComboBox()
        self._cmb_range.setFixedHeight(32)
        self._cmb_range.setObjectName("dashCombo")
        for label, key in self.RANGES:
            self._cmb_range.addItem(label, key)
        self._cmb_range.currentIndexChanged.connect(self._on_range_changed)
        tbl.addWidget(self._cmb_range)

        tbl.addSpacing(8)

        # Refresh button
        self._btn_refresh = QPushButton()
        self._btn_refresh.setFixedSize(32, 32)
        self._btn_refresh.setFlat(True)
        self._btn_refresh.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self._btn_refresh.setToolTip("Actualizar datos")
        self._btn_refresh.clicked.connect(self.refresh_data)
        tbl.addWidget(self._btn_refresh)

        root.addWidget(topbar)

        # ── Scrollable content ─────────────────────────────────────────
        scroll = QScrollArea()
        scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        scroll.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.NoFrame)
        scroll.setObjectName("dashScroll")

        content = QWidget()
        content.setObjectName("dashContent")
        self._content_layout = QVBoxLayout(content)
        self._content_layout.setContentsMargins(24, 24, 24, 24)
        self._content_layout.setSpacing(24)

        # KPI section header
        self._content_layout.addWidget(
            _SectionHeader("Resumen del periodo")
        )

        # KPI grid (3 per row)
        kpi_grid_widget = QWidget()
        self._kpi_grid = QGridLayout(kpi_grid_widget)
        self._kpi_grid.setSpacing(16)
        self._kpi_grid.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self._content_layout.addWidget(kpi_grid_widget)

        # Build KPI cards
        self._cards: dict[str, _KPICard] = {}
        kpi_defs = [
            ("balance",       "Saldo actual",        "mdi.bank-outline",
             self._t["accent"]),
            ("income",        "Ingresos",             "mdi.trending-up",
             self._t["income"]),
            ("expense",       "Gastos",               "mdi.trending-down",
             self._t["expense"]),
            ("investment",    "Inversiones",          "mdi.chart-line",
             self._t["investment"]),
            ("savings_rate",  "Tasa de ahorro",       "mdi.piggy-bank-outline",
             self._t["accent"]),
            ("uncategorized", "Sin categorizar",      "mdi.tag-off-outline",
             self._t["warning"]),
        ]
        for idx, (key, label, icon, colour) in enumerate(kpi_defs):
            card = _KPICard(label, icon, colour, self._t)
            row, col = divmod(idx, 3)
            self._kpi_grid.addWidget(card, row, col)
            self._cards[key] = card

        # Make columns equal width
        for col in range(3):
            self._kpi_grid.setColumnStretch(col, 1)

        # Charts section header
        self._content_layout.addWidget(
            _SectionHeader("Análisis visual")
        )

        # ── Chart row: category donut + monthly bars ──────────────────
        charts_row = QHBoxLayout()
        charts_row.setSpacing(16)

        self._chart_category = _CategoryChartWidget(self._t)
        self._chart_monthly  = _MonthlyChartWidget(self._t)
        charts_row.addWidget(self._chart_category)
        charts_row.addWidget(self._chart_monthly)
        self._content_layout.addLayout(charts_row)

        # ── Recent transactions ───────────────────────────────────────
        self._content_layout.addWidget(
            _SectionHeader("Últimas transacciones")
        )
        self._recent_widget = _RecentTransactionsWidget(self._t)
        self._content_layout.addWidget(self._recent_widget)

        self._content_layout.addStretch()

        scroll.setWidget(content)
        root.addWidget(scroll)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Data loading
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def refresh_data(self) -> None:
        """Query DB and refresh all KPI cards."""
        today      = date.today()
        date_from, date_to = self._get_date_range(today)

        try:
            # Running balance — saldo of the latest transaction that has one
            # (running_balance is nullable; a row without it must not crash
            # the whole dashboard).
            balance = (
                self._session.query(Transaction.running_balance)
                .filter(Transaction.user_id == self._user.id,
                        Transaction.running_balance.isnot(None))
                .order_by(Transaction.transaction_date.desc(),
                          Transaction.id.desc())
                .limit(1)
                .scalar()
            ) or 0.0

            # Period aggregates
            period_q = (
                self._session.query(
                    Transaction.type,
                    func.sum(Transaction.amount).label("total")
                )
                .filter(
                    Transaction.user_id == self._user.id,
                    Transaction.transaction_date >= date_from,
                    Transaction.transaction_date <= date_to,
                )
                .group_by(Transaction.type)
                .all()
            )

            totals = {row.type: (row.total or 0.0) for row in period_q}

            income     = totals.get(TransactionType.income,     0.0)
            expense    = abs(totals.get(TransactionType.expense, 0.0))
            investment = abs(totals.get(
                TransactionType.investment, 0.0
            ))

            savings_rate = (
                ((income - expense) / income * 100) if income > 0 else 0.0
            )

            # Uncategorized count
            uncat = (
                self._session.query(func.count(Transaction.id))
                .filter(
                    Transaction.user_id    == self._user.id,
                    Transaction.category_id == None,  # noqa: E711
                    Transaction.transaction_date >= date_from,
                    Transaction.transaction_date <= date_to,
                )
                .scalar() or 0
            )

        except Exception:
            balance = expense = income = investment = 0.0
            savings_rate = 0.0
            uncat = 0

        # ── Chart data — category breakdown ───────────────────────────
        try:
            # ── Categorized expenses ──────────────────────────────────
            cat_q = (
                self._session.query(
                    Category.name,
                    func.sum(Transaction.amount).label("total"),
                )
                .join(Transaction, Transaction.category_id == Category.id)
                .filter(
                    Transaction.user_id          == self._user.id,
                    Transaction.type             == TransactionType.expense,
                    Transaction.transaction_date >= date_from,
                    Transaction.transaction_date <= date_to,
                )
                .group_by(Category.name)
                .order_by(func.sum(Transaction.amount).asc())
                .all()
            )
            cat_data = [
                (name, abs(total or 0.0))
                for name, total in cat_q
                if total
            ]

            # ── Uncategorized expenses — always include if > 0 ────────
            uncat_q = (
                self._session.query(
                    func.sum(Transaction.amount).label("total"),
                    func.count(Transaction.id).label("n"),
                )
                .filter(
                    Transaction.user_id      == self._user.id,
                    Transaction.type         == TransactionType.expense,
                    Transaction.category_id  == None,   # noqa: E711
                    Transaction.transaction_date >= date_from,
                    Transaction.transaction_date <= date_to,
                )
                .one()
            )
            uncat_total = abs(uncat_q.total or 0.0)
            uncat_n     = int(uncat_q.n or 0)

            if uncat_total > 0:
                cat_data.append((f"Sin categorizar ({uncat_n})", uncat_total))

            cat_data.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
            self._chart_category.set_data(cat_data)
            logger.debug(
                "Chart categorías: %d categorías + %d sin categorizar (€%.2f)",
                len(cat_data) - (1 if uncat_total > 0 else 0),
                uncat_n, uncat_total,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception("Chart categorías — error inesperado: %s", exc)
            self._chart_category.set_data([])

        # ── Chart data — monthly evolution (last 12 months) ──────────
        try:
            from sqlalchemy import extract
            monthly_q = (
                self._session.query(
                    extract("year",  Transaction.transaction_date).label("yr"),
                    extract("month", Transaction.transaction_date).label("mo"),
                    Transaction.type,
                    func.sum(Transaction.amount).label("total"),
                )
                .filter(Transaction.user_id == self._user.id)
                .group_by("yr", "mo", Transaction.type)
                .order_by("yr", "mo")
                .all()
            )

            monthly: Dict[Tuple, Dict] = defaultdict(
                lambda: {"income": 0.0, "expense": 0.0}
            )
            for yr, mo, txn_type, total in monthly_q:
                key = (int(yr), int(mo))
                if txn_type == TransactionType.income:
                    monthly[key]["income"] += abs(total or 0.0)
                elif txn_type == TransactionType.expense:
                    monthly[key]["expense"] += abs(total or 0.0)

            month_abbr = ["", "Ene", "Feb", "Mar", "Abr", "May",
                          "Jun", "Jul", "Ago", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dic"]
            monthly_data = [
                (f"{month_abbr[mo]}\n{str(yr)[2:]}",
                 v["income"], v["expense"])
                for (yr, mo), v in sorted(monthly.items())
            ]
            self._chart_monthly.set_data(monthly_data)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("Chart mensual: %s", exc)
            self._chart_monthly.set_data([])

        # ── Recent transactions (last 5) ──────────────────────────────
        try:
            recent_txns = (
                self._session.query(Transaction)
                .filter(Transaction.user_id == self._user.id)
                .order_by(
                    Transaction.transaction_date.desc(),
                    Transaction.id.desc(),
                )
                .limit(5)
                .all()
            )
            cat_map = {
                c.id: c.name
                for c in self._session.query(Category)
                .filter(Category.user_id == self._user.id).all()
            }
            recent_rows = [
                {
                    "transaction_date": txn.transaction_date,
                    "issuer_name":      txn.issuer_name,
                    "concept":          txn.concept,
                    "amount":           txn.amount,
                    "category_name":    cat_map.get(txn.category_id),
                }
                for txn in recent_txns
            ]
            self._recent_widget.set_data(recent_rows)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("Recent txns: %s", exc)
            self._recent_widget.set_data([])

        # Update cards
        self._cards["balance"].set_value(
            self._fmt_currency(balance),
            sub=f"A {today.strftime('%d %b %Y')}",
        )
        self._cards["income"].set_value(
            self._fmt_currency(income),
            sub=self._period_label(date_from, date_to),
        )
        self._cards["expense"].set_value(
            self._fmt_currency(expense),
            sub=self._period_label(date_from, date_to),
        )
        self._cards["investment"].set_value(
            self._fmt_currency(investment),
            sub=self._period_label(date_from, date_to),
        )
        self._cards["savings_rate"].set_value(
            f"{savings_rate:.1f}%",
            sub="Ingreso − Gasto / Ingreso",
            badge=(f"{'↑' if savings_rate >= 0 else '↓'} "
                   f"{abs(savings_rate):.1f}%"),
            badge_positive=savings_rate >= 0,
        )
        self._cards["uncategorized"].set_value(
            str(uncat),
            sub="transacciones pendientes",
        )

    def refresh_theme(self, theme: str) -> None:
        self._theme = theme
        self._t     = THEMES[theme]
        self._apply_theme()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Helpers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _get_date_range(
        self, today: date
    ) -> tuple[date, date]:
        if self._range == "month":
            return date(today.year, today.month, 1), today
        elif self._range == "year":
            return date(today.year, 1, 1), today
        else:
            return date(2000, 1, 1), today

    def _period_label(self, d_from: date, d_to: date) -> str:
        return f"{d_from.strftime('%d %b')} – {d_to.strftime('%d %b %Y')}"

    @staticmethod
    def _fmt_currency(value: float) -> str:
        return f"€{abs(value):,.2f}".replace(",", "X").replace(
            ".", ","
        ).replace("X", ".")

    def _on_range_changed(self, index: int) -> None:
        self._range = self._cmb_range.currentData()
        self.refresh_data()

    def _apply_theme(self) -> None:
        t = self._t

        self._btn_refresh.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.refresh", color=t["text_secondary"])
        )

        # Re-style cards
        for card in self._cards.values():
            card.apply_style(t)

        # Re-style chart widgets
        if hasattr(self, "_chart_category"):
            self._chart_category.apply_theme(t)
        if hasattr(self, "_chart_monthly"):
            self._chart_monthly.apply_theme(t)
        if hasattr(self, "_recent_widget"):
            self._recent_widget.apply_theme(t)

        self.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QWidget#dashContent {{
                background-color: {t["bg_primary"]};
            }}
            QScrollArea#dashScroll {{
                background-color: {t["bg_primary"]};
                border: none;
            }}
            QFrame#dashTopBar {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border-bottom: 1px solid {t["border"]};
            }}
            QLabel#dashTitle {{
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 16px;
                font-weight: 700;
            }}
            QLabel#dashRangeLabel {{
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 12px;
            }}
            QComboBox#dashCombo {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 6px;
                padding: 4px 10px;
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 12px;
                min-width: 130px;
            }}
            QComboBox#dashCombo::drop-down {{
                border: none;
            }}
            QLabel#sectionHeader {{
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 13px;
                font-weight: 700;
                padding-bottom: 4px;
                border-bottom: 2px solid {t["accent"]};
            }}
            QFrame#chartCard {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border-radius: 12px;
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
            }}
            QLabel#chartTitle {{
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 13px;
                font-weight: 600;
            }}
            QLabel#chartPlaceholder {{
                color: {t["neutral"]};
                font-size: 12px;
            }}
            QTableWidget#recentTable {{
                background-color: {t["bg_primary"]};
                alternate-background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
```
