```python
                border-radius: 8px;
                gridline-color: {t["border"]};
                font-size: 12px;
                selection-background-color: {t["accent"]};
                selection-color: #FFFFFF;
            }}
            QTableWidget#recentTable::item {{
                padding: 6px 8px;
                border: none;
            }}
            QTableWidget#recentTable::item:alternate {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
            }}
            QTableWidget#recentTable::item:selected {{
                background-color: {t["accent"]};
                color: #FFFFFF;
            }}
            QHeaderView::section {{
                background-color: {t["bg_hover"]};
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 11px; font-weight: 600;
                border: none; padding: 5px 8px;
            }}
        """)
```

## `app/gui/expenses_view.py`

```python
"""
app/gui/expenses_view.py
=========================
FAMILYFIN — Expenses View (Gastos)

Sections:
  1. Period selector  — Mes actual / Mes anterior / Año actual / Año anterior / Todo
  2. KPI strip        — Total, nº transacciones, media, mayor gasto, % categorizado
  3. Distribution     — _CategoryChartWidget reused + Line chart toggle (3 modes)
  4. Comparison       — Grouped bars: current vs previous period by category
  5. Detail table     — Sortable, top-50, paginable
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from datetime    import date
from typing      import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from PySide6.QtCore    import Qt, QRectF, QPointF
from PySide6.QtGui     import (
    QColor, QFont, QPainter, QPen, QBrush, QFontMetrics,
)
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, QFrame,
    QScrollArea, QPushButton, QComboBox, QTableWidget,
    QTableWidgetItem, QHeaderView, QAbstractItemView,
    QSizePolicy, QButtonGroup,
)
import qtawesome as qta

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.analytics.period_summary import (
    category_totals,
    period_totals,
    summarize_period,
)
from app.config    import THEMES
from app.db.models import TransactionType, User
from app.gui._style_utils import apply_card_shadow

# Reuse chart palette and helpers from dashboard
from app.gui.dashboard import (
    _CHART_PALETTE, _CHART_COLOR_OVERRIDES,
    _chart_color, _CategoryChartWidget,
    _PaintCanvas, _fmt_currency_short, _fmt_amount,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Comparison chart — grouped bars: current vs previous period by category
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class _ComparisonChartWidget(QFrame):
    """
    Grouped bar chart comparing expenses by category across two periods.
    data_a = current period, data_b = comparison period.
    """

    def __init__(self, theme: dict) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self._t      = theme
        self._data_a: List[Tuple[str, float]] = []   # (label, value) current
        self._data_b: List[Tuple[str, float]] = []   # (label, value) previous
        self._label_a = "Período actual"
        self._label_b = "Período anterior"

        self.setObjectName("chartCard")
        self.setMinimumHeight(280)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding,
                           QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)

        root = QVBoxLayout(self)
        root.setContentsMargins(20, 14, 20, 14)
        root.setSpacing(8)

        # Header + legend
        hdr = QHBoxLayout()
        self._lbl_title = QLabel("Comparativa por categoría")
        self._lbl_title.setObjectName("chartTitle")
        hdr.addWidget(self._lbl_title)
        hdr.addStretch()
        for attr, color, text in [
            ("_dot_a", "#2563EB", ""),
            ("_lbl_a", "#2563EB", "Actual"),
            ("_dot_b", "#94A3B8", ""),
            ("_lbl_b", "#94A3B8", "Anterior"),
        ]:
            w = QLabel("●" if "dot" in attr else text)
            w.setStyleSheet(f"color: {color}; font-size: 11px;")
            hdr.addWidget(w)
            hdr.addSpacing(2)
        root.addLayout(hdr)

        # Canvas grows wide with many categories — wrap in a horizontal
        # scroll area so groups stay readable instead of squeezing.
        self._canvas = _PaintCanvas(self._paint_chart)
        self._canvas.setMinimumHeight(220)
        self._scroll = QScrollArea()
        self._scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        self._scroll.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.NoFrame)
        self._scroll.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(
            Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAsNeeded
        )
        self._scroll.setVerticalScrollBarPolicy(
            Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAlwaysOff
        )
        self._scroll.setWidget(self._canvas)
        root.addWidget(self._scroll)
        apply_card_shadow(self, self._t)

    def set_data(
        self,
        data_a: List[Tuple[str, float]],
        data_b: List[Tuple[str, float]],
        label_a: str = "Período actual",
        label_b: str = "Período anterior",
    ) -> None:
        # Align both lists to same category set
        cats = list({c for c, _ in data_a} | {c for c, _ in data_b})
        cats.sort()
        map_a = dict(data_a)
        map_b = dict(data_b)
        self._data_a  = [(c, map_a.get(c, 0.0)) for c in cats]
        self._data_b  = [(c, map_b.get(c, 0.0)) for c in cats]
        self._label_a = label_a
        self._label_b = label_b
        # Keep each category group at least 70px wide; beyond that the
        # scroll area exposes the remainder instead of squeezing labels.
        n = max(len(cats), 1)
        self._canvas.setMinimumWidth(max(400, n * 70))
        self._canvas.update()

    def apply_theme(self, theme: dict) -> None:
        self._t = theme
        self._canvas.update()
        apply_card_shadow(self, theme)

    def _paint_chart(self, painter: QPainter, rect: QRectF) -> None:
        if not self._data_a and not self._data_b:
            painter.setPen(QColor(self._t.get("text_secondary", "#888")))
            painter.drawText(rect, Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter,
                             "Sin datos para comparar")
            return

        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing)

        n        = max(len(self._data_a), len(self._data_b))
        if n == 0:
            return

        pad_l    = 44       # space for Y-axis value labels
        pad_r    = 8
        pad_top  = 8
        pad_bot  = 28
        usable_w = rect.width()  - pad_l - pad_r
        usable_h = rect.height() - pad_top - pad_bot

        group_w  = usable_w / n
        bar_w    = max(4.0, group_w * 0.30)
        gap      = max(1.0, group_w * 0.04)

        all_vals = [v for _, v in self._data_a] + [v for _, v in self._data_b]
        max_val  = max(all_vals) if all_vals else 1.0
        max_val  = max_val or 1.0

        color_a   = QColor("#2563EB")
        color_b   = QColor("#94A3B8")
        color_lbl = QColor(self._t.get("text_secondary", "#888"))
        color_grid = QColor(self._t.get("border", "#ddd"))
        color_axis = QColor(self._t.get("border_strong", "#9098A3"))

        # Gridlines + Y-axis value labels
        painter.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 7))
        for step in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
            gy = rect.y() + pad_top + usable_h * (1 - step)
            painter.setPen(color_lbl)
            painter.drawText(
                QRectF(rect.x(), gy - 8, pad_l - 10, 16),
                Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter,
                _fmt_currency_short(max_val * step),
            )
            painter.setPen(QPen(color_grid, 0.5, Qt.PenStyle.DashLine))
            painter.drawLine(
                QPointF(rect.x() + pad_l, gy),
                QPointF(rect.right() - pad_r, gy),
            )

        # Left axis line
        painter.setPen(QPen(color_axis, 1.0, Qt.PenStyle.SolidLine))
        painter.drawLine(
            QPointF(rect.x() + pad_l, rect.y() + pad_top),
            QPointF(rect.x() + pad_l, rect.y() + pad_top + usable_h),
        )

        painter.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 7))

        for i in range(n):
            label   = self._data_a[i][0] if i < len(self._data_a) else self._data_b[i][0]
            val_a   = self._data_a[i][1] if i < len(self._data_a) else 0.0
            val_b   = self._data_b[i][1] if i < len(self._data_b) else 0.0

            gx      = rect.x() + pad_l + i * group_w
            center  = gx + group_w / 2

            # Bar A (current — blue)
            h_a  = usable_h * val_a / max_val
            bx_a = center - bar_w - gap / 2
            painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
            painter.setBrush(QBrush(color_a))
            painter.drawRoundedRect(
                QRectF(bx_a, rect.y() + pad_top + usable_h - h_a, bar_w, h_a),
                2, 2,
            )

            # Bar B (previous — grey)
            h_b  = usable_h * val_b / max_val
            bx_b = center + gap / 2
            painter.setBrush(QBrush(color_b))
            painter.drawRoundedRect(
                QRectF(bx_b, rect.y() + pad_top + usable_h - h_b, bar_w, h_b),
                2, 2,
            )

            # Category label — elide dynamically to the group's width
            painter.setPen(color_lbl)
            fm = painter.fontMetrics()
            elided = fm.elidedText(label, Qt.TextElideMode.ElideRight,
                                   int(group_w))
            painter.drawText(
                QRectF(gx, rect.bottom() - pad_bot, group_w, pad_bot),
                Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignHCenter | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter,
                elided,
            )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Monthly top-category chart — stacked bars by month
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class _MonthlyCategoryChartWidget(QFrame):
    """Stacked monthly bars showing the main expense categories in the selected period."""

    def __init__(self, theme: dict) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self._t = theme
        self._data: List[Tuple[str, List[Tuple[str, float]]]] = []

        self.setObjectName("chartCard")
        self.setMinimumHeight(250)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding,
                            QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)

        root = QVBoxLayout(self)
        root.setContentsMargins(20, 14, 20, 14)
        root.setSpacing(8)

        hdr = QHBoxLayout()
        self._lbl_title = QLabel("Top categorías por mes")
        self._lbl_title.setObjectName("chartTitle")
        hdr.addWidget(self._lbl_title)
        hdr.addStretch()
        root.addLayout(hdr)

        # Canvas grows wide with many months — wrap in a horizontal
        # scroll area so bars stay readable instead of squeezing.
        self._canvas = _PaintCanvas(self._paint_chart)
        self._canvas.setMinimumHeight(180)
        self._scroll = QScrollArea()
        self._scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        self._scroll.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.NoFrame)
        self._scroll.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(
            Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAsNeeded
        )
        self._scroll.setVerticalScrollBarPolicy(
            Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAlwaysOff
        )
        self._scroll.setWidget(self._canvas)
        root.addWidget(self._scroll)
        apply_card_shadow(self, self._t)

    def set_data(self, data: List[Tuple[str, List[Tuple[str, float]]]]) -> None:
        self._data = data
        # Keep each month at least 56px wide; beyond that the scroll area
        # exposes the remainder instead of squeezing bars/labels.
        n = max(len(data), 1)
        self._canvas.setMinimumWidth(max(400, n * 56))
        self._canvas.update()

    def apply_theme(self, theme: dict) -> None:
        self._t = theme
        self._canvas.update()
        apply_card_shadow(self, theme)

    def _paint_chart(self, painter: QPainter, rect: QRectF) -> None:
        if not self._data:
            painter.setPen(QColor(self._t.get("text_secondary", "#888")))
            painter.drawText(rect, Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter,
                             "Sin datos por mes")
            return

        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing)

        n = len(self._data)
        pad_l = 44        # space for Y-axis value labels
        pad_r = 12
        pad_top = 10
        pad_bot = 28
        usable_w = rect.width() - pad_l - pad_r
        usable_h = rect.height() - pad_top - pad_bot
        group_w = usable_w / max(n, 1)
        bar_w = max(18.0, group_w * 0.72)
        max_total = max(sum(value for _, value in cats) for _, cats in self._data) if self._data else 1.0
        max_total = max_total or 1.0
        color_grid = QColor(self._t.get("border", "#ddd"))
        color_lbl = QColor(self._t.get("text_secondary", "#888"))
        color_axis = QColor(self._t.get("border_strong", "#9098A3"))

        painter.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 7))
        for step in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
            gy = rect.y() + pad_top + usable_h * (1 - step)
            painter.setPen(color_lbl)
            painter.drawText(
                QRectF(rect.x(), gy - 8, pad_l - 10, 16),
                Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter,
                _fmt_currency_short(max_total * step),
            )
            painter.setPen(QPen(color_grid, 0.5, Qt.PenStyle.DashLine))
            painter.drawLine(QPointF(rect.x() + pad_l, gy), QPointF(rect.right() - pad_r, gy))

        painter.setPen(QPen(color_axis, 1.0, Qt.PenStyle.SolidLine))
        painter.drawLine(
            QPointF(rect.x() + pad_l, rect.y() + pad_top),
            QPointF(rect.x() + pad_l, rect.y() + pad_top + usable_h),
        )

        for i, (label, cats) in enumerate(self._data):
            total = sum(value for _, value in cats)
            month_x = rect.x() + pad_l + i * group_w + (group_w - bar_w) / 2
            h = usable_h * total / max_total
            base_y = rect.y() + pad_top + usable_h
            covered = 0.0
            stack_total = total or 1.0

            for idx, (cat_name, value) in enumerate(cats):
                seg_h = max(0.0, h * (value / stack_total))
                if seg_h <= 0:
                    continue
                painter.setBrush(QBrush(_chart_color(idx, cat_name)))
                painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
                painter.drawRect(
                    QRectF(month_x, base_y - covered - seg_h, bar_w, seg_h)
                )
                covered += seg_h

            painter.setPen(color_lbl)
            painter.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 7))
            painter.drawText(
                QRectF(month_x, rect.bottom() - pad_bot, bar_w, pad_bot),
                Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignHCenter | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter,
                label,
            )

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Line chart — expenses over time (daily aggregated)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class _LineChartWidget(QFrame):
    """
    Daily expense line chart for the selected period.
    """

    def __init__(self, theme: dict) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self._t    = theme
        self._data: List[Tuple[str, float]] = []   # (day_label, total)

        self.setObjectName("chartCard")
        self.setMinimumHeight(260)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding,
                           QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)

        root = QVBoxLayout(self)
        root.setContentsMargins(20, 14, 20, 14)
        root.setSpacing(8)

        hdr = QHBoxLayout()
        self._lbl_title = QLabel("Evolución diaria")
        self._lbl_title.setObjectName("chartTitle")
        hdr.addWidget(self._lbl_title)
        hdr.addStretch()
        root.addLayout(hdr)

        self._canvas = _PaintCanvas(self._paint_chart)
        self._canvas.setMinimumHeight(200)
        root.addWidget(self._canvas)
        apply_card_shadow(self, self._t)

    def set_title(self, title: str) -> None:
        self._lbl_title.setText(title)

    def set_data(self, data: List[Tuple[str, float]]) -> None:
        self._data = data
        self._canvas.update()

    def apply_theme(self, theme: dict) -> None:
        self._t = theme
        self._canvas.update()
        apply_card_shadow(self, theme)

    def _paint_chart(self, painter: QPainter, rect: QRectF) -> None:
        if not self._data:
            painter.setPen(QColor(self._t.get("text_secondary", "#888")))
            painter.drawText(rect, Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter,
                            "Sin datos para el período")
            return

        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing)

        pad_l   = 52
        pad_r   = 10
        pad_top = 12
        pad_bot = 24
        uw      = rect.width() - pad_l - pad_r
        uh      = rect.height() - pad_top - pad_bot

        max_val = max((v for _, v in self._data), default=1.0) or 1.0
        n       = len(self._data)

        color_line = QColor(self._t.get("accent", "#2563EB"))
        color_fill = QColor(self._t.get("accent", "#2563EB"))
        color_fill.setAlpha(30)
        color_grid = QColor(self._t.get("border", "#ddd"))
        color_lbl  = QColor(self._t.get("text_secondary", "#888"))

        painter.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 7))
        painter.setPen(color_lbl)
        for step in range(5):
            ratio = step / 4
            value = max_val * (1.0 - ratio)
            y = rect.y() + pad_top + uh * ratio
            painter.drawText(
                QRectF(rect.x() + 4, y - 8, pad_l - 10, 16),
                Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter,
                _fmt_currency_short(value),
            )
            painter.setPen(QPen(color_grid, 0.5, Qt.PenStyle.DashLine))
            painter.drawLine(
                QPointF(rect.x() + pad_l, y),
                QPointF(rect.right() - pad_r, y),
            )
            painter.setPen(color_lbl)

        # Build points
        pts: List[QPointF] = []
        for i, (_, val) in enumerate(self._data):
            x = rect.x() + pad_l + uw * i / max(n - 1, 1)
            y = rect.y() + pad_top + uh * (1 - val / max_val)
            pts.append(QPointF(x, y))

        # Fill area under the curve
        from PySide6.QtGui import QPainterPath
        path = QPainterPath()
        path.moveTo(pts[0].x(), rect.y() + pad_top + uh)
        for p in pts:
            path.lineTo(p)
        path.lineTo(pts[-1].x(), rect.y() + pad_top + uh)
        path.closeSubpath()
        painter.setBrush(QBrush(color_fill))
        painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
        painter.drawPath(path)

        # Line
        painter.setPen(QPen(color_line, 2, Qt.PenStyle.SolidLine))
        painter.setBrush(Qt.BrushStyle.NoBrush)
        for i in range(len(pts) - 1):
            painter.drawLine(pts[i], pts[i + 1])

        # Dots on data points (only if few points)
        if n <= 31:
            painter.setBrush(QBrush(color_line))
            painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
            for p in pts:
                painter.drawEllipse(p, 3, 3)

        axis_x = rect.x() + pad_l
        axis_y = rect.y() + pad_top
        axis_bottom = rect.y() + pad_top + uh
        axis_right = rect.right() - pad_r
        painter.setPen(QPen(QColor(self._t.get("border_strong", "#9098A3")), 1.0, Qt.PenStyle.SolidLine))
        painter.drawLine(QPointF(axis_x, axis_y), QPointF(axis_x, axis_bottom))
        painter.drawLine(QPointF(axis_x, axis_bottom), QPointF(axis_right, axis_bottom))

        painter.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 7, QFont.Weight.DemiBold))
        painter.setPen(color_lbl)
        painter.drawText(
            QRectF(rect.x() + 4, rect.y() + 8, 24, 18),
            Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter,
            "€",
        )

        # X labels (show max 8 evenly spaced)
        painter.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 7))
        step_lbl = max(1, n // 8)
        for i, (label, _) in enumerate(self._data):
            if i % step_lbl == 0 or i == n - 1:
                x = rect.x() + pad_l + uw * i / max(n - 1, 1)
                painter.drawText(
                    QRectF(x - 20, rect.bottom() - pad_bot, 40, pad_bot),
                    Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignHCenter | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter,
                    label,
                )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# KPI strip — compact horizontal row of metric chips
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class _ExpenseKPI(QFrame):
    """Compact KPI chip: label + value (+ optional delta)."""

    def __init__(self, label: str, theme: dict) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self._t = theme
        self.setObjectName("kpiChip")

        lay = QVBoxLayout(self)
        lay.setContentsMargins(16, 12, 16, 12)
        lay.setSpacing(4)

        self._lbl = QLabel(label)
        self._lbl.setObjectName("kpiChipLabel")

        self._val = QLabel("—")
        self._val.setObjectName("kpiChipValue")

        self._delta = QLabel("")
        self._delta.setObjectName("kpiChipDelta")
        self._delta.setVisible(False)

        lay.addWidget(self._lbl)
        lay.addWidget(self._val)
        lay.addWidget(self._delta)

        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding,
                           QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)
        apply_card_shadow(self, self._t, blur_radius=14, y_offset=3)

    def set_value(self, value: str,
                  delta: Optional[str] = None,
                  delta_good: bool = True) -> None:
        self._val.setText(value)
        if delta:
            self._delta.setText(delta)
            color = self._t.get("income" if delta_good else "expense", "#888")
            self._delta.setStyleSheet(f"color: {color};")
            self._delta.setVisible(True)
        else:
            self._delta.setVisible(False)

    def apply_theme(self, t: dict) -> None:
        self._t = t
        apply_card_shadow(self, t, blur_radius=14, y_offset=3)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main view
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Period options: (label, months_back_start, months_back_end)
_PERIODS = [
    ("Mes actual",       0,  0),
    ("Mes anterior",     1,  1),
    ("Últimos 3 meses",  2,  0),
    ("Últimos 6 meses",  5,  0),
    ("Año actual",      -1, -1),   # special: Jan 1 of current year
    ("Año anterior",    -2, -2),   # special: full previous year
    ("Todo",            -9, -9),   # special: all data
]


def _period_dates(option_idx: int) -> Tuple[date, date]:
    """Return (date_from, date_to) for the selected period index."""
    today  = date.today()
    _, code_s, code_e = _PERIODS[option_idx]

    if code_s == -9:    # All
        return date(2000, 1, 1), today

    if code_s == -1:    # Año actual
        return date(today.year, 1, 1), today

    if code_s == -2:    # Año anterior
        return date(today.year - 1, 1, 1), date(today.year - 1, 12, 31)

    def _month_start(months_back: int) -> date:
        m = today.month - months_back
        y = today.year
        while m <= 0:
            m += 12
            y -= 1
        return date(y, m, 1)

    def _month_end(d: date) -> date:
        import calendar
        return date(d.year, d.month, calendar.monthrange(d.year, d.month)[1])

    d_from = _month_start(code_s)
    if code_e == code_s:
        d_to = _month_end(d_from)
    else:
        d_to = today
    return d_from, d_to


def _prev_period(date_from: date, date_to: date) -> Tuple[date, date]:
    """Return a comparison period of identical length ending just before date_from."""
    span = (date_to - date_from).days + 1
    prev_to   = date_from - __import__("datetime").timedelta(days=1)
    prev_from = prev_to   - __import__("datetime").timedelta(days=span - 1)
    return prev_from, prev_to


class ExpensesView(QWidget):
    """
    Expenses analysis view — distribution charts, comparison, detail table.
    Auto-refreshes on showEvent (same pattern as TransactionsView).
    """

    CHART_DIST   = "dist"
    CHART_COMP   = "comp"
    CHART_LINE   = "line"
    CHART_TABLE  = "table"
    TRANSACTION_TYPE = TransactionType.expense
    VIEW_TITLE = "Gastos"
    TOTAL_LABEL = "Total gastos"
    MAX_LABEL = "Mayor gasto"
    TABLE_OBJECT_NAME = "expenseTable"
    AMOUNT_COLOR_KEY = "expense"
    AMOUNT_COLOR_FALLBACK = "#DC2626"
    LINE_TITLE = "Evolución diaria de gastos"
    LOG_NAME = "ExpensesView"
    DETAIL_HEADER = "Detalle de transacciones"

    def __init__(self, session: Session, user, theme_key: str) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self._session   = session
        self._user      = user
        self._theme_key = theme_key
        self._t         = THEMES[theme_key]
        self._period_idx = 0           # default: Mes actual
        self._chart_mode = self.CHART_DIST

        self._build_ui()
        self._apply_theme()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Qt lifecycle
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def showEvent(self, event) -> None:
        """Auto-refresh every time this view becomes visible."""
        super().showEvent(event)
        self._load_data()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # UI construction
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        root = QVBoxLayout(self)
        root.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        root.setSpacing(0)

        # ── Top bar ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
        top = QFrame()
        top.setObjectName("dashTopBar")
        top.setFixedHeight(52)
        tl = QHBoxLayout(top)
        tl.setContentsMargins(24, 0, 24, 0)
        tl.setSpacing(12)

        lbl_title = QLabel(self.VIEW_TITLE)
        lbl_title.setObjectName("dashTitle")
        tl.addWidget(lbl_title)

        tl.addSpacing(24)

        lbl_per = QLabel("Período:")
        lbl_per.setObjectName("filterLabel")
        tl.addWidget(lbl_per)

        self._cmb_period = QComboBox()
        self._cmb_period.setFixedHeight(32)
        self._cmb_period.setMinimumWidth(160)
        for label, _, _ in _PERIODS:
            self._cmb_period.addItem(label)
        self._cmb_period.currentIndexChanged.connect(self._on_period_changed)
        tl.addWidget(self._cmb_period)

        tl.addStretch()

        # Chart mode toggle buttons
        self._btn_group = QButtonGroup(self)
        self._btn_group.setExclusive(True)
        for mode, icon_name, tip in [
            (self.CHART_DIST, "mdi.chart-donut",   "Distribución por categoría"),
            (self.CHART_COMP, "mdi.chart-bar",      "Comparativa período anterior"),
            (self.CHART_LINE, "mdi.chart-line",     "Evolución diaria"),
            (self.CHART_TABLE, "mdi.table-large",   "Vista en tabla"),
        ]:
            btn = QPushButton()
            btn.setFixedSize(32, 32)
            btn.setCheckable(True)
            btn.setFlat(True)
            btn.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
            btn.setToolTip(tip)
            btn.setProperty("chart_mode", mode)
            btn.setObjectName("chartToggleBtn")
            self._btn_group.addButton(btn)
            tl.addWidget(btn)
            if mode == self.CHART_DIST:
                btn.setChecked(True)
                self._active_toggle_btn = btn

        self._btn_group.buttonClicked.connect(self._on_chart_toggle)

        root.addWidget(top)

        # ── Scroll area ───────────────────────────────────────────────
        scroll = QScrollArea()
        scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        scroll.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.NoFrame)

        content = QWidget()
        self._content = QVBoxLayout(content)
        self._content.setContentsMargins(24, 20, 24, 24)
        self._content.setSpacing(16)

        # KPI strip
        kpi_row = QHBoxLayout()
        kpi_row.setSpacing(12)
        self._kpis: Dict[str, _ExpenseKPI] = {}
        for key, label in [
            ("total",     self.TOTAL_LABEL),
            ("count",     "Nº transacciones"),
            ("avg",       "Media / transacción"),
            ("max_txn",   self.MAX_LABEL),
            ("pct_cat",   "% categorizado"),
        ]:
            chip = _ExpenseKPI(label, self._t)
            self._kpis[key] = chip
            kpi_row.addWidget(chip)
        self._content.addLayout(kpi_row)

        # Distribution chart (category donut/hbar)
        self._chart_dist = _CategoryChartWidget(self._t)
        self._chart_dist.setMinimumHeight(300)
        self._content.addWidget(self._chart_dist)

        # Comparison chart
        self._chart_comp = _ComparisonChartWidget(self._t)
        self._content.addWidget(self._chart_comp)

        # Monthly category chart
        self._chart_monthly_cat = _MonthlyCategoryChartWidget(self._t)
        self._content.addWidget(self._chart_monthly_cat)

        # Line chart
        self._chart_line = _LineChartWidget(self._t)
        self._content.addWidget(self._chart_line)

        # Detail table header
        tbl_hdr = QHBoxLayout()
        lbl_tbl = QLabel(self.DETAIL_HEADER)
        lbl_tbl.setObjectName("sectionHeader")
        tbl_hdr.addWidget(lbl_tbl)
        tbl_hdr.addStretch()
        self._lbl_count = QLabel("")
        self._lbl_count.setObjectName("txnCount")
        tbl_hdr.addWidget(self._lbl_count)
        self._content.addLayout(tbl_hdr)

        # Detail table
        self._table = QTableWidget(0, 4)
        self._table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(
            ["Fecha", "Emisor / Concepto", "Categoría", "Importe"]
        )
        self._table.setEditTriggers(
            QAbstractItemView.EditTrigger.NoEditTriggers
        )
        self._table.setSelectionBehavior(
            QAbstractItemView.SelectionBehavior.SelectRows
        )
        self._table.setSelectionMode(QAbstractItemView.SelectionMode.SingleSelection)
        self._table.setShowGrid(False)
        self._table.verticalHeader().setVisible(False)
        self._table.setAlternatingRowColors(True)
        self._table.setObjectName(self.TABLE_OBJECT_NAME)
        self._table.setSortingEnabled(True)
        self._table.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding,
                                  QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding)

        hdr = self._table.horizontalHeader()
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(1, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(2, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(3, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)

        self._content.addWidget(self._table, 1)

        scroll.setWidget(content)
        root.addWidget(scroll)

        # Initial chart visibility
        self._refresh_chart_visibility()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Slots
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _on_period_changed(self, idx: int) -> None:
        self._period_idx = idx
        self._load_data()

    def _on_chart_toggle(self, btn: QPushButton) -> None:
        self._chart_mode = btn.property("chart_mode")
        self._refresh_chart_visibility()
        self._update_toggle_icons()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Data loading
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _load_data(self) -> None:
        date_from, date_to = _period_dates(self._period_idx)
        prev_from, prev_to = _prev_period(date_from, date_to)

        period_label   = _PERIODS[self._period_idx][0]
        logger.debug("ExpensesView: cargando período %s (%s → %s)",
                     period_label, date_from, date_to)

        try:
            summary = summarize_period(
                self._session, self._user.id, self.TRANSACTION_TYPE,
                date_from, date_to,
            )
            total_prev, count_prev = period_totals(
                self._session, self._user.id, self.TRANSACTION_TYPE,
                prev_from, prev_to,
            )
            total_cur = summary.total
            count_cur = summary.count
            pct_cat   = summary.categorized_pct

            # ── KPIs ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
            # Delta vs previous period
            if total_prev > 0:
                delta_pct = (total_cur - total_prev) / total_prev * 100
                delta_str = f"{'▲' if delta_pct > 0 else '▼'} {abs(delta_pct):.1f}% vs anterior"
                delta_good = delta_pct < 0   # less spending = good
            else:
                delta_str = None
                delta_good = True

            self._kpis["total"].set_value(
                _fmt_amount(total_cur), delta_str, delta_good
            )
            self._kpis["count"].set_value(
                str(count_cur),
                f"{'▲' if count_prev and count_cur > count_prev else '▼'} "
                f"{abs(count_cur - count_prev)} vs anterior"
                if count_prev else None,
                count_cur <= count_prev,
            )
            self._kpis["avg"].set_value(_fmt_amount(summary.average))
            self._kpis["max_txn"].set_value(_fmt_amount(summary.max_amount))
            self._kpis["pct_cat"].set_value(f"{pct_cat:.1f}%",
                                            None if pct_cat >= 80 else "⚠ Pendientes")

            # ── Distribution + comparison charts ──────────────────────
            dist_data = summary.by_category
            self._chart_dist.set_data(dist_data)
            self._chart_comp.set_data(
                dist_data,
                category_totals(
                    self._session, self._user.id, self.TRANSACTION_TYPE,
                    prev_from, prev_to,
                ),
                label_a=period_label,
                label_b="Período anterior",
            )

            # ── Monthly category breakdown ─────────────────────────────
            self._chart_monthly_cat.set_data([
                (month_start.strftime("%b %Y"), cat_items)
                for month_start, cat_items in summary.by_month_category
            ])

            # ── Line chart — daily aggregation (chronological) ────────
            self._chart_line.set_title(f"{self.LINE_TITLE} · {period_label}")
            self._chart_line.set_data([
                (day.strftime("%d/%m"), amount) for day, amount in summary.by_day
            ])

            # ── Detail table ──────────────────────────────────────────
            self._table.setSortingEnabled(False)
            self._table.setRowCount(0)

            for row in summary.latest:
                r = self._table.rowCount()
                self._table.insertRow(r)

                date_str = str(row.transaction_date)[:10]
                issuer   = row.description[:50]
                cat      = row.category or "—"
                amt_str  = _fmt_amount(row.amount)

                for col, (text, align) in enumerate([
                    (date_str, Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft),
                    (issuer,   Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft),
                    (cat,      Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft),
                    (amt_str,  Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight),
                ]):
                    item = QTableWidgetItem(text)
                    item.setTextAlignment(
                        align | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter
                    )
                    item.setFlags(Qt.ItemFlag.ItemIsEnabled |
                                  Qt.ItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable)
                    if col == 3:
                        item.setForeground(
                            QColor(self._t.get(self.AMOUNT_COLOR_KEY, self.AMOUNT_COLOR_FALLBACK))
                        )
                    self._table.setItem(r, col, item)

            self._table.setSortingEnabled(True)
            self._table.resizeRowsToContents()
            self._lbl_count.setText(
                f"{count_cur} transacciones  •  {date_from} → {date_to}"
            )

            logger.debug(
                "ExpensesView: %d transacciones cargadas, total=%.2f€",
                count_cur, total_cur,
            )

        except Exception as exc:
            self._session.rollback()
            logger.exception("ExpensesView._load_data error: %s", exc)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Chart toggle helpers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _refresh_chart_visibility(self) -> None:
        self._chart_dist.setVisible(self._chart_mode == self.CHART_DIST)
        self._chart_comp.setVisible(self._chart_mode == self.CHART_COMP)
        self._chart_line.setVisible(self._chart_mode == self.CHART_LINE)
        self._table.setVisible(self._chart_mode == self.CHART_TABLE)

    def _update_toggle_icons(self) -> None:
        for btn in self._btn_group.buttons():
            mode   = btn.property("chart_mode")
            active = (mode == self._chart_mode)
            color  = self._t.get("accent" if active else "text_secondary",
                                 "#888")
            icon_map = {
                self.CHART_DIST: "mdi.chart-donut",
                self.CHART_COMP: "mdi.chart-bar",
                self.CHART_LINE: "mdi.chart-line",
                self.CHART_TABLE: "mdi.table-large",
            }
            btn.setIcon(qta.icon(icon_map[mode], color=color))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Theme
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def apply_theme(self, theme_key: str) -> None:
        self._theme_key = theme_key
        self._t         = THEMES[theme_key]
        self._apply_theme()

    def _apply_theme(self) -> None:
        t = self._t

        # Refresh toggle icons
        self._update_toggle_icons()

        # Refresh charts
        if hasattr(self, "_chart_dist"):
            self._chart_dist.apply_theme(t)
        if hasattr(self, "_chart_comp"):
            self._chart_comp.apply_theme(t)
        if hasattr(self, "_chart_monthly_cat"):
            self._chart_monthly_cat.apply_theme(t)
        if hasattr(self, "_chart_line"):
            self._chart_line.apply_theme(t)
        for chip in self._kpis.values():
            chip.apply_theme(t)

        self.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QWidget {{
                background-color: {t["bg_primary"]};
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-family: "Segoe UI";
                font-size: 13px;
            }}
            QFrame#dashTopBar {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border-bottom: 1px solid {t["border"]};
            }}
            QLabel#dashTitle {{
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700;
            }}
            QLabel#filterLabel {{
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 12px;
            }}
            QLabel#txnCount {{
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 12px;
            }}
            QLabel#sectionHeader {{
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 11px; font-weight: 600;
                text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1px;
            }}
            QFrame#kpiChip {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 8px;
            }}
            QLabel#kpiChipLabel {{
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 11px;
            }}
            QLabel#kpiChipValue {{
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 18px; font-weight: 700;
            }}
            QLabel#kpiChipDelta {{
                font-size: 11px;
            }}
            QFrame#chartCard {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 10px;
            }}
            QLabel#chartTitle {{
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600;
            }}
            QPushButton#chartToggleBtn {{
                border: none;
                border-radius: 6px;
                background-color: transparent;
            }}
            QPushButton#chartToggleBtn:checked {{
                background-color: {t["bg_hover"]};
            }}
            QPushButton#chartToggleBtn:hover {{
                background-color: {t["bg_hover"]};
            }}
            QComboBox {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 6px;
                padding: 0 10px;
            }}
            QTableWidget#{self.TABLE_OBJECT_NAME} {{
                background-color: {t["bg_primary"]};
                alternate-background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 8px;
                gridline-color: {t["border"]};
                font-size: 12px;
                selection-background-color: {t["accent"]};
                selection-color: #FFFFFF;
            }}
            QTableWidget#{self.TABLE_OBJECT_NAME}::item {{
                padding: 6px 8px;
                border: none;
            }}
            QTableWidget#{self.TABLE_OBJECT_NAME}::item:alternate {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
            }}
            QTableWidget#{self.TABLE_OBJECT_NAME}::item:selected {{
                background-color: {t["accent"]};
                color: #FFFFFF;
            }}
            QHeaderView::section {{
                background-color: {t["bg_hover"]};
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 11px; font-weight: 600;
                border: none; padding: 5px 8px;
            }}
        """)
```

```python
"""Income analysis view.

The income screen deliberately shares the complete period/filter/chart
implementation with the expense screen so both financial views stay aligned.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.db.models import TransactionType
from app.gui.expenses_view import ExpensesView


class IncomesWindow(ExpensesView):
    """Income analysis using the expense view's period-aware presentation."""

    TRANSACTION_TYPE = TransactionType.income
    VIEW_TITLE = "Ingresos"
    TOTAL_LABEL = "Total ingresos"
    MAX_LABEL = "Mayor ingreso"
    TABLE_OBJECT_NAME = "incomeTable"
    AMOUNT_COLOR_KEY = "income"
    AMOUNT_COLOR_FALLBACK = "#16A34A"
    LINE_TITLE = "Evolución diaria de ingresos"
    LOG_NAME = "IncomesWindow"
    DETAIL_HEADER = "Detalle de ingresos"

    def __init__(
        self,
        user,
        session: Session,
        theme: str = "light",
        parent=None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(session, user, theme)
        if parent is not None:
            self.setParent(parent)

    def load_entries(self) -> None:
        """Compatibility alias for callers of the previous income view."""
        self._load_data()
```

## `app/gui/categories_view.py`

```python
"""
app/gui/categories_view.py
===========================
FAMILYFIN — Categories & Subcategories View (Fase 4b)

Features:
  - Tree view: Type → Category hierarchy
  - Add / Rename / Delete categories
  - Seed default categories button (first-time setup)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Optional

from PySide6.QtCore    import Qt
from PySide6.QtGui     import QFont, QStandardItemModel, QStandardItem
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel,
    QPushButton, QFrame, QTreeView, QHeaderView,
    QInputDialog, QMessageBox, QSizePolicy,
)
import qtawesome as qta

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.config      import THEMES
from app.db.models   import Category, CategoryLevel, User
from app.gui._style_utils import apply_card_shadow
from app.categorization.category_manager import (
    get_all_categories, create_category,
    update_category_name, delete_category,
    seed_default_categories,
    seed_default_rules,
)


class CategoriesView(QWidget):

    def __init__(self, user: User, session: Session,
                 theme: str = "light") -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self._user    = user
        self._session = session
        self._theme   = theme
        self._t       = THEMES[theme]

        self._build_ui()
        self._apply_theme()
        self.refresh()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # UI construction
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        root = QVBoxLayout(self)
        root.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        root.setSpacing(0)

        root.addWidget(self._make_topbar())

        # Body — tree + action panel side by side
        body = QHBoxLayout()
        body.setContentsMargins(24, 16, 24, 16)
        body.setSpacing(16)

        # Tree view
        self._tree = QTreeView()
        self._tree.setHeaderHidden(True)
        self._tree.setEditTriggers(QTreeView.EditTrigger.NoEditTriggers)
        self._tree.setAlternatingRowColors(True)
        self._tree.setAnimated(True)
        self._tree.setObjectName("catTree")
        self._tree.setSizePolicy(
            QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding
        )
        self._tree_model = QStandardItemModel()
        self._tree.setModel(self._tree_model)
        self._tree.selectionModel().currentChanged.connect(
            self._on_selection_changed
        )
        body.addWidget(self._tree, stretch=2)

        # Action panel
        self._action_panel = self._make_action_panel()
        body.addWidget(self._action_panel, stretch=1)

        body_widget = QWidget()
        body_widget.setLayout(body)
        root.addWidget(body_widget)

    def _make_topbar(self) -> QFrame:
        bar = QFrame()
        bar.setObjectName("dashTopBar")
        bar.setFixedHeight(56)
        layout = QHBoxLayout(bar)
        layout.setContentsMargins(24, 0, 24, 0)

        lbl = QLabel("Categorías")
        lbl.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 15, QFont.Weight.Bold))
        lbl.setObjectName("dashTitle")
        layout.addWidget(lbl)

        layout.addStretch()

        btn_seed = QPushButton("  Cargar categorías por defecto")
        btn_seed.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.database-import-outline", color="#FFFFFF")
        )
        btn_seed.setFixedHeight(34)
        btn_seed.setObjectName("btnPrimary")
        btn_seed.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_seed.clicked.connect(self._on_seed)
        layout.addWidget(btn_seed)

        return bar

    def _make_action_panel(self) -> QFrame:
        panel = QFrame()
        panel.setObjectName("actionPanel")
        panel.setMinimumWidth(220)
        layout = QVBoxLayout(panel)
        layout.setContentsMargins(16, 16, 16, 16)
        layout.setSpacing(12)

        lbl = QLabel("Acciones")
        lbl.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 13, QFont.Weight.Bold))
        lbl.setObjectName("panelTitle")
        layout.addWidget(lbl)

        sep = QFrame()
        sep.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.HLine)
        sep.setObjectName("panelSep")
        layout.addWidget(sep)

        # Selected info
        self._lbl_selected = QLabel("Selecciona una categoría")
        self._lbl_selected.setObjectName("selectedInfo")
        self._lbl_selected.setWordWrap(True)
        layout.addWidget(self._lbl_selected)

        layout.addSpacing(8)

        # Add subcategory
        self._btn_add_sub = QPushButton("  Añadir subcategoría")
        self._btn_add_sub.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.plus", color="#FFFFFF")
        )
        self._btn_add_sub.setFixedHeight(38)
        self._btn_add_sub.setObjectName("btnPrimary")
        self._btn_add_sub.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self._btn_add_sub.setEnabled(False)
        self._btn_add_sub.clicked.connect(self._on_add_sub)
        layout.addWidget(self._btn_add_sub)

        # Rename
        self._btn_rename = QPushButton("  Renombrar")
        self._btn_rename.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.pencil-outline",
                     color=self._t["text_secondary"])
        )
        self._btn_rename.setFixedHeight(38)
        self._btn_rename.setObjectName("btnSecondary")
        self._btn_rename.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self._btn_rename.setEnabled(False)
        self._btn_rename.clicked.connect(self._on_rename)
        layout.addWidget(self._btn_rename)

        # Delete
        self._btn_delete = QPushButton("  Eliminar")
        self._btn_delete.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.delete-outline", color="#FFFFFF")
        )
        self._btn_delete.setFixedHeight(38)
        self._btn_delete.setObjectName("btnDanger")
        self._btn_delete.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self._btn_delete.setEnabled(False)
        self._btn_delete.clicked.connect(self._on_delete)
        layout.addWidget(self._btn_delete)

        layout.addStretch()

        return panel

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Data loading
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def refresh(self) -> None:
        self._tree_model.clear()
        self._cat_id_map: dict = {}  # item -> category_id

        types = get_all_categories(
            self._session, self._user.id, level=CategoryLevel.type
        )
        cats  = get_all_categories(
            self._session, self._user.id, level=CategoryLevel.category
        )

        # Group categories by parent_id
        by_parent: dict = {}
        for cat in cats:
            by_parent.setdefault(cat.parent_id, []).append(cat)

        type_icons = {
            "GASTO":          ("mdi.trending-down",  self._t["expense"]),
            "INGRESO":        ("mdi.trending-up",    self._t["income"]),
            "INVERSION":      ("mdi.chart-line",     self._t["investment"]),
            "SERV. BANCARIO": ("mdi.bank-outline",   self._t["accent"]),
        }

        for type_node in types:
            icon_name, color = type_icons.get(
                type_node.name,
                ("mdi.tag-outline", self._t["accent"])
            )
            type_item = QStandardItem(
                qta.icon(icon_name, color=color), type_node.name
            )
            type_item.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 12, QFont.Weight.Bold))
            type_item.setData(type_node.id, Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole)
            type_item.setData("type", Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole + 1)
            self._tree_model.appendRow(type_item)

            for cat in by_parent.get(type_node.id, []):
                cat_item = QStandardItem(
                    qta.icon("mdi.tag-outline",
                             color=self._t["text_secondary"]),
                    cat.name
                )
                cat_item.setData(cat.id, Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole)
                cat_item.setData("category", Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole + 1)
                type_item.appendRow(cat_item)

        self._tree.collapseAll()
        self._btn_add_sub.setEnabled(False)
        self._btn_rename.setEnabled(False)
        self._btn_delete.setEnabled(False)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Slots
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _on_selection_changed(self, current, previous) -> None:
        item = self._tree_model.itemFromIndex(current)
        if item is None:
            return

        level    = item.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole + 1)
        cat_name = item.text()

        self._lbl_selected.setText(
            f"<b>{cat_name}</b><br>"
            f"<small>Nivel: {level or '—'}</small>"
        )

        is_type     = level == "type"
        is_category = level == "category"

        self._btn_add_sub.setEnabled(is_type)
        self._btn_rename.setEnabled(is_type or is_category)
        self._btn_delete.setEnabled(is_category)

    def _on_add_sub(self) -> None:
        idx  = self._tree.currentIndex()
        item = self._tree_model.itemFromIndex(idx)
        if item is None:
            return
        parent_id = item.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole)

        name, ok = QInputDialog.getText(
            self, "Nueva categoría",
            "Nombre de la nueva categoría:"
        )
        if not ok or not name.strip():
            return
        try:
            create_category(
                self._session, self._user.id,
                name.strip(), CategoryLevel.category,
                parent_id=parent_id,
            )
            self.refresh()
        except ValueError as exc:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Error", str(exc))

    def _on_rename(self) -> None:
        idx  = self._tree.currentIndex()
        item = self._tree_model.itemFromIndex(idx)
        if item is None:
            return
        cat_id   = item.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole)
        old_name = item.text()

        name, ok = QInputDialog.getText(
            self, "Renombrar",
            "Nuevo nombre:", text=old_name
        )
        if not ok or not name.strip():
            return
        try:
            update_category_name(self._session, cat_id, name.strip())
            self.refresh()
        except ValueError as exc:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Error", str(exc))

    def _on_delete(self) -> None:
        idx  = self._tree.currentIndex()
        item = self._tree_model.itemFromIndex(idx)
        if item is None:
            return
        cat_id   = item.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole)
        cat_name = item.text()

        reply = QMessageBox.question(
            self, "Confirmar eliminación",
            f"¿Eliminar la categoría <b>{cat_name}</b>?<br>"
            "Las transacciones asignadas quedarán sin categorizar.",
            QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes |
            QMessageBox.StandardButton.No
        )
        if reply != QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes:
            return
        try:
            delete_category(self._session, cat_id)
            self.refresh()
        except ValueError as exc:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Error", str(exc))

    def _on_seed(self) -> None:
        reply = QMessageBox.question(
            self, "Cargar categorías por defecto",
            "Se añadirán las categorías estándar de FAMILYFIN "
            "(solo si aún no existen).<br>¿Continuar?",
            QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes |
            QMessageBox.StandardButton.No
        )
        if reply != QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes:
            return

        created = seed_default_categories(
            self._session, self._user.id
        )
        rules_created = seed_default_rules(
            self._session, self._user.id
        )
        self.refresh()
        if created > 0 or rules_created > 0:
            QMessageBox.information(
                self, "Categorías cargadas",
                f"Se crearon {created} categorías y {rules_created} reglas "
                "por defecto."
            )
        else:
            QMessageBox.information(
                self, "Sin cambios",
                "Las categorías y reglas por defecto ya estaban creadas."
            )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Theme
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def apply_theme(self, theme_key: str) -> None:
        self._theme = theme_key
        self._t = THEMES[theme_key]
        self._apply_theme()

    def _apply_theme(self) -> None:
        t = self._t
        is_dark = self._theme == "dark"
        selection_bg = (
            f"background-color: {t['accent']}; color: #FFFFFF;"
            if is_dark else
            f"background: qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:1, y2:0, "
            f"stop:0 {t['selection_grad_start']}, stop:1 {t['selection_grad_end']}); "
            f"color: {t['selection_text']};"
        )
        selection_hover = (
            f"background-color: {t['accent_hover']}; color: #FFFFFF;"
            if is_dark else
            f"background: qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:1, y2:0, "
            f"stop:0 {t['selection_hover_start']}, stop:1 {t['selection_hover_end']}); "
            f"color: {t['selection_text']};"
        )
        self.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QFrame#dashTopBar {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border-bottom: 1px solid {t["border"]};
            }}
            QLabel#dashTitle {{
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700;
            }}
            QPushButton#btnPrimary {{
                background-color: {t["accent"]}; color: #FFFFFF;
                border: none; border-radius: 8px;
                font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600;
                padding: 0 16px;
            }}
            QPushButton#btnPrimary:hover {{
                background-color: {t["accent_hover"]};
            }}
            QPushButton#btnSecondary {{
                background-color: transparent;
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 8px; font-size: 13px;
                padding: 0 16px;
            }}
            QPushButton#btnSecondary:hover {{
                background-color: {t["bg_hover"]};
            }}
            QPushButton#btnDanger {{
                background-color: {t["expense"]}; color: #FFFFFF;
                border: none; border-radius: 8px;
                font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600;
                padding: 0 16px;
            }}
            QPushButton#btnDanger:disabled {{
                background-color: {t["border"]};
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
            }}
            QTreeView#catTree {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 8px;
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 13px;
                alternate-background-color: {t["bg_primary"]};
            }}
            QTreeView#catTree::item {{
                padding: 6px 8px;
                border-radius: 4px;
            }}
            QTreeView#catTree::item:hover {{
                background-color: {t["bg_hover"]};
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
            }}
            QTreeView#catTree::item:selected {{
                {selection_bg}
            }}
            QTreeView#catTree::item:selected:hover {{
                {selection_hover}
            }}
            QFrame#actionPanel {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 12px;
            }}
            QLabel#panelTitle {{
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 13px; font-weight: 700;
            }}
            QLabel#selectedInfo {{
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 12px;
            }}
            QFrame#panelSep {{
                color: {t["border"]};
                background-color: {t["border"]};
                max-height: 1px;
            }}
        """)
        apply_card_shadow(self._action_panel, t)
```

## `app/gui/import_view.py`

```python
"""
app/gui/import_view.py
=======================
FAMILYFIN — Import View (Fase 4b)

Flow:
  Step 1 — File selection  : Browse for .xlsx file
  Step 2 — Preview         : Table showing new vs duplicate rows
                             + duplicate resolution dialogs
  Step 3 — Confirm         : Commit to DB, show import summary

Design decisions:
  - Uses prepare_import() (no DB writes) for preview
  - Uses commit_import() for final DB writes
  - Preview table columns: Fecha | Emisor | Concepto | Importe | Tipo | Saldo
  - Duplicates flagged in orange; user resolves via inline checkbox
  - Auto-categorization runs after commit
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from typing import Optional, Dict, List

from PySide6.QtCore    import Qt, QThread, Signal, QObject
from PySide6.QtGui     import QFont, QColor
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel,
    QPushButton, QFrame, QFileDialog, QTableWidget,
    QTableWidgetItem, QHeaderView, QProgressBar,
    QMessageBox, QSizePolicy, QStackedWidget,
    QAbstractItemView, QCheckBox, QScrollArea,
)
import qtawesome as qta

from app.config      import THEMES
from app.db.models   import User, Account, TransactionType
from sqlalchemy.orm  import Session


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Background worker for parse + validate (keeps GUI responsive)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class _ParseWorker(QObject):
    finished = Signal(object)   # ImportPreview or Exception
    error    = Signal(str)

    def __init__(self, file_path: str, user_id: int, account_id: int) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self._file_path  = file_path
        self._user_id    = user_id
        self._account_id = account_id

    def run(self) -> None:
        # A SQLAlchemy Session is not thread-safe: this worker thread opens
        # its own short-lived session instead of using the GUI's shared one.
        from app.db.database import get_session
        from app.ingestion.importer import prepare_import

        session = get_session()
        try:
            preview = prepare_import(
                file_path  = self._file_path,
                session    = session,
                user_id    = self._user_id,
                account_id = self._account_id,
            )
            self.finished.emit(preview)
        except Exception as exc:
            self.error.emit(str(exc))
        finally:
            session.close()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main Import View
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class ImportView(QWidget):
    """Three-step import wizard embedded in the main stacked view."""

    STEP_SELECT  = 0
    STEP_PREVIEW = 1
    STEP_SUMMARY = 2

    def __init__(self, user: User, session: Session,
                 theme: str = "light") -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self._user     = user
        self._session  = session
        self._theme    = theme
        self._t        = THEMES[theme]
        self._preview  = None   # ImportPreview object
        self._decisions: Dict[int, bool] = {}  # row_idx -> import (True) / skip (False)
        self._account_id: Optional[int] = None

        self._build_ui()
        self._apply_theme()
        self._ensure_account()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # UI construction
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        root = QVBoxLayout(self)
        root.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        root.setSpacing(0)

        # Top bar
        topbar = self._make_topbar()
        root.addWidget(topbar)

        # Step indicator
        self._step_bar = self._make_step_bar()
        root.addWidget(self._step_bar)

        # Stacked steps
        self._stack = QStackedWidget()
        root.addWidget(self._stack)

        self._stack.addWidget(self._make_step_select())
        self._stack.addWidget(self._make_step_preview())
        self._stack.addWidget(self._make_step_summary())

        self._go_to_step(self.STEP_SELECT)

    def _make_topbar(self) -> QFrame:
        bar = QFrame()
        bar.setObjectName("dashTopBar")
        bar.setFixedHeight(56)
        layout = QHBoxLayout(bar)
        layout.setContentsMargins(24, 0, 24, 0)

        lbl = QLabel("Importar movimientos")
        lbl.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 15, QFont.Weight.Bold))
        lbl.setObjectName("dashTitle")
        layout.addWidget(lbl)
        layout.addStretch()
        return bar

    def _make_step_bar(self) -> QFrame:
        bar = QFrame()
        bar.setFixedHeight(48)
        bar.setObjectName("stepBar")
        layout = QHBoxLayout(bar)
        layout.setContentsMargins(24, 0, 24, 0)
        layout.setSpacing(0)

        steps = ["1  Seleccionar fichero",
                 "2  Previsualizar",
                 "3  Resultado"]
        self._step_labels: List[QLabel] = []
        for i, text in enumerate(steps):
            lbl = QLabel(text)
            lbl.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
            lbl.setObjectName(f"step_{i}")
            lbl.setFixedHeight(48)
            lbl.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding,
                              QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)
            layout.addWidget(lbl)
            self._step_labels.append(lbl)

            if i < len(steps) - 1:
                arrow = QLabel("›")
                arrow.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
                arrow.setFixedWidth(20)
                arrow.setObjectName("stepArrow")
                layout.addWidget(arrow)

        return bar

    def _make_step_select(self) -> QWidget:
        w = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(w)
        layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        layout.setSpacing(20)
        layout.setContentsMargins(48, 48, 48, 48)

        # Drop zone
        self._drop_zone = QFrame()
        self._drop_zone.setObjectName("dropZone")
        self._drop_zone.setFixedSize(480, 220)
        dz_layout = QVBoxLayout(self._drop_zone)
        dz_layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        dz_layout.setSpacing(12)

        lbl_icon = QLabel()
        lbl_icon.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        lbl_icon.setPixmap(
            qta.icon("mdi.file-excel-outline",
                     color=self._t["accent"]).pixmap(56, 56)
        )
        dz_layout.addWidget(lbl_icon)

        lbl_drop = QLabel("Selecciona el fichero de CaixaBank (.xls / .xlsx)")
        lbl_drop.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        lbl_drop.setObjectName("dropZoneText")
        dz_layout.addWidget(lbl_drop)

        lbl_hint = QLabel("Formato: export CaixaBank — 24 columnas sin cabecera")
        lbl_hint.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        lbl_hint.setObjectName("dropZoneHint")
        dz_layout.addWidget(lbl_hint)

        dz_layout.addSpacing(8)

        self._btn_browse = QPushButton(
            "  Buscar fichero..."
        )
        self._btn_browse.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.folder-open-outline", color="#FFFFFF")
        )
        self._btn_browse.setFixedHeight(40)
        self._btn_browse.setFixedWidth(200)
        self._btn_browse.setObjectName("btnPrimary")
        self._btn_browse.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self._btn_browse.clicked.connect(self._on_browse)
        dz_layout.addWidget(self._btn_browse,
                            alignment=Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)

        layout.addWidget(self._drop_zone)

        # Selected file label
        self._lbl_selected = QLabel("")
        self._lbl_selected.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        self._lbl_selected.setObjectName("selectedFile")
        layout.addWidget(self._lbl_selected)

        # Progress bar (hidden by default)
        self._progress = QProgressBar()
        self._progress.setFixedWidth(480)
        self._progress.setFixedHeight(6)
        self._progress.setTextVisible(False)
        self._progress.setRange(0, 0)   # indeterminate
        self._progress.setVisible(False)
        self._progress.setObjectName("importProgress")
        layout.addWidget(self._progress,
                         alignment=Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)

        # Parse button
        self._btn_parse = QPushButton("  Analizar fichero")
        self._btn_parse.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.magnify", color="#FFFFFF")
        )
        self._btn_parse.setFixedHeight(44)
        self._btn_parse.setFixedWidth(200)
        self._btn_parse.setObjectName("btnPrimary")
        self._btn_parse.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self._btn_parse.setEnabled(False)
        self._btn_parse.clicked.connect(self._on_parse)
        layout.addWidget(self._btn_parse,
                         alignment=Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)

        self._selected_path: Optional[str] = None
        return w

    def _make_step_preview(self) -> QWidget:
        w = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(w)
        layout.setContentsMargins(24, 16, 24, 16)
        layout.setSpacing(12)

        # Summary bar
        self._preview_summary = QFrame()
        self._preview_summary.setObjectName("previewSummary")
        self._preview_summary.setFixedHeight(52)
        sum_layout = QHBoxLayout(self._preview_summary)
        sum_layout.setContentsMargins(16, 0, 16, 0)
        sum_layout.setSpacing(24)

        self._lbl_total = self._make_summary_chip(
            "mdi.database-import-outline", "0 filas", "accent"
        )
        self._lbl_new = self._make_summary_chip(
            "mdi.plus-circle-outline", "0 nuevas", "income"
        )
        self._lbl_dups = self._make_summary_chip(
            "mdi.content-copy", "0 duplicados", "warning"
        )
        self._lbl_skip = self._make_summary_chip(
            "mdi.skip-next-outline", "0 omitidas", "neutral"
        )
        for chip in [self._lbl_total, self._lbl_new,
                     self._lbl_dups, self._lbl_skip]:
            sum_layout.addWidget(chip)
        sum_layout.addStretch()

        layout.addWidget(self._preview_summary)

        # Transaction table
        self._tbl_preview = QTableWidget()
        self._tbl_preview.setColumnCount(7)
        self._tbl_preview.setHorizontalHeaderLabels([
            "Fecha", "Emisor", "Concepto",
            "Importe", "Tipo", "Saldo", "Importar"
        ])
        self._tbl_preview.setEditTriggers(
            QAbstractItemView.EditTrigger.NoEditTriggers
        )
        self._tbl_preview.setSelectionBehavior(
            QAbstractItemView.SelectionBehavior.SelectRows
        )
        self._tbl_preview.setAlternatingRowColors(True)
        self._tbl_preview.verticalHeader().setVisible(False)
        self._tbl_preview.setShowGrid(False)

        hdr = self._tbl_preview.horizontalHeader()
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(1, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(2, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(3, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(4, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(5, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(6, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Fixed)
        self._tbl_preview.setColumnWidth(6, 80)

        layout.addWidget(self._tbl_preview)

        # Bottom button row
        btn_row = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_row.setSpacing(12)

        self._btn_back_select = QPushButton("  Volver")
        self._btn_back_select.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.arrow-left", color=self._t["text_secondary"])
        )
        self._btn_back_select.setFixedHeight(40)
        self._btn_back_select.setObjectName("btnSecondary")
        self._btn_back_select.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self._btn_back_select.clicked.connect(
            lambda: self._go_to_step(self.STEP_SELECT)
        )

        self._btn_commit = QPushButton("  Confirmar importación")
        self._btn_commit.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.database-check-outline", color="#FFFFFF")
        )
        self._btn_commit.setFixedHeight(40)
        self._btn_commit.setObjectName("btnPrimary")
        self._btn_commit.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self._btn_commit.clicked.connect(self._on_commit)

        btn_row.addWidget(self._btn_back_select)
        btn_row.addStretch()
        btn_row.addWidget(self._btn_commit)
        layout.addLayout(btn_row)

        return w

    def _make_step_summary(self) -> QWidget:
        w = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(w)
        layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        layout.setSpacing(20)
        layout.setContentsMargins(48, 48, 48, 48)

        self._lbl_summary_icon = QLabel()
        self._lbl_summary_icon.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        layout.addWidget(self._lbl_summary_icon)

        self._lbl_summary_title = QLabel("")
        self._lbl_summary_title.setFont(
            QFont("Segoe UI", 18, QFont.Weight.Bold)
        )
        self._lbl_summary_title.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        layout.addWidget(self._lbl_summary_title)

        self._lbl_summary_detail = QLabel("")
        self._lbl_summary_detail.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        self._lbl_summary_detail.setObjectName("summaryDetail")
        self._lbl_summary_detail.setWordWrap(True)
        layout.addWidget(self._lbl_summary_detail)

        btn_new = QPushButton("  Nueva importación")
        btn_new.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.upload-outline", color="#FFFFFF")
        )
        btn_new.setFixedHeight(44)
        btn_new.setFixedWidth(220)
        btn_new.setObjectName("btnPrimary")
        btn_new.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_new.clicked.connect(
            lambda: self._go_to_step(self.STEP_SELECT)
        )
        layout.addWidget(btn_new, alignment=Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)

        return w

    def _make_summary_chip(self, icon_name: str,
                            text: str, color_key: str) -> QLabel:
        lbl = QLabel(f"  {text}")
        lbl.setObjectName(f"chip_{color_key}")
        return lbl

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Step navigation
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _go_to_step(self, step: int) -> None:
        self._stack.setCurrentIndex(step)
        t = self._t
        for i, lbl in enumerate(self._step_labels):
            if i == step:
                lbl.setStyleSheet(
                    f"color: {t['accent']}; font-weight: 700;"
                    f"border-bottom: 2px solid {t['accent']};"
                )
            else:
                lbl.setStyleSheet(
                    f"color: {t['text_secondary']}; font-weight: 400;"
                    "border-bottom: none;"
                )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Slots — Step 1
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _on_browse(self) -> None:
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(
            self, "Seleccionar fichero CaixaBank",
            os.path.expanduser("~"),
            "Ficheros CaixaBank (*.xls *.xlsx);;Todos los ficheros (*)"
        )
        if path:
            self._selected_path = path
            fname = os.path.basename(path)
            self._lbl_selected.setText(f"📄  {fname}")
            self._btn_parse.setEnabled(True)

    def _on_parse(self) -> None:
        if not self._selected_path:
            return
        if self._account_id is None:
            QMessageBox.warning(
                self, "Sin cuenta",
                "No se encontró ninguna cuenta bancaria. "
                "Por favor crea una cuenta antes de importar."
            )
            return

        self._btn_parse.setEnabled(False)
        self._btn_browse.setEnabled(False)
        self._progress.setVisible(True)

        # Run in background thread
        self._thread = QThread()
        from pathlib import Path
        self._worker = _ParseWorker(
            Path(self._selected_path), self._user.id, self._account_id
        )
        self._worker.moveToThread(self._thread)
        self._thread.started.connect(self._worker.run)
        self._worker.finished.connect(self._on_parse_done)
        self._worker.error.connect(self._on_parse_error)
        self._worker.finished.connect(self._thread.quit)
        self._worker.error.connect(self._thread.quit)
        self._thread.start()

    def _on_parse_done(self, preview) -> None:
        self._progress.setVisible(False)
        self._btn_parse.setEnabled(True)
        self._btn_browse.setEnabled(True)
        self._preview = preview
        self._populate_preview_table(preview)
        self._go_to_step(self.STEP_PREVIEW)

    def _on_parse_error(self, error_msg: str) -> None:
        self._progress.setVisible(False)
        self._btn_parse.setEnabled(True)
        self._btn_browse.setEnabled(True)
        QMessageBox.critical(
            self, "Error de validación", error_msg
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Preview table population
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _populate_preview_table(self, preview) -> None:
        """
        Populate the preview table from an ImportPreview object.

        ImportPreview structure (real):
          preview.df                  → pd.DataFrame (all rows)
          preview.duplicate_result    → DuplicateCheckResult
            .clean        → List[int]  row indices safe to import
            .auto_skip    → List[int]  certain duplicates (auto-skipped)
            .needs_review → List[int]  possible duplicates (user decides)
            .review_reasons → dict[int, str]
        """
        t          = self._t
        df         = preview.df
        dup_result = preview.duplicate_result

        n_total    = len(df)
        n_skip     = len(dup_result.auto_skip)
        n_review   = len(dup_result.needs_review)
        n_clean    = len(dup_result.clean)

        self._lbl_total.setText(f"  {n_total} filas")
        self._lbl_new.setText(f"  {n_clean} nuevas")
        self._lbl_dups.setText(f"  {n_review} posibles duplicados")
        self._lbl_skip.setText(f"  {n_skip} omitidas (exactas)")

        # Only show clean + needs_review rows (auto_skip are hidden)
        visible_indices = sorted(dup_result.clean + dup_result.needs_review)

        self._tbl_preview.setRowCount(len(visible_indices))
        self._decisions = {}

        type_labels = {
            TransactionType.income:     "Ingreso",
            TransactionType.expense:    "Gasto",
            TransactionType.investment: "Inversión",
        }

        for tbl_row, df_idx in enumerate(visible_indices):
            row       = df.iloc[df_idx]
            is_review = df_idx in dup_result.needs_review

            # -- Fecha
            d = row.get("transaction_date", "")
            item_date = QTableWidgetItem(str(d) if d else "")

            # -- Emisor
            item_issuer = QTableWidgetItem(
                str(row.get("issuer_name", "") or "")[:40]
            )

            # -- Concepto
            item_concept = QTableWidgetItem(
                str(row.get("concept", "") or "")[:80]
            )

            # -- Importe
            amount = float(row.get("amount", 0.0) or 0.0)
            item_amount = QTableWidgetItem(self._fmt_amount(amount))
            item_amount.setTextAlignment(
                Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight |
                Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter
            )
```
