```python
        actions.setSpacing(12)
        self._btn_backup_now = QPushButton("Crear respaldo ahora")
        self._btn_backup_now.setObjectName("btnPrimary")
        self._btn_backup_now.clicked.connect(self._create_backup)
        actions.addWidget(self._btn_backup_now)

        self._btn_restore_now = QPushButton("Restaurar respaldo")
        self._btn_restore_now.clicked.connect(self._restore_backup)
        actions.addWidget(self._btn_restore_now)
        actions.addStretch()
        layout.addLayout(actions)

        layout.addWidget(group)
        return page

    def _load_into_form(self) -> None:
        self._language_cb.setCurrentText("Español" if self._settings.language == "es" else "English")
        self._default_period_cb.setCurrentText(self._settings.default_period)
        self._currency_cb.setCurrentText({"EUR": "EUR (€)", "USD": "USD ($)", "GBP": "GBP (£)"}.get(self._settings.default_currency, "EUR (€)"))
        self._theme_cb.setCurrentText("Claro" if self._settings.theme == "light" else "Oscuro")
        self._save_appearance_start_cb.setChecked(self._settings.save_appearance_on_start)
        self._show_hints_cb.setChecked(self._settings.enable_hints)
        self._import_folder_edit.setText(self._settings.import_folder)
        self._export_folder_edit.setText(self._settings.export_folder)
        self._data_folder_edit.setText(self._settings.data_folder or str(get_default_data_dir()))
        self._backup_folder_edit.setText(self._settings.backup_folder)
        self._require_password_cb.setChecked(self._settings.require_password_on_start)
        self._protect_data_cb.setChecked(self._settings.protect_sensitive_data)
        self._auto_backup_cb.setChecked(self._settings.auto_backup)
        self._backup_interval_spin.setValue(self._settings.backup_interval_hours)
        self._weekly_summary_cb.setChecked(self._settings.weekly_summary_email)

    def _collect_from_form(self) -> None:
        settings = self._settings
        settings.language = "es" if self._language_cb.currentText() == "Español" else "en"
        settings.default_period = self._default_period_cb.currentText()
        settings.default_currency = {
            "EUR (€)": "EUR",
            "USD ($)": "USD",
            "GBP (£)": "GBP",
        }[self._currency_cb.currentText()]
        settings.theme = "light" if self._theme_cb.currentText() == "Claro" else "dark"
        settings.save_appearance_on_start = self._save_appearance_start_cb.isChecked()
        settings.enable_hints = self._show_hints_cb.isChecked()
        settings.import_folder = self._import_folder_edit.text().strip()
        settings.export_folder = self._export_folder_edit.text().strip()
        settings.data_folder = self._data_folder_edit.text().strip()
        settings.backup_folder = self._backup_folder_edit.text().strip()
        settings.require_password_on_start = self._require_password_cb.isChecked()
        settings.protect_sensitive_data = self._protect_data_cb.isChecked()
        settings.auto_backup = self._auto_backup_cb.isChecked()
        settings.backup_interval_hours = self._backup_interval_spin.value()
        settings.weekly_summary_email = self._weekly_summary_cb.isChecked()
        self._settings = settings

    def _browse_folder(self, field: QLineEdit) -> None:
        folder = QFileDialog.getExistingDirectory(self, "Seleccionar carpeta", field.text() or ".")
        if folder:
            field.setText(folder)

    def _detect_data_folder(self) -> None:
        candidates = []
        for folder in (get_legacy_data_dir(), get_default_data_dir()):
            if database_has_users(folder / "profin.db"):
                candidates.append(folder)

        if not candidates:
            QMessageBox.information(
                self,
                "Base de datos no encontrada",
                "No se encontró una base de datos con usuarios válidos en las rutas conocidas del proyecto ni del usuario local.",
            )
            return

        chosen = sorted(candidates, key=lambda path: len(str(path))) [0]
        self._data_folder_edit.setText(str(chosen))
        QMessageBox.information(
            self,
            "Base de datos detectada",
            f"Se ha localizado la base de datos actual en:\n{chosen}",
        )

    def _save_settings(self) -> None:
        self._collect_from_form()
        # save() overwrites settings/data_folder, so remember the folder in use.
        current_data_dir = get_user_data_dir()
        self._settings_manager.ensure_dirs(self._settings)
        self._settings_manager.save(self._settings)

        qsettings = QSettings("GEVernova", "FAMILYFIN")
        qsettings.setValue("ui/theme", self._settings.theme if self._settings.save_appearance_on_start else "light")
        qsettings.sync()

        parent = self.parentWidget()
        while parent is not None and not hasattr(parent, "_theme"):
            parent = parent.parentWidget()
        if parent is not None:
            parent._theme = self._settings.theme
            parent._t = THEMES[self._settings.theme]
            if hasattr(parent, "_apply_theme"):
                parent._apply_theme()
            if hasattr(parent, "_views"):
                for view in parent._views.values():
                    if hasattr(view, "apply_theme"):
                        view.apply_theme(self._settings.theme)
                    elif hasattr(view, "refresh_theme"):
                        view.refresh_theme(self._settings.theme)
        self.apply_theme(self._settings.theme)

        if self._settings.data_folder:
            try:
                self._switch_data_folder(self._settings.data_folder, current_data_dir)
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.exception("No se pudo cambiar la carpeta de datos.")
                QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error al migrar datos", str(exc))

    def _migrate_data_folder(self) -> None:
        target = self._data_folder_edit.text().strip()
        if not target:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Ubicación no válida", "Selecciona una carpeta para la ubicación de datos del usuario.")
            return
        try:
            self._settings.data_folder = target
            if not self._switch_data_folder(target, get_user_data_dir()):
                QMessageBox.information(self, "Ubicación de datos", f"Los datos ya se guardan en:\n{target}")
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception("No se pudo cambiar la carpeta de datos.")
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error al migrar datos", str(exc))

    def _switch_data_folder(self, target: str, current_dir: Path) -> bool:
        """Move the app to another data folder. Returns False if it was already in use.

        The live session is bound to the old database file, so after the
        switch the database is re-initialised (integrity check + schema
        migrations) and the user is logged out; logging in again opens a
        session on the new file and nothing more is written to the old one.
        """
        new_dir = self._settings_manager.apply_data_folder(target, current_dir=current_dir)
        if new_dir.resolve() == current_dir.resolve():
            return False
        self._session.commit()
        init_db()
        QMessageBox.information(
            self,
            "Ubicación de datos actualizada",
            "FAMILYFIN usará a partir de ahora la base de datos de:\n"
            f"{new_dir}\n\nVuelve a iniciar sesión para continuar.",
        )
        parent = self.parentWidget()
        while parent is not None and not hasattr(parent, "_on_logout"):
            parent = parent.parentWidget()
        if parent is not None:
            parent._on_logout()
        return True

    def _create_backup(self) -> None:
        self._collect_from_form()
        try:
            self._settings_manager.ensure_dirs(self._settings)
            target_dir = self._settings.backup_folder or self._settings.export_folder or "."
            backup_path = self._settings_manager.backup_all_data(target_dir, user_name=self._user.username)
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Copia de seguridad", f"Se creó la copia de seguridad en:\n{backup_path}")
        except Exception as exc:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error al crear la copia de seguridad", str(exc))

    def _restore_backup(self) -> None:
        backup_path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(
            self,
            "Selecciona un respaldo de FAMILYFIN",
            self._settings.backup_folder or ".",
            "Archivo ZIP (*.zip)",
        )
        if not backup_path:
            return
        try:
            ok = self._settings_manager.restore_all_data(backup_path)
            if ok:
                QMessageBox.information(
                    self,
                    "Restauración preparada",
                    "La copia de seguridad es válida y está preparada.\n\n"
                    "Cierra y vuelve a abrir FAMILYFIN para completar la restauración. "
                    "Los datos actuales se guardarán antes en una copia (profin.before-restore.*.db).",
                )
            else:
                QMessageBox.warning(self, "Restauración inválida", "El archivo de backup no es válido para FAMILYFIN.")
        except Exception as exc:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error al restaurar", str(exc))

    def apply_theme(self, theme_key: str) -> None:
        self._theme = theme_key
        self._t = THEMES[theme_key]
        self._apply_theme()

    def _apply_theme(self) -> None:
        t = self._t
        self.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QWidget {{
                background-color: {t["bg_primary"]};
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-family: "Segoe UI";
            }}
            QFrame#dashTopBar {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border-bottom: 1px solid {t["border"]};
            }}
            QLabel#dashTitle {{
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 15px; font-weight: 700;
            }}
            QFrame#settingsSidebar {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 10px;
            }}
            QPushButton#settingsNavButton {{
                text-align: left;
                padding-left: 12px;
                border-radius: 8px;
                background-color: transparent;
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 12px;
                font-weight: 600;
            }}
            QPushButton#settingsNavButton:checked {{
                background-color: {t["accent_light"]};
                color: {t["accent"]};
            }}
            QPushButton#btnPrimary {{
                background-color: {t["accent"]};
                color: #FFFFFF;
                border: none;
                border-radius: 8px;
                padding: 0 16px;
                font-weight: 600;
            }}
            QGroupBox {{
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 10px;
                margin-top: 12px;
                font-size: 12px;
                font-weight: 600;
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
            }}
            QGroupBox::title {{
                subcontrol-origin: margin;
                left: 12px;
                padding: 0 6px;
            }}
            QComboBox, QLineEdit, QSpinBox {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 7px;
                min-height: 32px;
                padding: 0 8px;
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
            }}
            QCheckBox {{
                spacing: 8px;
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
            }}
            QLabel {{
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
            }}
        """)
        apply_card_shadow(self._nav_panel, t)

```

## `app/gui/transactions_view.py`

```python
"""
app/gui/transactions_view.py
=============================
FAMILYFIN — Transactions View (Fase 4b)

Features:
  - Filterable/sortable table: Fecha | Emisor | Concepto | Importe
                               | Tipo | Categoría | Saldo
  - Inline category assignment via dropdown in Categoría column
  - Filter bar: search text + type filter + date range
  - Pagination for large datasets (100 rows per page)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date
from typing   import List, Optional

from PySide6.QtCore    import Qt, QSortFilterProxyModel, QDate
from PySide6.QtGui     import QFont, QColor, QStandardItemModel, QStandardItem
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel,
    QPushButton, QFrame, QTableView, QHeaderView,
    QLineEdit, QComboBox, QSizePolicy, QAbstractItemView,
    QDateEdit, QApplication,
)
import qtawesome as qta

from sqlalchemy        import or_
from sqlalchemy.orm    import Session

from app.config      import THEMES
from app.db.models   import (
    Transaction, TransactionType, Category, CategoryLevel, User
)
from app.categorization.category_manager import (
    assign_transaction_category, get_all_categories
)
from app.gui._style_utils import apply_card_shadow


PAGE_SIZE = 100


class TransactionsView(QWidget):

    def __init__(self, user: User, session: Session,
                 theme: str = "light") -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self._user    = user
        self._session = session
        self._theme   = theme
        self._t       = THEMES[theme]
        self._page    = 0
        self._total   = 0
        self._categories: List[Category] = []

        self._build_ui()
        self._apply_theme()
        self._load_categories()
        self.refresh()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # UI construction
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        root = QVBoxLayout(self)
        root.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        root.setSpacing(0)

        # Top bar
        root.addWidget(self._make_topbar())

        # Filter bar
        self._filter_bar = self._make_filter_bar()
        root.addWidget(self._filter_bar)

        # Table
        self._table = QTableView()
        self._table.setSelectionBehavior(
            QAbstractItemView.SelectionBehavior.SelectRows
        )
        self._table.setEditTriggers(
            QAbstractItemView.EditTrigger.NoEditTriggers
        )
        self._table.setAlternatingRowColors(True)
        self._table.setShowGrid(False)
        self._table.verticalHeader().setVisible(False)
        self._table.setSortingEnabled(True)
        self._table.horizontalHeader().setStretchLastSection(False)

        self._model = QStandardItemModel()
        self._model.setHorizontalHeaderLabels([
            "Fecha", "Emisor", "Concepto",
            "Importe", "Tipo", "Categoría", "Saldo"
        ])
        self._table.setModel(self._model)

        hdr = self._table.horizontalHeader()
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(1, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(2, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(3, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(4, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(5, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(6, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)

        self._table.doubleClicked.connect(self._on_row_double_clicked)
        root.addWidget(self._table)

        # Pagination bar
        self._pagination_bar = self._make_pagination_bar()
        root.addWidget(self._pagination_bar)

    def _make_topbar(self) -> QFrame:
        bar = QFrame()
        bar.setObjectName("dashTopBar")
        bar.setFixedHeight(56)
        layout = QHBoxLayout(bar)
        layout.setContentsMargins(24, 0, 24, 0)

        lbl = QLabel("Transacciones")
        lbl.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 15, QFont.Weight.Bold))
        lbl.setObjectName("dashTitle")
        layout.addWidget(lbl)

        layout.addStretch()

        self._lbl_count = QLabel("")
        self._lbl_count.setObjectName("txnCount")
        layout.addWidget(self._lbl_count)

        layout.addSpacing(12)

        btn_refresh = QPushButton()
        btn_refresh.setFixedSize(32, 32)
        btn_refresh.setFlat(True)
        btn_refresh.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_refresh.setToolTip("Actualizar — re-aplica reglas y recarga tabla")
        btn_refresh.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.refresh", color=self._t["text_secondary"])
        )
        btn_refresh.clicked.connect(self._refresh_with_rules)
        layout.addWidget(btn_refresh)
        self._btn_refresh_top = btn_refresh

        return bar

    def _make_filter_bar(self) -> QFrame:
        bar = QFrame()
        bar.setObjectName("filterBar")
        bar.setFixedHeight(52)
        layout = QHBoxLayout(bar)
        layout.setContentsMargins(16, 8, 16, 8)
        layout.setSpacing(10)

        # Search
        self._inp_search = QLineEdit()
        self._inp_search.setPlaceholderText("🔍  Buscar por emisor o concepto...")
        self._inp_search.setFixedHeight(34)
        self._inp_search.setObjectName("filterInput")
        self._inp_search.setMinimumWidth(240)
        self._inp_search.textChanged.connect(self._on_filter_changed)
        layout.addWidget(self._inp_search)

        # Type filter
        self._cmb_type = QComboBox()
        self._cmb_type.setFixedHeight(34)
        self._cmb_type.setObjectName("filterCombo")
        self._cmb_type.addItem("Todos los tipos", None)
        self._cmb_type.addItem("Ingresos",   TransactionType.income)
        self._cmb_type.addItem("Gastos",     TransactionType.expense)
        self._cmb_type.addItem("Inversiones",TransactionType.investment)
        self._cmb_type.currentIndexChanged.connect(self._on_filter_changed)
        layout.addWidget(self._cmb_type)

        # Category filter
        self._cmb_cat = QComboBox()
        self._cmb_cat.setFixedHeight(34)
        self._cmb_cat.setObjectName("filterCombo")
        self._cmb_cat.setMinimumWidth(160)
        self._cmb_cat.addItem("Todas las categorías", None)
        self._cmb_cat.addItem("Sin categorizar", -1)
        self._cmb_cat.currentIndexChanged.connect(self._on_filter_changed)
        layout.addWidget(self._cmb_cat)

        layout.addStretch()

        # Clear filters
        btn_clear = QPushButton("Limpiar filtros")
        btn_clear.setFixedHeight(34)
        btn_clear.setObjectName("btnSecondary")
        btn_clear.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_clear.clicked.connect(self._clear_filters)
        layout.addWidget(btn_clear)

        return bar

    def _make_pagination_bar(self) -> QFrame:
        bar = QFrame()
        bar.setObjectName("paginationBar")
        bar.setFixedHeight(44)
        layout = QHBoxLayout(bar)
        layout.setContentsMargins(16, 0, 16, 0)
        layout.setSpacing(8)

        self._btn_prev = QPushButton()
        self._btn_prev.setFixedSize(32, 32)
        self._btn_prev.setFlat(True)
        self._btn_prev.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.chevron-left", color=self._t["text_secondary"])
        )
        self._btn_prev.clicked.connect(self._prev_page)
        layout.addWidget(self._btn_prev)

        self._lbl_page = QLabel("Página 1")
        self._lbl_page.setObjectName("pageLabel")
        layout.addWidget(self._lbl_page)

        self._btn_next = QPushButton()
        self._btn_next.setFixedSize(32, 32)
        self._btn_next.setFlat(True)
        self._btn_next.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.chevron-right", color=self._t["text_secondary"])
        )
        self._btn_next.clicked.connect(self._next_page)
        layout.addWidget(self._btn_next)

        layout.addStretch()
        return bar

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Data loading
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _load_categories(self) -> None:
        """Load categories for the inline dropdown."""
        self._categories = get_all_categories(
            self._session, self._user.id, level=CategoryLevel.category
        )
        # Refresh category filter combo
        self._cmb_cat.clear()
        self._cmb_cat.addItem("Todas las categorías", None)
        self._cmb_cat.addItem("Sin categorizar", -1)
        for cat in self._categories:
            self._cmb_cat.addItem(cat.name, cat.id)

    def _refresh_with_rules(self) -> None:
        """
        Re-apply all active categorization rules to uncategorized
        transactions, then reload the table.
        Called by the top-bar refresh button.
        """
        import logging
        _log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

        try:
            from app.db.models import Transaction as TxnModel
            from app.categorization.rules_engine import apply_rules_and_commit

            # overwrite=False only ever fills empty categories, so only the
            # uncategorized rows need to go through the engine — not the
            # whole history on every visit to this tab.
            uncategorized_ids = [
                row.id for row in (
                    self._session.query(TxnModel.id)
                    .filter(
                        TxnModel.user_id == self._user.id,
                        TxnModel.category_id.is_(None),
                    )
                    .all()
                )
            ]

            if uncategorized_ids:
                result = apply_rules_and_commit(
                    self._session,
                    uncategorized_ids,
                    self._user.id,
                    overwrite=False,
                )
                n = len(result.assigned)
                if n:
                    _log.info(
                        "Refresh: %d transacciones auto-categorizadas", n
                    )

        except Exception as exc:
            self._session.rollback()
            _log.warning("Error al re-aplicar reglas en refresh: %s", exc)

        # Always reload the table regardless of rule results
        self.refresh()

    def showEvent(self, event) -> None:
        """
        Called automatically by Qt every time this widget becomes visible
        — including when the user clicks 'Transacciones' in the sidebar.
        Re-applies categorization rules silently and reloads the table.
        """
        super().showEvent(event)
        self._refresh_with_rules()

    def refresh(self) -> None:
        self._page = 0
        self._load_categories()
        self._load_data()

    def _load_data(self) -> None:
        t = self._t
        search_text = self._inp_search.text().strip().lower()
        type_filter = self._cmb_type.currentData()
        cat_filter  = self._cmb_cat.currentData()

        query = (
            self._session.query(Transaction)
            .filter(Transaction.user_id == self._user.id)
        )

        if type_filter is not None:
            query = query.filter(Transaction.type == type_filter)

        if cat_filter == -1:
            query = query.filter(
                Transaction.category_id == None  # noqa: E711
            )
        elif cat_filter is not None:
            query = query.filter(Transaction.category_id == cat_filter)

        if search_text:
            query = query.filter(
                or_(
                    Transaction.issuer_name.ilike(f"%{search_text}%"),
                    Transaction.concept.ilike(f"%{search_text}%"),
                )
            )

        query = query.order_by(
            Transaction.transaction_date.desc(),
            Transaction.id.desc()
        )

        self._total = query.count()
        offset      = self._page * PAGE_SIZE
        transactions = query.offset(offset).limit(PAGE_SIZE).all()

        self._lbl_count.setText(
            f"{self._total} transacciones"
        )
        total_pages = max(1, (self._total + PAGE_SIZE - 1) // PAGE_SIZE)
        self._lbl_page.setText(
            f"Página {self._page + 1} de {total_pages}"
        )
        self._btn_prev.setEnabled(self._page > 0)
        self._btn_next.setEnabled(
            (self._page + 1) * PAGE_SIZE < self._total
        )

        # Build category lookup
        cat_map = {c.id: c.name for c in self._categories}
        type_labels = {
            TransactionType.income:     "Ingreso",
            TransactionType.expense:    "Gasto",
            TransactionType.investment: "Inversión",
            TransactionType.unknown:    "—",
        }

        self._model.removeRows(0, self._model.rowCount())
        self._txn_ids: List[int] = []

        for txn in transactions:
            self._txn_ids.append(txn.id)

            amount  = txn.amount or 0.0
            balance = txn.running_balance or 0.0

            # Fecha
            item_date = QStandardItem(
                str(txn.transaction_date) if txn.transaction_date else ""
            )

            # Emisor
            item_issuer = QStandardItem(
                (txn.issuer_name or "")[:40]
            )

            # Concepto
            item_concept = QStandardItem(
                (txn.concept or "")[:80]
            )

            # Importe
            amt_str = self._fmt_amount(amount)
            item_amount = QStandardItem(amt_str)
            item_amount.setTextAlignment(
                Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight |
                Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter
            )
            clr = t["income"] if amount >= 0 else t["expense"]
            item_amount.setForeground(QColor(clr))

            # Tipo
            item_type = QStandardItem(
                type_labels.get(txn.type, "—")
            )

            # Categoría
            cat_name = cat_map.get(txn.category_id, "Sin categorizar")
            item_cat = QStandardItem(cat_name)
            if txn.category_id is None:
                item_cat.setForeground(QColor(t["warning"]))

            # Saldo
            item_balance = QStandardItem(
                self._fmt_amount(balance)
            )
            item_balance.setTextAlignment(
                Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight |
                Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter
            )

            row = [item_date, item_issuer, item_concept,
                   item_amount, item_type, item_cat, item_balance]
            for item in row:
                item.setEditable(False)
            self._model.appendRow(row)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Category assignment dialog (double-click on row)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _on_row_double_clicked(self, index) -> None:
        row = index.row()
        if row >= len(self._txn_ids):
            return
        txn_id = self._txn_ids[row]
        self._open_category_dialog(txn_id, row)

    def _open_category_dialog(self, txn_id: int, row: int) -> None:
        from PySide6.QtWidgets import QDialog, QDialogButtonBox

        t   = self._t
        txn = self._session.get(Transaction, txn_id)
        if txn is None:
            return

        dlg = QDialog(self)
        dlg.setWindowTitle("Asignar categoría")
        dlg.setFixedWidth(400)
        dlg.setModal(True)

        layout = QVBoxLayout(dlg)
        layout.setContentsMargins(24, 24, 24, 24)
        layout.setSpacing(14)

        # Transaction info
        lbl_info = QLabel(
            f"<b>{txn.issuer_name or txn.concept or 'Transacción'}</b><br>"
            f"<small>{txn.transaction_date} · "
            f"{self._fmt_amount(txn.amount or 0)}</small>"
        )
        lbl_info.setWordWrap(True)
        lbl_info.setStyleSheet(
            f"color: {t['text_primary']}; font-size: 13px;"
        )
        layout.addWidget(lbl_info)

        sep = QFrame()
        sep.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.HLine)
        sep.setStyleSheet(f"color: {t['border']};")
        layout.addWidget(sep)

        lbl_cat = QLabel("Categoría:")
        lbl_cat.setStyleSheet(
            f"font-size: 12px; font-weight: 600; color: {t['text_secondary']};"
        )
        layout.addWidget(lbl_cat)

        cmb = QComboBox()
        cmb.setFixedHeight(38)
        cmb.addItem("Sin categorizar", None)
        current_idx = 0
        for i, cat in enumerate(self._categories, start=1):
            cmb.addItem(cat.name, cat.id)
            if cat.id == txn.category_id:
                current_idx = i
        cmb.setCurrentIndex(current_idx)
        cmb.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QComboBox {{
                background-color: {t["bg_primary"]};
                border: 1.5px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 8px;
                padding: 6px 12px;
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 13px;
            }}
            QComboBox:focus {{ border-color: {t["accent"]}; }}
        """)
        layout.addWidget(cmb)

        btn_box = QDialogButtonBox(
            QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Ok |
            QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Cancel
        )
        btn_box.button(QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Ok).setText(
            "Guardar"
        )
        btn_box.button(QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Ok).setObjectName(
            "btnPrimary"
        )
        btn_box.accepted.connect(dlg.accept)
        btn_box.rejected.connect(dlg.reject)
        layout.addWidget(btn_box)

        dlg.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QDialog {{ background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]}; }}
            QPushButton#btnPrimary {{
                background-color: {t["accent"]}; color: #FFFFFF;
                border: none; border-radius: 6px;
                font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600;
                padding: 6px 16px;
            }}
        """)

        if dlg.exec() == QDialog.DialogCode.Accepted:
            new_cat_id = cmb.currentData()
            assign_transaction_category(
                self._session, txn_id, new_cat_id
            )
            # Update model cell directly (no full reload)
            cat_name = next(
                (c.name for c in self._categories
                 if c.id == new_cat_id),
                "Sin categorizar"
            )
            self._model.item(row, 5).setText(cat_name)
            if new_cat_id is None:
                self._model.item(row, 5).setForeground(
                    QColor(t["warning"])
                )
            else:
                self._model.item(row, 5).setForeground(
                    QColor(t["text_primary"])
                )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Filter / pagination slots
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _on_filter_changed(self) -> None:
        self._page = 0
        self._load_data()

    def _clear_filters(self) -> None:
        self._inp_search.clear()
        self._cmb_type.setCurrentIndex(0)
        self._cmb_cat.setCurrentIndex(0)
        self._page = 0
        self._load_data()

    def _prev_page(self) -> None:
        if self._page > 0:
            self._page -= 1
            self._load_data()

    def _next_page(self) -> None:
        if (self._page + 1) * PAGE_SIZE < self._total:
            self._page += 1
            self._load_data()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Helpers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @staticmethod
    def _fmt_amount(value: float) -> str:
        sign = "+" if value >= 0 else ""
        return (
            f"{sign}{abs(value):,.2f} €"
            .replace(",", "X").replace(".", ",").replace("X", ".")
        )

    def apply_theme(self, theme_key: str) -> None:
        self._theme = theme_key
        self._t = THEMES[theme_key]
        self._apply_theme()

    def _apply_theme(self) -> None:
        t = self._t
        is_dark = self._theme == "dark"
        selection_bg = (
            f"background-color: {t['accent']}; color: #FFFFFF;"
            if is_dark else
            f"background: qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:1, y2:0, "
            f"stop:0 {t['selection_grad_start']}, stop:1 {t['selection_grad_end']}); "
            f"color: {t['selection_text']};"
        )
        selection_hover = (
            f"background-color: {t['accent_hover']}; color: #FFFFFF;"
            if is_dark else
            f"background: qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:1, y2:0, "
            f"stop:0 {t['selection_hover_start']}, stop:1 {t['selection_hover_end']}); "
            f"color: {t['selection_text']};"
        )
        self.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QFrame#dashTopBar {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border-bottom: 1px solid {t["border"]};
            }}
            QLabel#dashTitle {{
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700;
            }}
            QLabel#txnCount {{
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 12px;
            }}
            QFrame#filterBar {{
                background-color: {t["bg_primary"]};
                border-bottom: 1px solid {t["border"]};
            }}
            QLineEdit#filterInput {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 6px;
                padding: 4px 10px;
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 13px;
            }}
            QLineEdit#filterInput:focus {{
                border-color: {t["accent"]};
            }}
            QComboBox#filterCombo {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 6px;
                padding: 4px 10px;
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 13px;
            }}
            QPushButton#btnSecondary {{
                background-color: transparent;
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 6px;
                font-size: 12px;
                padding: 0 12px;
            }}
            QPushButton#btnSecondary:hover {{
                background-color: {t["bg_hover"]};
            }}
            QTableView {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border: none;
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 12px;
                alternate-background-color: {t["bg_primary"]};
            }}
            QTableView::item {{
                padding: 6px 8px;
                border-bottom: 1px solid {t["border"]};
            }}
            QTableView::item:hover {{
                background-color: {t["bg_hover"]};
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
            }}
            QTableView::item:selected {{
                {selection_bg}
            }}
            QTableView::item:selected:hover {{
                {selection_hover}
            }}
            QHeaderView::section {{
                background-color: {t["bg_primary"]};
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700;
                padding: 6px 8px; border: none;
                border-bottom: 2px solid {t["border"]};
                text-transform: uppercase;
            }}
            QFrame#paginationBar {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border-top: 1px solid {t["border"]};
            }}
            QLabel#pageLabel {{
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 12px;
            }}
        """)
        apply_card_shadow(self._filter_bar, t, blur_radius=12, y_offset=2)
        apply_card_shadow(self._pagination_bar, t, blur_radius=12, y_offset=-2)
```

## `app/settings/settings_manager.py`

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import shutil
import tempfile
import zipfile
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path

from PySide6.QtCore import QSettings

from app.db.database import (
    PENDING_RESTORE_NAME,
    database_has_users,
    get_default_data_dir,
    get_user_data_dir,
    snapshot_database,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@dataclass
class AppSettings:
    theme: str = "light"
    save_appearance_on_start: bool = True
    language: str = "es"
    default_currency: str = "EUR"
    default_period: str = "Mes actual"
    auto_backup: bool = True
    backup_interval_hours: int = 24
    backup_folder: str = ""
    weekly_summary_email: bool = False
    enable_hints: bool = True
    require_password_on_start: bool = True
    protect_sensitive_data: bool = True
    import_folder: str = ""
    export_folder: str = ""
    data_folder: str = ""

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return asdict(self)


class SettingsManager:
    """Simple, stable persistence layer for application configuration."""

    ORG = "GEVernova"
    APP = "FAMILYFIN"

    def __init__(self, settings: QSettings | None = None):
        self._settings = settings or QSettings(self.ORG, self.APP)
        self._defaults = AppSettings()

    def load(self) -> AppSettings:
        values = {
            "theme": self._settings.value("settings/theme", self._defaults.theme, type=str),
            "save_appearance_on_start": self._settings.value(
                "settings/save_appearance_on_start",
                self._defaults.save_appearance_on_start,
                type=bool,
            ),
            "language": self._settings.value("settings/language", self._defaults.language, type=str),
            "default_currency": self._settings.value(
                "settings/default_currency", self._defaults.default_currency, type=str
            ),
            "default_period": self._settings.value(
                "settings/default_period", self._defaults.default_period, type=str
            ),
            "auto_backup": self._settings.value(
                "settings/auto_backup", self._defaults.auto_backup, type=bool
            ),
            "backup_interval_hours": self._settings.value(
                "settings/backup_interval_hours", self._defaults.backup_interval_hours, type=int
            ),
            "backup_folder": self._settings.value(
                "settings/backup_folder", self._defaults.backup_folder, type=str
            ),
            "weekly_summary_email": self._settings.value(
                "settings/weekly_summary_email", self._defaults.weekly_summary_email, type=bool
            ),
            "enable_hints": self._settings.value(
                "settings/enable_hints", self._defaults.enable_hints, type=bool
            ),
            "require_password_on_start": self._settings.value(
                "settings/require_password_on_start", self._defaults.require_password_on_start, type=bool
            ),
            "protect_sensitive_data": self._settings.value(
                "settings/protect_sensitive_data", self._defaults.protect_sensitive_data, type=bool
            ),
            "import_folder": self._settings.value(
                "settings/import_folder", self._defaults.import_folder, type=str
            ),
            "export_folder": self._settings.value(
                "settings/export_folder", self._defaults.export_folder, type=str
            ),
            "data_folder": self._settings.value(
                "settings/data_folder", str(get_user_data_dir()), type=str
            ),
        }
        return AppSettings(**values)

    def save(self, settings: AppSettings) -> None:
        self._settings.setValue("settings/theme", settings.theme)
        self._settings.setValue("settings/save_appearance_on_start", settings.save_appearance_on_start)
        self._settings.setValue("settings/language", settings.language)
        self._settings.setValue("settings/default_currency", settings.default_currency)
        self._settings.setValue("settings/default_period", settings.default_period)
        self._settings.setValue("settings/auto_backup", settings.auto_backup)
        self._settings.setValue("settings/backup_interval_hours", settings.backup_interval_hours)
        self._settings.setValue("settings/backup_folder", settings.backup_folder)
        self._settings.setValue("settings/weekly_summary_email", settings.weekly_summary_email)
        self._settings.setValue("settings/enable_hints", settings.enable_hints)
        self._settings.setValue("settings/require_password_on_start", settings.require_password_on_start)
        self._settings.setValue("settings/protect_sensitive_data", settings.protect_sensitive_data)
        self._settings.setValue("settings/import_folder", settings.import_folder)
        self._settings.setValue("settings/export_folder", settings.export_folder)
        self._settings.setValue("settings/data_folder", settings.data_folder or str(get_default_data_dir()))
        self._settings.sync()

    def ensure_dirs(self, settings: AppSettings) -> None:
        for folder_key in ("import_folder", "export_folder", "backup_folder", "data_folder"):
            folder = getattr(settings, folder_key)
            if folder:
                Path(folder).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    def apply_data_folder(self, folder: str, current_dir: Path | None = None) -> Path:
        """Point the app at a new data directory, copying the current database there.

        A target folder that already holds a database with users is used as
        it is and never overwritten (it may be another copy of the family's
        data). A user-less database there is kept aside as
        profin.replaced.<timestamp>.db before the current one is copied in.
        The copy is a consistent SQLite snapshot (includes pending WAL).
        current_dir is the folder in use now; pass it when the setting may
        already have been overwritten (e.g. after save()).
        The caller must re-initialise the database (init_db) before using it.
        """
        target_dir = Path(folder).expanduser().resolve() if folder else get_default_data_dir()
        target_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        current_dir = current_dir or get_user_data_dir()
        if current_dir.resolve() != target_dir.resolve():
            source_db = current_dir / "profin.db"
            target_db = target_dir / "profin.db"
            if database_has_users(target_db):
                logger.info(
                    "La carpeta %s ya contiene una base con usuarios; se usa sin sobrescribirla.",
                    target_dir,
                )
            elif source_db.exists():
                if target_db.exists():
                    timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")
                    target_db.replace(target_dir / f"profin.replaced.{timestamp}.db")
                    for suffix in ("-wal", "-shm"):
                        (target_dir / f"profin.db{suffix}").unlink(missing_ok=True)
                snapshot_database(source_db, target_db)
                logger.info("Base de datos copiada a %s", target_db)

        self._settings.setValue("settings/data_folder", str(target_dir))
        self._settings.sync()
        return target_dir

    def backup_all_data(
        self,
        backup_dir: str,
        user_name: str | None = None,
        prefix: str = "profin_backup",
    ) -> Path:
        if not backup_dir:
            raise ValueError("Debe seleccionar una carpeta de respaldo.")

        backup_root = Path(backup_dir)
        backup_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        db_path = get_user_data_dir() / "profin.db"

        timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")
        safe_user = (user_name or "usuario").replace(" ", "_").lower()
        backup_path = backup_root / f"{prefix}_{safe_user}_{timestamp}.zip"

        manifest = {
            "created_at": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
            "app": "FAMILYFIN",
            "user": safe_user,
            "database_files": ["profin.db"],
            "settings_keys": sorted(self._settings.allKeys()),
        }

        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            # Consistent single-file snapshot (includes pending WAL content),
            # instead of zipping profin.db/-wal/-shm while they may change.
            snapshot_path = Path(tmp) / "profin.db"
            snapshot_database(db_path, snapshot_path)

            with zipfile.ZipFile(backup_path, "w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as archive:
                archive.writestr("metadata.json", json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
                snapshot = {key: self._settings.value(key) for key in self._settings.allKeys()}
                archive.writestr("settings.json", json.dumps(snapshot, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
                archive.write(snapshot_path, arcname="data/profin.db")

        return backup_path

    # -- Automatic backups ---------------------------------------------

    AUTO_BACKUP_PREFIX = "profin_autobackup"
    AUTO_BACKUP_KEEP = 10
    KEY_LAST_AUTO_BACKUP = "backup/last_auto_backup"

    def auto_backup_dir(self, settings: AppSettings) -> Path:
        """Configured backup folder, or a backups/ folder next to the data."""
        if settings.backup_folder:
            return Path(settings.backup_folder).expanduser()
        return get_user_data_dir().parent / "backups"

    def run_auto_backup_if_due(
        self,
        user_name: str | None = None,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> Path | None:
        """Create an automatic backup when enabled and the interval has passed.

        Returns the new backup path, or None when no backup was due. Only the
        oldest *automatic* backups are pruned; manual ones are never deleted.
        """
        settings = self.load()
        if not settings.auto_backup:
            return None

        now = now or datetime.now()
        last_raw = self._settings.value(self.KEY_LAST_AUTO_BACKUP, "", type=str)
        if last_raw:
            try:
                last = datetime.fromisoformat(last_raw)
            except ValueError:
                last = None
            if last is not None and now - last < timedelta(hours=settings.backup_interval_hours):
                return None

        backup_dir = self.auto_backup_dir(settings)
        backup_path = self.backup_all_data(
            str(backup_dir), user_name=user_name, prefix=self.AUTO_BACKUP_PREFIX
        )
        self._settings.setValue(self.KEY_LAST_AUTO_BACKUP, now.isoformat(timespec="seconds"))
        self._settings.sync()

        # Each backup holds the whole family database, whoever was logged
        # in, so keep the newest N overall (by time, not by name).
        old_backups = sorted(
            backup_dir.glob(f"{self.AUTO_BACKUP_PREFIX}_*.zip"),
            key=lambda p: p.stat().st_mtime,
        )
        for stale in old_backups[: -self.AUTO_BACKUP_KEEP]:
            stale.unlink(missing_ok=True)

        logger.info("Copia de seguridad automática creada: %s", backup_path)
        return backup_path

    def restore_all_data(self, backup_path: str) -> bool:
        """Validate a backup and stage it; it is applied on the next app start.

        The live profin.db is never overwritten while the app has it open.
        Settings are restored immediately, except the data folder, which
        stays pointing at this machine's current location.
        """
        archive_path = Path(backup_path)
        if not archive_path.exists():
            return False

        db_dir = get_user_data_dir()

        with zipfile.ZipFile(archive_path, "r") as archive:
            names = archive.namelist()
            if "metadata.json" not in names or "data/profin.db" not in names:
                return False

            pending = db_dir / PENDING_RESTORE_NAME
            with archive.open("data/profin.db") as src, open(pending, "wb") as dst:
                shutil.copyfileobj(src, dst)

            if "settings.json" in names:
                settings_payload = json.loads(archive.read("settings.json").decode("utf-8"))
                for key in list(self._settings.allKeys()):
                    self._settings.remove(key)
                for key, value in settings_payload.items():
                    self._settings.setValue(key, value)
                self._settings.setValue("settings/data_folder", str(db_dir))
                self._settings.sync()

        return True
```
