## `tests/test_auth.py`

```python
"""
Unit tests for authentication service (Phase 1).

Tests:
  - create_user: happy path, duplicate username, empty inputs
  - verify_login: correct credentials, wrong password, unknown user
  - last_login updated on successful login
"""

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from app.db.models import Base
from app.auth.auth_service import create_user, verify_login
from app.db.database import ensure_demo_users


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Fixtures — in-memory SQLite DB per test
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@pytest.fixture
def db_session():
    """Provide a fresh in-memory SQLite session for each test."""
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:", connect_args={"check_same_thread": False})
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    Session = sessionmaker(bind=engine)
    session = Session()
    yield session
    session.close()
    engine.dispose()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# create_user tests
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestCreateUser:
    def test_create_user_happy_path(self, db_session):
        user = create_user(db_session, "pedro", "secret123")
        assert user.id is not None
        assert user.username == "pedro"
        assert user.password_hash != "secret123"  # never store plaintext
        assert user.password_hash.startswith("$2b$")  # bcrypt prefix

    def test_create_user_duplicate_raises(self, db_session):
        create_user(db_session, "pedro", "secret123")
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="ya existe"):
            create_user(db_session, "pedro", "otherpassword")

    def test_create_user_empty_username_raises(self, db_session):
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="vacío"):
            create_user(db_session, "   ", "secret123")

    def test_create_user_empty_password_raises(self, db_session):
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="vacía"):
            create_user(db_session, "pedro", "")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# verify_login tests
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestVerifyLogin:
    def test_login_correct_credentials(self, db_session):
        create_user(db_session, "maria", "mypassword")
        user = verify_login(db_session, "maria", "mypassword")
        assert user is not None
        assert user.username == "maria"

    def test_login_wrong_password_returns_none(self, db_session):
        create_user(db_session, "maria", "mypassword")
        result = verify_login(db_session, "maria", "wrongpassword")
        assert result is None

    def test_login_unknown_user_returns_none(self, db_session):
        result = verify_login(db_session, "fantasma", "anything")
        assert result is None

    def test_login_updates_last_login(self, db_session):
        create_user(db_session, "comun", "pass123")
        user = verify_login(db_session, "comun", "pass123")
        assert user.last_login is not None


def test_ensure_demo_users_does_not_create_automatic_credentials(db_session):
    created = ensure_demo_users(db_session)

    assert created == []
    assert verify_login(db_session, "usuario", "123456") is None
    assert verify_login(db_session, "admin", "123456") is None
```

## `tests/test_duplicate_detector.py`

```python
"""
tests/test_duplicate_detector.py
==================================
Unit tests for duplicate detection logic (Phase 2).

Covers:
  - D-1: exact 4-field match → auto_skip
  - D-1 intra-batch: exact duplicate within the same file → auto_skip
  - D-2: same date+concepto+amount, different balance → clean (not flagged)
  - D-3: exact match on a zero-amount row (DB or same file) → needs_review
  - Completely clean batch → all rows in clean
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from datetime import date

import pandas as pd
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from app.db.models import Base, Transaction, TransactionType, Account, User
from app.auth.auth_service import create_user
from app.ingestion.duplicate_detector import check_duplicates


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Fixtures
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@pytest.fixture
def db_session():
    """In-memory SQLite session with schema created."""
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite:///:memory:",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    Session = sessionmaker(bind=engine)
    sess = Session()
    yield sess
    sess.close()
    engine.dispose()


@pytest.fixture
def user_and_account(db_session):
    """Create a user + account and return (user_id, account_id)."""
    user = create_user(db_session, "pedro_test", "password123")
    account = Account(
        user_id=user.id,
        account_name="CaixaBank Principal",
        iban_last4="7363",
    )
    db_session.add(account)
    db_session.commit()
    return user.id, account.id


def _make_df(rows: list[dict]) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Build a minimal DataFrame for duplicate detection tests."""
    defaults = {
        "transaction_date": date(2025, 6, 1),
        "value_date":       date(2025, 6, 1),
        "amount":           -50.0,
        "running_balance":  1000.0,
        "concepto_comun":   "044",
        "concepto_propio":  "000",
        "issuer_name":      "MERCADONA",
        "concept":          "MERCADONA | TARJETA CREDITO",
        "raw_col14":        "MERCADONA",
        "currency":         "EUR",
        "iban_raw":         "2100 0732 21 0102117363",
    }
    return pd.DataFrame([{**defaults, **r} for r in rows])


def _add_existing_txn(
    session, user_id, account_id,
    txn_date, concepto_comun, amount, running_balance
) -> None:
    """Insert one existing Transaction into DB for testing."""
    txn = Transaction(
        user_id          = user_id,
        account_id       = account_id,
        transaction_date = txn_date,
        value_date       = txn_date,
        amount           = amount,
        running_balance  = running_balance,
        type             = TransactionType.expense,
        concepto_comun   = concepto_comun,
        source_file      = "test_existing.xlsx",
    )
    session.add(txn)
    session.commit()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tests
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestDuplicateDetector:

    def test_clean_batch_no_existing(self, db_session, user_and_account):
        """All rows are new — should all land in clean."""
        user_id, account_id = user_and_account
        df = _make_df([
            {"amount": -50.0,  "running_balance": 950.0},
            {"amount": -100.0, "running_balance": 850.0,
             "transaction_date": date(2025, 6, 2)},
        ])
        result = check_duplicates(df, db_session, user_id, account_id)
        assert result.auto_skip == []
        assert result.needs_review == []
        assert len(result.clean) == 2

    def test_d1_exact_match_db(self, db_session, user_and_account):
        """D-1: row matches existing DB record on all 4 fields → auto_skip."""
        user_id, account_id = user_and_account
        _add_existing_txn(
            db_session, user_id, account_id,
            txn_date        = date(2025, 6, 1),
            concepto_comun  = "044",
            amount          = -50.0,
            running_balance = 950.0,
        )
        df = _make_df([{"amount": -50.0, "running_balance": 950.0}])
        result = check_duplicates(df, db_session, user_id, account_id)
        assert 0 in result.auto_skip
        assert result.clean == []
        assert "Duplicado exacto en BD" in result.skip_reasons[0]

    def test_d1_intra_batch_duplicate(self, db_session, user_and_account):
        """D-1 intra-batch: two identical rows in same file → second auto_skip."""
        user_id, account_id = user_and_account
        df = _make_df([
            {"amount": -50.0, "running_balance": 950.0},
            {"amount": -50.0, "running_balance": 950.0},  # exact copy
        ])
        result = check_duplicates(df, db_session, user_id, account_id)
        assert 0 in result.clean    # first occurrence: clean
        assert 1 in result.auto_skip  # second: intra-batch duplicate
        assert "dentro del fichero" in result.skip_reasons[1]

    def test_d2_same_fields_different_balance_not_flagged(
        self, db_session, user_and_account
    ):
        """
        D-2: same date+concepto+amount but different running_balance
        vs DB → NOT a duplicate, import as clean.
        """
        user_id, account_id = user_and_account
        _add_existing_txn(
            db_session, user_id, account_id,
            txn_date        = date(2025, 6, 1),
            concepto_comun  = "044",
            amount          = -50.0,
            running_balance = 950.0,  # different from incoming
        )
        df = _make_df([{"amount": -50.0, "running_balance": 1200.0}])
        result = check_duplicates(df, db_session, user_id, account_id)
        assert 0 in result.clean
        assert result.auto_skip == []
        assert result.needs_review == []

    def test_d2_intra_batch_partial_diff_balance_is_clean(
        self, db_session, user_and_account
    ):
        """
        D-2: same date+concepto+amount but different running_balance in the
        same batch remains a valid import, not a false duplicate flag.
        """
        user_id, account_id = user_and_account
        df = _make_df([
            {"amount": -50.0, "running_balance": 950.0},
            {"amount": -50.0, "running_balance": 900.0},  # same partial, diff balance
        ])
        result = check_duplicates(df, db_session, user_id, account_id)
        assert 0 in result.clean
        assert 1 in result.clean
        assert result.auto_skip == []
        assert result.needs_review == []

    def test_mixed_batch(self, db_session, user_and_account):
        """Mixed batch: clean + auto_skip + normal repeated partial rows in one call."""
        user_id, account_id = user_and_account
        # Pre-seed one exact match
        _add_existing_txn(
            db_session, user_id, account_id,
            txn_date        = date(2025, 6, 1),
            concepto_comun  = "044",
            amount          = -50.0,
            running_balance = 950.0,
        )
        df = _make_df([
            # row 0: clean (different date)
            {"transaction_date": date(2025, 7, 1),
             "amount": -75.0, "running_balance": 800.0},
            # row 1: D-1 exact match DB → auto_skip
            {"amount": -50.0, "running_balance": 950.0},
            # row 2: same partial as row 0 (same date+conc+amount), diff balance
            {"transaction_date": date(2025, 7, 1),
             "amount": -75.0, "running_balance": 750.0},
        ])
        result = check_duplicates(df, db_session, user_id, account_id)
        assert 0 in result.clean
        assert 1 in result.auto_skip
        assert 2 in result.clean
        assert result.needs_review == []
    def test_d3_zero_amount_exact_match_in_db_needs_review(
        self, db_session, user_and_account
    ):
        user_id, account_id = user_and_account
        _add_existing_txn(
            db_session, user_id, account_id,
            txn_date        = date(2025, 6, 1),
            concepto_comun  = "044",
            amount          = 0.0,
            running_balance = 950.0,
        )
        df = _make_df([{"amount": 0.0, "running_balance": 950.0}])
        result = check_duplicates(df, db_session, user_id, account_id)
        assert result.needs_review == [0]
        assert result.auto_skip == []
        assert "0,00" in result.review_reasons[0]

    def test_d3_zero_amount_repeated_in_file_needs_review(
        self, db_session, user_and_account
    ):
        user_id, account_id = user_and_account
        df = _make_df([
            {"amount": 0.0, "running_balance": 950.0},
            {"amount": 0.0, "running_balance": 950.0},
        ])
        result = check_duplicates(df, db_session, user_id, account_id)
        assert result.clean == [0]
        assert result.needs_review == [1]

```

## `tests/test_validator.py`

```python
"""
tests/test_validator.py
========================
Unit tests for import validation (Phase 2).

Covers all V-01 to V-08 rules.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from datetime import date

import pandas as pd

from app.ingestion.validator import (
    ValidationError,
    ValidationWarning,
    validate_dataframe,
)


def _base_df(n: int = 3) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Return a minimal valid DataFrame for validation tests."""
    return pd.DataFrame({
        "transaction_date": [date(2025, 1, i + 1) for i in range(n)],
        "value_date":       [date(2025, 1, i + 1) for i in range(n)],
        "amount":           [-50.0 * (i + 1) for i in range(n)],
        "running_balance":  [1000.0 - 50.0 * (i + 1) for i in range(n)],
        "concepto_comun":   ["044"] * n,
        "concepto_propio":  ["000"] * n,
        "issuer_name":      ["MERCADONA"] * n,
        "concept":          ["MERCADONA | TCR"] * n,
        "raw_col14":        ["MERCADONA"] * n,
        "currency":         ["EUR"] * n,
        "iban_raw":         ["2100 0732 21 0102117363"] * n,
    })


class TestValidateDataframe:

    def test_valid_df_passes(self):
        result = validate_dataframe(_base_df())
        assert result.row_count == 3
        assert result.iban == "2100 0732 21 0102117363"
        assert result.warnings == []

    def test_v01_empty_df(self):
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="no contiene filas"):
            validate_dataframe(pd.DataFrame())

    def test_v02_missing_column(self):
        df = _base_df()
        df = df.drop(columns=["amount"])
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="'amount'"):
            validate_dataframe(df)

    def test_v03_null_date(self):
        df = _base_df()
        df.loc[1, "transaction_date"] = None
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="Fecha operación"):
            validate_dataframe(df)

    def test_v04_null_amount(self):
        df = _base_df()
        df.loc[0, "amount"] = None
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="Importe"):
            validate_dataframe(df)

    def test_v05_null_balance(self):
        df = _base_df()
        df.loc[2, "running_balance"] = None
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="Saldo"):
            validate_dataframe(df)

    def test_v06_non_eur_currency(self):
        df = _base_df()
        df.loc[0, "currency"] = "USD"
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="EUR"):
            validate_dataframe(df)

    def test_v07_multiple_ibans(self):
        df = _base_df()
        df.loc[0, "iban_raw"] = "2100 0001 00 0000000000"
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="IBANs distintos"):
            validate_dataframe(df)

    def test_v07_no_iban(self):
        df = _base_df()
        df["iban_raw"] = None
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="IBAN"):
            validate_dataframe(df)

    def test_v08_zero_amount_warning(self):
        df = _base_df()
        df.loc[1, "amount"] = 0.0
        result = validate_dataframe(df)
        assert len(result.warnings) == 1
        assert result.warnings[0].rule == "V-08"
        assert 1 in result.warnings[0].row_indices
```

## `tests/test_rules_engine.py`

```python
"""
tests/test_rules_engine.py
===========================
Unit tests for the auto-categorization rules engine (Phase 3).

Covers:
  - R-01: No rules defined → all transactions unmatched
  - R-02: Single contains rule → matched via issuer_name
  - R-03: Single contains rule → matched via concept (OR gate)
  - R-04: Exact match rule
  - R-05: Regex match rule
  - R-06: Priority — lower number wins over higher number
  - R-07: Conflict — two rules same priority, different categories
  - R-08: Global rule applies when user_id=None
  - R-09: apply_rules_and_commit persists assignments
  - R-10: No match → unmatched list
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from datetime import date

from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from app.db.models import (
    Base, Transaction, TransactionType,
    Account, Category, CategoryLevel, AutoCategorizationRule, MatchType,
)
from app.auth.auth_service import create_user
from app.categorization.rules_engine import (
    apply_rules,
    apply_rules_and_commit,
    create_rule,
    RulesEngineResult,
)
from app.categorization.category_manager import seed_default_categories


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Fixtures
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@pytest.fixture
def db_session():
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite:///:memory:",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    Session = sessionmaker(bind=engine)
    sess = Session()
    yield sess
    sess.close()
    engine.dispose()


@pytest.fixture
def base_data(db_session):
    """Create user, account, and two categories (GASTO + INGRESO type nodes)."""
    user = create_user(db_session, "pedro_test", "password123")
    account = Account(
        user_id      = user.id,
        account_name = "CaixaBank Principal",
        iban_last4   = "7363",
    )
    db_session.add(account)
    db_session.flush()

    # Type nodes
    type_gasto = Category(
        user_id          = user.id,
        name             = "GASTO",
        level            = CategoryLevel.type,
        transaction_type = TransactionType.expense,
    )
    type_ingreso = Category(
        user_id          = user.id,
        name             = "INGRESO",
        level            = CategoryLevel.type,
        transaction_type = TransactionType.income,
    )
    db_session.add_all([type_gasto, type_ingreso])
    db_session.flush()

    # Category nodes
    cat_super = Category(
        user_id   = user.id,
        parent_id = type_gasto.id,
        name      = "Supermercados",
        level     = CategoryLevel.category,
    )
    cat_nomina = Category(
        user_id   = user.id,
        parent_id = type_ingreso.id,
        name      = "Nomina Pedro",
        level     = CategoryLevel.category,
    )
    db_session.add_all([cat_super, cat_nomina])
    db_session.commit()

    return {
        "user_id":    user.id,
        "account_id": account.id,
        "cat_super":  cat_super,
        "cat_nomina": cat_nomina,
    }


def _make_txn(db_session, user_id, account_id, issuer=None, concept=None,
              amount=-50.0) -> Transaction:
    """Helper: create and persist a minimal Transaction."""
    txn = Transaction(
        user_id          = user_id,
        account_id       = account_id,
        transaction_date = date(2025, 6, 1),
        amount           = amount,
        type             = TransactionType.expense if amount < 0 else TransactionType.income,
        issuer_name      = issuer,
        concept          = concept,
        source_file      = "test.xlsx",
    )
    db_session.add(txn)
    db_session.commit()
    return txn


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tests
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestRulesEngine:

    def test_r01_no_rules_all_unmatched(self, db_session, base_data):
        """R-01: No rules defined → all transactions land in unmatched."""
        txn = _make_txn(db_session, base_data["user_id"],
                        base_data["account_id"], issuer="MERCADONA")
        result = apply_rules(db_session, [txn.id], base_data["user_id"])
        assert txn.id in result.unmatched
        assert result.assigned == {}
        assert result.conflicts == {}

    def test_r02_contains_match_issuer(self, db_session, base_data):
        """R-02: contains rule matches via issuer_name."""
        create_rule(
            db_session,
            category_id   = base_data["cat_super"].id,
            match_pattern = "MERCADONA",
            match_type    = MatchType.contains,
        )
        txn = _make_txn(db_session, base_data["user_id"],
                        base_data["account_id"], issuer="MERCADONA SA")
        result = apply_rules(db_session, [txn.id], base_data["user_id"])
        assert result.assigned[txn.id] == base_data["cat_super"].id
        rule = db_session.query(AutoCategorizationRule).one()
        assert result.assigned_by_rule == {rule.id: 1}
        assert result.transaction_ids_by_rule == {rule.id: [txn.id]}
        assert txn.id not in result.unmatched

    def test_r03_contains_match_concept_or_gate(self, db_session, base_data):
        """R-03: contains rule matches via concept when issuer_name is None."""
        create_rule(
            db_session,
            category_id   = base_data["cat_super"].id,
            match_pattern = "supermercado",
            match_type    = MatchType.contains,
        )
        txn = _make_txn(db_session, base_data["user_id"],
                        base_data["account_id"],
                        issuer=None,
                        concept="COMPRA SUPERMERCADO ONLINE")
        result = apply_rules(db_session, [txn.id], base_data["user_id"])
        assert result.assigned[txn.id] == base_data["cat_super"].id

    def test_r04_exact_match(self, db_session, base_data):
        """R-04: exact match rule — only full-string match triggers."""
        create_rule(
            db_session,
            category_id   = base_data["cat_nomina"].id,
            match_pattern = "NOMINA PEDRO",
            match_type    = MatchType.exact,
        )
        txn_match = _make_txn(db_session, base_data["user_id"],
                              base_data["account_id"],
                              issuer="NOMINA PEDRO", amount=2000.0)
        txn_no_match = _make_txn(db_session, base_data["user_id"],
                                 base_data["account_id"],
                                 issuer="NOMINA PEDRO SA", amount=2000.0)
        result = apply_rules(
            db_session,
            [txn_match.id, txn_no_match.id],
            base_data["user_id"]
        )
        assert result.assigned[txn_match.id] == base_data["cat_nomina"].id
        assert txn_no_match.id in result.unmatched

    def test_r05_regex_match(self, db_session, base_data):
        """R-05: regex rule matches using re.search."""
        create_rule(
            db_session,
            category_id   = base_data["cat_nomina"].id,
            match_pattern = r"N[OÓ]MINA\s+PEDRO",
            match_type    = MatchType.regex,
        )
        txn = _make_txn(db_session, base_data["user_id"],
                        base_data["account_id"],
                        issuer="NÓMINA PEDRO 2025", amount=2000.0)
        result = apply_rules(db_session, [txn.id], base_data["user_id"])
        assert result.assigned[txn.id] == base_data["cat_nomina"].id

    def test_r06_priority_lower_wins(self, db_session, base_data):
        """R-06: rule with lower priority number wins over higher number."""
        # Higher priority (lower number) → Supermercados
        create_rule(
            db_session,
            category_id   = base_data["cat_super"].id,
            match_pattern = "MERCADONA",
            match_type    = MatchType.contains,
            priority      = 10,
        )
        # Lower priority (higher number) → Nomina (should NOT win)
        create_rule(
            db_session,
            category_id   = base_data["cat_nomina"].id,
            match_pattern = "MERCADONA",
            match_type    = MatchType.contains,
            priority      = 50,
        )
        txn = _make_txn(db_session, base_data["user_id"],
                        base_data["account_id"], issuer="MERCADONA")
        result = apply_rules(db_session, [txn.id], base_data["user_id"])
        assert result.assigned[txn.id] == base_data["cat_super"].id
        rules = db_session.query(AutoCategorizationRule).order_by(
            AutoCategorizationRule.priority
        ).all()
        assert result.assigned_by_rule == {rules[0].id: 1}

    def test_r07_conflict_same_priority_different_categories(
        self, db_session, base_data
    ):
        """R-07: two rules at same priority, different categories → conflict."""
        create_rule(
            db_session,
            category_id   = base_data["cat_super"].id,
            match_pattern = "AMAZON",
            match_type    = MatchType.contains,
            priority      = 100,
        )
        create_rule(
            db_session,
            category_id   = base_data["cat_nomina"].id,
            match_pattern = "AMAZON",
            match_type    = MatchType.contains,
            priority      = 100,
        )
        txn = _make_txn(db_session, base_data["user_id"],
                        base_data["account_id"], issuer="AMAZON EU")
        result = apply_rules(db_session, [txn.id], base_data["user_id"])
        assert txn.id in result.conflicts
        assert txn.id not in result.assigned

    def test_r08_global_rule_user_id_none(self, db_session, base_data):
        """R-08: global rule (user_id=None) applies to any user."""
        create_rule(
            db_session,
            category_id   = base_data["cat_super"].id,
            match_pattern = "LIDL",
            match_type    = MatchType.contains,
            user_id       = None,  # global
        )
        txn = _make_txn(db_session, base_data["user_id"],
                        base_data["account_id"], issuer="LIDL ESPAÑA")
        result = apply_rules(db_session, [txn.id], base_data["user_id"])
        assert result.assigned[txn.id] == base_data["cat_super"].id

    def test_r09_apply_and_commit_persists(self, db_session, base_data):
        """R-09: apply_rules_and_commit writes category_id to DB."""
        create_rule(
            db_session,
            category_id   = base_data["cat_super"].id,
            match_pattern = "CARREFOUR",
            match_type    = MatchType.contains,
        )
        txn = _make_txn(db_session, base_data["user_id"],
                        base_data["account_id"], issuer="CARREFOUR")
        assert txn.category_id is None  # before

        apply_rules_and_commit(db_session, [txn.id], base_data["user_id"])

        db_session.refresh(txn)
        assert txn.category_id == base_data["cat_super"].id  # after

    def test_r10_unmatched_no_category_assigned(self, db_session, base_data):
        """R-10: unmatched transaction keeps category_id = None."""
        create_rule(
            db_session,
            category_id   = base_data["cat_super"].id,
            match_pattern = "MERCADONA",
            match_type    = MatchType.contains,
        )
        txn = _make_txn(db_session, base_data["user_id"],
                        base_data["account_id"], issuer="REPSOL")
        result = apply_rules(db_session, [txn.id], base_data["user_id"])
        assert txn.id in result.unmatched
        assert txn.category_id is None

    def test_r11_no_overwrite_preserves_manual_category(self, db_session, base_data):
        """R-11: apply_rules_and_commit(overwrite=False) must not touch a
        transaction that a user already categorized manually, even if a
        rule matches it — this is the passive-refresh/tab-visit path used
        by TransactionsView, which must never silently revert a manual fix."""
        create_rule(
            db_session,
            category_id   = base_data["cat_super"].id,
            match_pattern = "MERCADONA",
            match_type    = MatchType.contains,
        )
        txn = _make_txn(db_session, base_data["user_id"],
                        base_data["account_id"], issuer="MERCADONA")
        # User manually assigns a different category than the rule would.
        txn.category_id = base_data["cat_nomina"].id
        db_session.commit()

        apply_rules_and_commit(
            db_session, [txn.id], base_data["user_id"], overwrite=False
        )

        db_session.refresh(txn)
        assert txn.category_id == base_data["cat_nomina"].id

    def test_r12_apply_and_commit_persists_multiple_assignments(
        self, db_session, base_data
    ):
        """R-12: apply_rules_and_commit's write-back loop must persist
        category_id for every assigned transaction, not just a single one."""
        create_rule(
            db_session,
            category_id   = base_data["cat_super"].id,
            match_pattern = "MERCADONA",
            match_type    = MatchType.contains,
        )
        txns = [
            _make_txn(db_session, base_data["user_id"],
                      base_data["account_id"], issuer="MERCADONA")
            for _ in range(5)
        ]
        assert all(txn.category_id is None for txn in txns)

        result = apply_rules_and_commit(
            db_session, [t.id for t in txns], base_data["user_id"]
        )

        assert len(result.assigned) == 5
        for txn in txns:
            db_session.refresh(txn)
            assert txn.category_id == base_data["cat_super"].id
    def test_r13_overwrite_never_touches_manual_category(self, db_session, base_data):
        """R-13: the explicit "re-apply all rules" action (overwrite=True)
        must still keep a category a family member chose by hand."""
        from app.categorization.category_manager import assign_transaction_category
        from app.db.models import CATEGORY_SOURCE_MANUAL

        create_rule(
            db_session,
            category_id   = base_data["cat_super"].id,
            match_pattern = "MERCADONA",
            match_type    = MatchType.contains,
        )
        txn = _make_txn(db_session, base_data["user_id"],
                        base_data["account_id"], issuer="MERCADONA")
        assign_transaction_category(db_session, txn.id, base_data["cat_nomina"].id)

        apply_rules_and_commit(
            db_session, [txn.id], base_data["user_id"], overwrite=True
        )

        db_session.refresh(txn)
        assert txn.category_id == base_data["cat_nomina"].id
        assert txn.category_source == CATEGORY_SOURCE_MANUAL

    def test_r14_overwrite_updates_rule_assigned_category(self, db_session, base_data):
        """R-14: overwrite=True still re-categorizes rows a rule set earlier,
        and marks every rule assignment with category_source='rule'."""
        from app.db.models import CATEGORY_SOURCE_RULE

        rule = create_rule(
            db_session,
            category_id   = base_data["cat_nomina"].id,
            match_pattern = "MERCADONA",
            match_type    = MatchType.contains,
        )
        txn = _make_txn(db_session, base_data["user_id"],
                        base_data["account_id"], issuer="MERCADONA")
        apply_rules_and_commit(db_session, [txn.id], base_data["user_id"])
        db_session.refresh(txn)
        assert txn.category_source == CATEGORY_SOURCE_RULE

        rule.category_id = base_data["cat_super"].id
        db_session.commit()
        apply_rules_and_commit(
            db_session, [txn.id], base_data["user_id"], overwrite=True
        )

        db_session.refresh(txn)
        assert txn.category_id == base_data["cat_super"].id
        assert txn.category_source == CATEGORY_SOURCE_RULE

    def test_r15_clearing_category_hands_back_to_rules(self, db_session, base_data):
        """R-15: clearing a category manually removes the manual protection."""
        from app.categorization.category_manager import assign_transaction_category

        txn = _make_txn(db_session, base_data["user_id"],
                        base_data["account_id"], issuer="MERCADONA")
        assign_transaction_category(db_session, txn.id, base_data["cat_nomina"].id)
        assign_transaction_category(db_session, txn.id, None)

        db_session.refresh(txn)
        assert txn.category_id is None
        assert txn.category_source is None


def _second_user(db_session, with_super: bool):
    """Another family member with an optional own 'GASTO > Supermercados'."""
    user = create_user(db_session, "maria_test", "password123")
    account = Account(user_id=user.id, account_name="Cuenta María")
    db_session.add(account)
    db_session.flush()
    own_super = None
    if with_super:
        gasto = Category(user_id=user.id, name="GASTO", level=CategoryLevel.type,
                         transaction_type=TransactionType.expense)
        db_session.add(gasto)
        db_session.flush()
        own_super = Category(user_id=user.id, parent_id=gasto.id,
                             name="Supermercados", level=CategoryLevel.category)
        db_session.add(own_super)
    db_session.commit()
    return user.id, account.id, own_super


def test_r16_global_rule_maps_to_other_users_equivalent_category(db_session, base_data):
    """R-16: a global rule assigns the other user's own same-named category."""
    create_rule(db_session, category_id=base_data["cat_super"].id,
                match_pattern="LIDL", user_id=None)
    user_id, account_id, own_super = _second_user(db_session, with_super=True)
    txn = _make_txn(db_session, user_id, account_id, issuer="LIDL")

    result = apply_rules_and_commit(db_session, [txn.id], user_id)

    assert result.assigned[txn.id] == own_super.id
    db_session.refresh(txn)
    assert txn.category_id == own_super.id


def test_r17_global_rule_skipped_without_equivalent_category(db_session, base_data):
    """R-17: never assign another user's category id."""
    create_rule(db_session, category_id=base_data["cat_super"].id,
                match_pattern="LIDL", user_id=None)
    user_id, account_id, _ = _second_user(db_session, with_super=False)
    txn = _make_txn(db_session, user_id, account_id, issuer="LIDL")

    result = apply_rules(db_session, [txn.id], user_id)

    assert result.assigned == {}
    assert result.unmatched == [txn.id]


def test_r18_other_users_personal_rule_never_applies(db_session, base_data):
    """R-18: a per-user rule is not loaded for a different user."""
    create_rule(db_session, category_id=base_data["cat_super"].id,
                match_pattern="LIDL", user_id=base_data["user_id"])
    user_id, account_id, _ = _second_user(db_session, with_super=True)
    txn = _make_txn(db_session, user_id, account_id, issuer="LIDL")

    assert apply_rules(db_session, [txn.id], user_id).assigned == {}

```

## `tests/test_theme_views.py`

```python
from PySide6.QtCore import QSettings

from app.config import THEMES
from app.gui.categories_view import CategoriesView
from app.gui.rules_view import RulesView
from app.gui.transactions_view import TransactionsView
from app.settings import AppSettings, SettingsManager


def test_theme_apply_updates_view_theme_state():
    for view_cls in (TransactionsView, RulesView, CategoriesView):
        view = view_cls.__new__(view_cls)
        view._theme = "dark"
        view._t = THEMES["dark"]
        view._apply_theme = lambda: None

        view.apply_theme("light")

        assert view._theme == "light"
        assert view._t == THEMES["light"]


def test_settings_manager_persists_appearance_on_start_flag():
    qsettings = QSettings("FAMILYFIN_TEST_THEME", "FAMILYFIN")
    qsettings.clear()
    manager = SettingsManager(qsettings)

    manager.save(AppSettings(theme="dark", save_appearance_on_start=False))
    loaded = manager.load()

    assert loaded.theme == "dark"
    assert loaded.save_appearance_on_start is False
    qsettings.clear()
```
