```python
"""
app/analytics/period_summary.py
================================
FAMILYFIN — Period aggregates for the Gastos / Ingresos views.

All sums, counts and groupings run in SQLite (one small result set per
chart) instead of loading every Transaction of the period as ORM objects
and adding them up in Python. Amounts are returned as positive magnitudes
(abs), rounded to cents.

Category labels: transactions are grouped by category name, so two
categories with the same name under different types (e.g. "Hipoteca")
share one slice, as the views always showed them. Uncategorized rows, or
rows whose category no longer exists, are labelled UNCATEGORIZED_LABEL.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import date
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from sqlalchemy import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.db.models import Category, Transaction, TransactionType

UNCATEGORIZED_LABEL = "Sin categorizar"


@dataclass
class DetailRow:
    """One row of the detail table (latest transactions first)."""
    transaction_date: date
    description:      str
    category:         Optional[str]
    amount:           float


@dataclass
class PeriodSummary:
    total:       float = 0.0
    count:       int = 0
    max_amount:  float = 0.0
    categorized: int = 0
    # [(category label, total)] sorted by total desc
    by_category: List[Tuple[str, float]] = field(default_factory=list)
    # [(first day of month, [(category label, total)] top N desc)] by month asc
    by_month_category: List[Tuple[date, List[Tuple[str, float]]]] = field(
        default_factory=list
    )
    # [(day, total)] by day asc
    by_day: List[Tuple[date, float]] = field(default_factory=list)
    latest: List[DetailRow] = field(default_factory=list)

    @property
    def average(self) -> float:
        return round(self.total / self.count, 2) if self.count else 0.0

    @property
    def categorized_pct(self) -> float:
        return self.categorized / self.count * 100 if self.count else 0.0


def _period_filter(user_id: int, txn_type: TransactionType, d_from: date, d_to: date):
    return (
        Transaction.user_id == user_id,
        Transaction.type == txn_type,
        Transaction.transaction_date >= d_from,
        Transaction.transaction_date <= d_to,
    )


def period_totals(
    session:  Session,
    user_id:  int,
    txn_type: TransactionType,
    d_from:   date,
    d_to:     date,
) -> Tuple[float, int]:
    """Return (sum of |amount|, row count) for the period."""
    total, count = (
        session.query(
            func.coalesce(func.sum(func.abs(Transaction.amount)), 0.0),
            func.count(Transaction.id),
        )
        .filter(*_period_filter(user_id, txn_type, d_from, d_to))
        .one()
    )
    return round(float(total), 2), int(count)


def category_totals(
    session:  Session,
    user_id:  int,
    txn_type: TransactionType,
    d_from:   date,
    d_to:     date,
) -> List[Tuple[str, float]]:
    """[(category label, sum of |amount|)] for the period, sorted desc."""
    names = _category_names(session, user_id)
    rows = (
        session.query(
            Transaction.category_id,
            func.sum(func.abs(Transaction.amount)),
        )
        .filter(*_period_filter(user_id, txn_type, d_from, d_to))
        .group_by(Transaction.category_id)
        .all()
    )
    return _merge_by_label(rows, names)


def summarize_period(
    session:       Session,
    user_id:       int,
    txn_type:      TransactionType,
    d_from:        date,
    d_to:          date,
    top_per_month: int = 4,
    latest_limit:  int = 50,
) -> PeriodSummary:
    """Compute every figure the Gastos/Ingresos view shows for one period."""
    where = _period_filter(user_id, txn_type, d_from, d_to)
    names = _category_names(session, user_id)
    summary = PeriodSummary()

    total, count, max_amount, categorized = (
        session.query(
            func.coalesce(func.sum(func.abs(Transaction.amount)), 0.0),
            func.count(Transaction.id),
            func.coalesce(func.max(func.abs(Transaction.amount)), 0.0),
            func.count(Transaction.category_id),
        )
        .filter(*where)
        .one()
    )
    summary.total = round(float(total), 2)
    summary.count = int(count)
    summary.max_amount = round(float(max_amount), 2)
    summary.categorized = int(categorized)
    if not summary.count:
        return summary

    summary.by_category = category_totals(session, user_id, txn_type, d_from, d_to)

    month = func.strftime("%Y-%m", Transaction.transaction_date)
    month_rows = (
        session.query(
            month,
            Transaction.category_id,
            func.sum(func.abs(Transaction.amount)),
        )
        .filter(*where)
        .group_by(month, Transaction.category_id)
        .all()
    )
    rows_by_month: Dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
    for month_key, category_id, amount in month_rows:
        rows_by_month[month_key].append((category_id, amount))
    for month_key in sorted(rows_by_month):
        year, month_number = (int(part) for part in month_key.split("-"))
        top = _merge_by_label(rows_by_month[month_key], names)[:top_per_month]
        summary.by_month_category.append((date(year, month_number, 1), top))

    day_rows = (
        session.query(
            Transaction.transaction_date,
            func.sum(func.abs(Transaction.amount)),
        )
        .filter(*where)
        .group_by(Transaction.transaction_date)
        .order_by(Transaction.transaction_date)
        .all()
    )
    summary.by_day = [(day, round(float(amount), 2)) for day, amount in day_rows]

    latest_rows = (
        session.query(
            Transaction.transaction_date,
            Transaction.issuer_name,
            Transaction.concept,
            Transaction.category_id,
            Transaction.amount,
        )
        .filter(*where)
        .order_by(Transaction.transaction_date.desc(), Transaction.id.desc())
        .limit(latest_limit)
        .all()
    )
    summary.latest = [
        DetailRow(
            transaction_date=txn_date,
            description=issuer or concept or "—",
            category=names.get(category_id),
            amount=round(abs(amount), 2),
        )
        for txn_date, issuer, concept, category_id, amount in latest_rows
    ]
    return summary


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Internal helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _category_names(session: Session, user_id: int) -> Dict[int, str]:
    return dict(
        session.query(Category.id, Category.name)
        .filter(Category.user_id == user_id)
        .all()
    )


def _merge_by_label(
    rows:  List[Tuple[Optional[int], float]],
    names: Dict[int, str],
) -> List[Tuple[str, float]]:
    totals: Dict[str, float] = defaultdict(float)
    for category_id, amount in rows:
        totals[names.get(category_id, UNCATEGORIZED_LABEL)] += float(amount or 0.0)
    return sorted(
        ((label, round(value, 2)) for label, value in totals.items()),
        key=lambda item: item[1],
        reverse=True,
    )

```

## `app/gui/_style_utils.py`

```python
"""
app/gui/_style_utils.py
=========================
FAMILYFIN — Small shared GUI style helpers.

Currently provides card elevation (drop shadow) built from the theme's
``card_shadow`` token, so it can be applied consistently to every card-like
container across views and re-applied whenever the theme toggles.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import re

from PySide6.QtGui     import QColor
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QGraphicsDropShadowEffect, QWidget

_RGBA_RE = re.compile(
    r"rgba?\(\s*(\d+)\s*,\s*(\d+)\s*,\s*(\d+)\s*(?:,\s*([\d.]+)\s*)?\)"
)


def _parse_shadow_color(card_shadow: str) -> QColor:
    """Parse a 'rgba(r,g,b,a)' token string into a QColor. Falls back to a
    sensible translucent black if the token is missing/malformed."""
    match = _RGBA_RE.match(card_shadow or "")
    if not match:
        return QColor(0, 0, 0, 60)
    r, g, b = (int(match.group(i)) for i in (1, 2, 3))
    alpha = float(match.group(4)) if match.group(4) is not None else 1.0
    color = QColor(r, g, b)
    color.setAlphaF(max(0.0, min(1.0, alpha)))
    return color


def apply_card_shadow(widget: QWidget, theme: dict,
                       blur_radius: int = 20,
                       x_offset: int = 0,
                       y_offset: int = 4) -> None:
    """
    Apply (or refresh) a soft drop shadow on ``widget`` using the theme's
    ``card_shadow`` token. Safe to call repeatedly (e.g. on every theme
    toggle) — it replaces any existing effect rather than stacking them.
    """
    effect = QGraphicsDropShadowEffect(widget)
    effect.setBlurRadius(blur_radius)
    effect.setOffset(x_offset, y_offset)
    effect.setColor(_parse_shadow_color(theme.get("card_shadow", "")))
    widget.setGraphicsEffect(effect)

```

## `tests/test_backup_and_migrations.py`

```python
"""
tests/test_backup_and_migrations.py
====================================
Schema migrations (PRAGMA user_version), automatic backups, and staged restore.

All files live under tmp_path; QSettings is redirected to temp INI files by
the root conftest.py, so the real registry and real profin.db are never used.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
import zipfile
from contextlib import closing
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta

import pytest
from PySide6.QtCore import QSettings
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.db.database import (
    PENDING_RESTORE_NAME,
    SCHEMA_VERSION,
    apply_pending_restore,
    migrate_schema,
)
from app.db.models import (
    CATEGORY_SOURCE_MANUAL,
    CATEGORY_SOURCE_RULE,
    Account,
    AutoCategorizationRule,
    Base,
    Category,
    CategoryLevel,
    MatchType,
    Transaction,
    TransactionType,
    User,
)
from app.settings import SettingsManager


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _user_version(path) -> int:
    with closing(sqlite3.connect(path)) as conn:
        return conn.execute("PRAGMA user_version").fetchone()[0]


def _make_legacy_db(path) -> dict:
    """Build a pre-migration database (no category_source column, version 0)."""
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{path}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine) as s:
        user = User(username="familia", password_hash="x")
        s.add(user)
        s.flush()
        account = Account(user_id=user.id, account_name="Cuenta")
        s.add(account)
        s.flush()
        super_ = Category(user_id=user.id, name="Supermercados", level=CategoryLevel.category)
        ocio = Category(user_id=user.id, name="Ocio", level=CategoryLevel.category)
        s.add_all([super_, ocio])
        s.flush()
        s.add(AutoCategorizationRule(
            user_id=user.id, category_id=super_.id,
            match_pattern="MERCADONA", match_type=MatchType.contains,
        ))

        def txn(cat_id):
            t = Transaction(
                user_id=user.id, account_id=account.id,
                transaction_date=date(2026, 9, 1), amount=-20.0,
                type=TransactionType.expense, issuer_name="MERCADONA",
                category_id=cat_id,
            )
            s.add(t)
            return t

        by_rule, by_hand, uncategorized = txn(super_.id), txn(ocio.id), txn(None)
        s.commit()
        ids = {"rule": by_rule.id, "manual": by_hand.id, "none": uncategorized.id}
    engine.dispose()

    with closing(sqlite3.connect(path)) as conn:
        conn.execute("ALTER TABLE transactions DROP COLUMN category_source")
        conn.execute("PRAGMA user_version = 0")
        conn.commit()
    return ids


@pytest.fixture
def data_dir(tmp_path):
    """A data folder with a small real database, configured in QSettings."""
    folder = tmp_path / "data"
    folder.mkdir()
    db = folder / "profin.db"
    with closing(sqlite3.connect(db)) as conn:
        conn.execute("CREATE TABLE items (name TEXT)")
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO items VALUES ('original')")
        conn.commit()

    qsettings = QSettings("GEVernova", "FAMILYFIN")
    qsettings.clear()
    qsettings.setValue("settings/data_folder", str(folder))
    qsettings.sync()
    yield folder
    qsettings.clear()


def _read_items(db) -> list[str]:
    with closing(sqlite3.connect(db)) as conn:
        return [r[0] for r in conn.execute("SELECT name FROM items")]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Migrations
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_migration_adds_category_source_and_backfills(tmp_path):
    db = tmp_path / "profin.db"
    ids = _make_legacy_db(db)

    start = migrate_schema(db)

    assert start == 0
    assert _user_version(db) == SCHEMA_VERSION
    with closing(sqlite3.connect(db)) as conn:
        sources = dict(conn.execute("SELECT id, category_source FROM transactions"))
    # Matches what the current rules would assign → rule; differs → manual.
    assert sources[ids["rule"]] == CATEGORY_SOURCE_RULE
    assert sources[ids["manual"]] == CATEGORY_SOURCE_MANUAL
    assert sources[ids["none"]] is None
    # A safety copy is written before anything is changed.
    assert list(tmp_path.glob("profin.pre-migration-v*.db"))


def test_migration_2_adds_indexes_and_rounds_money_to_cents(tmp_path):
    db = tmp_path / "profin.db"
    ids = _make_legacy_db(db)
    with closing(sqlite3.connect(db)) as conn:
        for name in (
            "ix_transactions_user_date",
            "ix_transactions_user_category",
            "ix_transactions_user_account_date",
        ):
            conn.execute(f"DROP INDEX IF EXISTS {name}")
        conn.execute(
            "UPDATE transactions SET amount = -12.339999999, running_balance = 100.004 "
            "WHERE id = ?",
            (ids["none"],),
        )
        conn.commit()

    migrate_schema(db)

    with closing(sqlite3.connect(db)) as conn:
        indexes = {
            row[1] for row in conn.execute('PRAGMA index_list("transactions")')
        }
        amount, balance = conn.execute(
            "SELECT amount, running_balance FROM transactions WHERE id = ?",
            (ids["none"],),
        ).fetchone()
    assert {
        "ix_transactions_user_date",
        "ix_transactions_user_category",
        "ix_transactions_user_account_date",
    } <= indexes
    assert amount == -12.34
    assert balance == 100.0


def test_migration_2_resumes_from_version_1(tmp_path):
    db = tmp_path / "profin.db"
    _make_legacy_db(db)
    migrate_schema(db)
    with closing(sqlite3.connect(db)) as conn:
        conn.execute("DROP INDEX ix_transactions_user_date")
        conn.execute("PRAGMA user_version = 1")
        conn.commit()

    assert migrate_schema(db) == 1
    assert _user_version(db) == SCHEMA_VERSION
    with closing(sqlite3.connect(db)) as conn:
        indexes = {row[1] for row in conn.execute('PRAGMA index_list("transactions")')}
    assert "ix_transactions_user_date" in indexes


def test_migration_3_repairs_categories_owned_by_another_user(tmp_path):
    db = tmp_path / "profin.db"
    _make_legacy_db(db)
    migrate_schema(db)
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{db}")
    with Session(engine) as s:
        owner = s.query(User).one()
        gasto = Category(user_id=owner.id, name="GASTO", level=CategoryLevel.type)
        s.add(gasto)
        s.flush()
        owner_luz = Category(user_id=owner.id, parent_id=gasto.id, name="Luz",
                             level=CategoryLevel.category)
        owner_gym = Category(user_id=owner.id, parent_id=gasto.id, name="Gimnasio",
                             level=CategoryLevel.category)
        other = User(username="otra", password_hash="x")
        s.add_all([owner_luz, owner_gym, other])
        s.flush()
        other_gasto = Category(user_id=other.id, name="GASTO", level=CategoryLevel.type)
        s.add(other_gasto)
        s.flush()
        other_luz = Category(user_id=other.id, parent_id=other_gasto.id, name="Luz",
                             level=CategoryLevel.category)
        account = Account(user_id=other.id, account_name="Cuenta")
        s.add_all([other_luz, account])
        s.flush()

        def txn(cat):
            t = Transaction(user_id=other.id, account_id=account.id,
                            transaction_date=date(2026, 9, 1), amount=-5.0,
                            type=TransactionType.expense, category_id=cat.id,
                            category_source=CATEGORY_SOURCE_RULE)
            s.add(t)
            return t

        remap, clear = txn(owner_luz), txn(owner_gym)
        s.commit()
        remap_id, clear_id, other_luz_id = remap.id, clear.id, other_luz.id
    engine.dispose()
    with closing(sqlite3.connect(db)) as conn:
        conn.execute("PRAGMA user_version = 2")
        conn.commit()

    migrate_schema(db)

    with closing(sqlite3.connect(db)) as conn:
        rows = dict(
            (row[0], row[1:])
            for row in conn.execute("SELECT id, category_id, category_source FROM transactions")
        )
    assert rows[remap_id] == (other_luz_id, CATEGORY_SOURCE_RULE)
    assert rows[clear_id] == (None, None)


def test_migration_is_noop_when_up_to_date(tmp_path):
    db = tmp_path / "profin.db"
    _make_legacy_db(db)
    migrate_schema(db)
    for backup in tmp_path.glob("profin.pre-migration-v*.db"):
        backup.unlink()

    assert migrate_schema(db) == SCHEMA_VERSION
    assert not list(tmp_path.glob("profin.pre-migration-v*.db"))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Backups
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_backup_contains_consistent_database_snapshot(data_dir, tmp_path):
    manager = SettingsManager()
    backup = manager.backup_all_data(str(tmp_path / "backups"), user_name="Ana")

    with zipfile.ZipFile(backup) as archive:
        assert "data/profin.db" in archive.namelist()
        extracted = archive.extract("data/profin.db", tmp_path / "out")
    assert _read_items(extracted) == ["original"]


def test_auto_backup_respects_interval(data_dir, tmp_path):
    manager = SettingsManager()
    backup_dir = tmp_path / "auto"
    settings = manager.load()
    settings.backup_folder = str(backup_dir)
    settings.backup_interval_hours = 24
    manager.save(settings)
    now = datetime(2026, 9, 26, 10, 0, 0)

    assert manager.run_auto_backup_if_due(now=now) is not None
    assert manager.run_auto_backup_if_due(now=now + timedelta(hours=1)) is None
    assert manager.run_auto_backup_if_due(now=now + timedelta(hours=25)) is not None


def test_auto_backup_disabled_does_nothing(data_dir, tmp_path):
    manager = SettingsManager()
    settings = manager.load()
    settings.auto_backup = False
    settings.backup_folder = str(tmp_path / "auto")
    manager.save(settings)

    assert manager.run_auto_backup_if_due() is None
    assert not (tmp_path / "auto").exists()


def test_auto_backup_prunes_only_old_automatic_backups(data_dir, tmp_path):
    manager = SettingsManager()
    backup_dir = tmp_path / "auto"
    backup_dir.mkdir()
    manual = backup_dir / "profin_backup_ana_20260101-000000.zip"
    manual.write_bytes(b"manual")
    for i in range(SettingsManager.AUTO_BACKUP_KEEP + 3):
        old = backup_dir / f"{SettingsManager.AUTO_BACKUP_PREFIX}_ana_2026010{i:02d}.zip"
        old.write_bytes(b"old")
    settings = manager.load()
    settings.backup_folder = str(backup_dir)
    manager.save(settings)

    manager.run_auto_backup_if_due(user_name="ana")

    autos = list(backup_dir.glob(f"{SettingsManager.AUTO_BACKUP_PREFIX}_*.zip"))
    assert len(autos) == SettingsManager.AUTO_BACKUP_KEEP
    assert manual.exists()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Restore
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_restore_is_staged_then_applied_on_next_start(data_dir, tmp_path):
    manager = SettingsManager()
    backup = manager.backup_all_data(str(tmp_path / "backups"))

    db = data_dir / "profin.db"
    with closing(sqlite3.connect(db)) as conn:
        conn.execute("UPDATE items SET name = 'changed later'")
        conn.commit()

    assert manager.restore_all_data(str(backup)) is True
    # The live database is untouched until the next start.
    assert _read_items(db) == ["changed later"]
    assert (data_dir / PENDING_RESTORE_NAME).exists()
    # The data folder keeps pointing at this machine's location.
    assert QSettings("GEVernova", "FAMILYFIN").value("settings/data_folder") == str(data_dir)

    assert apply_pending_restore(data_dir) is True
    assert _read_items(db) == ["original"]
    assert not (data_dir / PENDING_RESTORE_NAME).exists()
    previous = list(data_dir.glob("profin.before-restore.*.db"))
    assert len(previous) == 1 and _read_items(previous[0]) == ["changed later"]


def test_invalid_staged_restore_is_discarded(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "profin.db").write_bytes(b"")
    pending = tmp_path / PENDING_RESTORE_NAME
    pending.write_bytes(b"not a database")

    assert apply_pending_restore(tmp_path) is False
    assert not pending.exists()


def test_restore_rejects_archive_without_database(tmp_path):
    archive_path = tmp_path / "bad.zip"
    with zipfile.ZipFile(archive_path, "w") as archive:
        archive.writestr("metadata.json", "{}")

    assert SettingsManager().restore_all_data(str(archive_path)) is False

```

## `tests/test_category_manager.py`

```python
"""
tests/test_category_manager.py
===============================
First-run seeding and category CRUD — what "Cargar categorías por defecto"
and the Categorías screen rely on.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, event
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from app.db.database import _set_sqlite_pragma

from app.auth.auth_service import create_user
from app.categorization.category_manager import (
    assign_transaction_category,
    create_category,
    delete_category,
    get_all_categories,
    seed_default_categories,
    seed_default_rules,
    update_category_name,
)
from app.db.models import (
    CATEGORY_SOURCE_MANUAL, Account, AutoCategorizationRule, Base, Category,
    CategoryLevel, Transaction, TransactionType,
)


@pytest.fixture
def session():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    # Same connection setup as the app: ON DELETE SET NULL / CASCADE need
    # PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON.
    event.listen(engine, "connect", _set_sqlite_pragma)
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sess = sessionmaker(bind=engine)()
    yield sess
    sess.close()
    engine.dispose()


@pytest.fixture
def user_id(session):
    return create_user(session, "ana", "secreto1").id


def test_seeding_is_idempotent_and_per_user(session, user_id):
    created = seed_default_categories(session, user_id)
    rules = seed_default_rules(session, user_id)
    assert created > 0 and rules > 0

    assert seed_default_categories(session, user_id) == 0
    assert seed_default_rules(session, user_id) == 0

    types = {c.name for c in get_all_categories(session, user_id, level=CategoryLevel.type)}
    assert types == {"GASTO", "INGRESO", "SERV. BANCARIO", "INVERSION"}
    other = create_user(session, "pedro", "secreto1").id
    assert get_all_categories(session, other) == []


def test_seeded_rules_point_to_the_users_own_categories(session, user_id):
    seed_default_categories(session, user_id)
    seed_default_rules(session, user_id)
    for rule in session.query(AutoCategorizationRule).all():
        assert rule.user_id == user_id
        assert rule.category.user_id == user_id
        assert rule.category.level == CategoryLevel.category


def test_duplicate_names_resolve_to_the_right_type(session, user_id):
    seed_default_categories(session, user_id)
    seed_default_rules(session, user_id)
    hbo_rule = (
        session.query(AutoCategorizationRule)
        .filter(AutoCategorizationRule.match_pattern == "HBO").one()
    )
    assert hbo_rule.category.parent.name == "GASTO"


def test_delete_category_uncategorizes_transactions_and_removes_rules(session, user_id):
    seed_default_categories(session, user_id)
    seed_default_rules(session, user_id)
    luz = next(c for c in get_all_categories(session, user_id) if c.name == "Luz")
    account = Account(user_id=user_id, account_name="Cuenta")
    session.add(account)
    session.flush()
    txn = Transaction(user_id=user_id, account_id=account.id,
                      transaction_date=date(2026, 9, 1), amount=-40.0,
                      type=TransactionType.expense, category_id=luz.id)
    session.add(txn)
    session.commit()

    delete_category(session, luz.id)

    session.refresh(txn)
    assert txn.category_id is None
    assert session.query(AutoCategorizationRule).filter_by(category_id=luz.id).count() == 0


def test_create_rename_and_validation(session, user_id):
    gasto = create_category(session, user_id, "GASTO", CategoryLevel.type,
                            transaction_type=TransactionType.expense)
    cat = create_category(session, user_id, "  Mascotas ", CategoryLevel.category, parent_id=gasto.id)
    assert cat.name == "Mascotas"
    assert update_category_name(session, cat.id, "Veterinario").name == "Veterinario"
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        create_category(session, user_id, "Sin padre", CategoryLevel.category)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        update_category_name(session, cat.id, "   ")


def test_manual_assignment_is_marked_and_clearing_resets_source(session, user_id):
    gasto = create_category(session, user_id, "GASTO", CategoryLevel.type)
    cat = create_category(session, user_id, "Varios", CategoryLevel.category, parent_id=gasto.id)
    account = Account(user_id=user_id, account_name="Cuenta")
    session.add(account)
    session.flush()
    txn = Transaction(user_id=user_id, account_id=account.id,
                      transaction_date=date(2026, 9, 1), amount=-1.0,
                      type=TransactionType.expense)
    session.add(txn)
    session.commit()

    assign_transaction_category(session, txn.id, cat.id)
    assert (txn.category_id, txn.category_source) == (cat.id, CATEGORY_SOURCE_MANUAL)
    assign_transaction_category(session, txn.id, None)
    assert (txn.category_id, txn.category_source) == (None, None)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        assign_transaction_category(session, 999_999, cat.id)

```

## `tests/test_excel_parser.py`

```python
from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd

from app.ingestion.excel_parser import parse_excel


def test_parse_excel_removes_repeated_caixabank_headers(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    header = [None] * 25
    header[4] = "F. Operación"
    header[5] = "F. Valor"

    first_transaction = [None] * 25
    first_transaction[1] = "2100 0000 00 0000000000"
    first_transaction[3] = "EUR"
    first_transaction[4] = "12/11/2021"
    first_transaction[5] = "10/11/2021"
    first_transaction[7] = 0.7
    first_transaction[8] = 100.0

    second_transaction = first_transaction.copy()
    second_transaction[4] = "11/11/2021"
    second_transaction[5] = "09/11/2021"

    raw = pd.DataFrame([
        [None] * 25,
        header,
        first_transaction,
        header,
        second_transaction,
    ])
    monkeypatch.setattr(pd, "read_excel", lambda *args, **kwargs: raw)

    source = tmp_path / "movimientos.xls"
    source.touch()
    result = parse_excel(Path(source))

    assert len(result) == 2
    assert result["transaction_date"].tolist() == [
        pd.Timestamp("2021-11-12").date(),
        pd.Timestamp("2021-11-11").date(),
    ]


def test_parse_excel_uses_negative_balance_column(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    """CaixaBank stores negative running balances in column 9 (Saldo -)."""
    header = [None] * 25
    header[4] = "F. Operación"
    header[5] = "F. Valor"
    header[8] = "Saldo (+)"
    header[9] = "Saldo (-)"

    positive_balance = [None] * 25
    positive_balance[1] = "2100 5602 67 1300043018"
    positive_balance[3] = "EUR"
    positive_balance[4] = "29/10/2021"
    positive_balance[5] = "29/10/2021"
    positive_balance[7] = 11.20
    positive_balance[8] = 1184.93

    negative_balance = positive_balance.copy()
    negative_balance[4] = "29/10/2021"
    negative_balance[7] = 36.36
    negative_balance[8] = None
    negative_balance[9] = -131.47

    raw = pd.DataFrame([[None] * 25, header, positive_balance,
                        negative_balance])
    monkeypatch.setattr(pd, "read_excel", lambda *args, **kwargs: raw)

    source = tmp_path / "movimientos.xls"
    source.touch()
    result = parse_excel(source)

    assert result["running_balance"].tolist() == [1184.93, -131.47]


def test_parse_excel_rounds_money_and_keeps_raw_details(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    header = [None] * 25
    header[4] = "F. Operación"

    row = [None] * 25
    row[1] = "2100 0000 00 0000000000"
    row[3] = "EUR"
    row[4] = "12/11/2021"
    row[7] = "12,339999999"
    row[8] = "100,004"
    row[14] = "Fecha de operación: 12-11-2021   MERCADONA"
    row[15] = "  TARJETA   1234 "
    row[16] = "COMPRA"

    raw = pd.DataFrame([header, row])
    monkeypatch.setattr(pd, "read_excel", lambda *args, **kwargs: raw)

    source = tmp_path / "movimientos.xls"
    source.touch()
    result = parse_excel(Path(source))

    assert result["amount"].tolist() == [-12.34]
    assert result["running_balance"].tolist() == [100.0]
    assert result["raw_details"].tolist() == ["TARJETA   1234 | COMPRA"]
    assert result["issuer_name"].tolist() == ["MERCADONA"]

```

## `tests/test_gui_smoke.py`

```python
"""
tests/test_gui_smoke.py
========================
Offscreen smoke test of the main shell: views are built lazily on first
navigation, the data views load from a throwaway database, and a theme
toggle reaches every built view. No real data folder is touched.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from datetime import date

os.environ.setdefault("QT_QPA_PLATFORM", "offscreen")

import pytest
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from app.auth.auth_service import create_user
from app.db.models import Account, Base, Transaction, TransactionType


@pytest.fixture
def qt_app():
    return QApplication.instance() or QApplication([])


@pytest.fixture
def session(tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'profin.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sess = sessionmaker(bind=engine)()
    yield sess
    sess.close()
    engine.dispose()


def test_main_window_builds_views_lazily_and_themes_them(qt_app, session, monkeypatch, caplog):
    from app.gui import main_window as main_window_module

    # Automatic backups would snapshot the real data folder.
    monkeypatch.setattr(main_window_module.MainWindow, "_run_auto_backup", lambda self: None)

    user = create_user(session, "ana", "secreto1")
    account = Account(user_id=user.id, account_name="Cuenta")
    session.add(account)
    session.flush()
    today = date.today()
    for amount in (-12.5, -7.25, 1500.0):
        session.add(Transaction(
            user_id=user.id, account_id=account.id, transaction_date=today,
            amount=amount,
            type=TransactionType.expense if amount < 0 else TransactionType.income,
            issuer_name="PRUEBA",
        ))
    session.commit()

    window = main_window_module.MainWindow(user=user, session=session, theme="light")
    window.show()  # as LoginWindow does after constructing it
    # Children added to an already visible window are shown by a queued call
    # on the next event-loop pass; the real app runs the loop, the test must.
    qt_app.processEvents()
    try:
        window._navigate("dashboard")
        assert set(window._views) == {"dashboard"}

        for key in ("expenses", "income", "transactions", "rules", "categories"):
            window._navigate(key)
            assert window._stack.currentWidget() is window._views[key]

        assert "import" not in window._views  # never visited → never built

        window._toggle_theme()
        for view in window._views.values():
            if hasattr(view, "_theme"):
                assert view._theme == window._theme

        window._navigate("unknown-view")
        assert window._current_view == "dashboard"

        # Views swallow load errors into the log; none may have happened.
        errors = [r.getMessage() for r in caplog.records if r.levelname in ("ERROR", "CRITICAL")]
        assert errors == []
        assert "2 transacciones" in window._views["expenses"]._lbl_count.text()
        assert "1 transacciones" in window._views["income"]._lbl_count.text()
    finally:
        window.close()
        window.deleteLater()

```

## `tests/test_import_pipeline.py`

```python
"""
tests/test_import_pipeline.py
==============================
End-to-end import of a real .xlsx laid out like a CaixaBank export:
parse → validate → duplicate check → commit → auto-categorize, then the
same file imported again must not create a single duplicate.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date

import pytest
from openpyxl import Workbook
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from app.auth.auth_service import create_user
from app.categorization.category_manager import (
    seed_default_categories,
    seed_default_rules,
)
from app.categorization.rules_engine import apply_rules_and_commit
from app.db.models import (
    CATEGORY_SOURCE_RULE, Account, AuditLog, Category, ImportBatch,
    ImportStatus, Transaction, TransactionType,
)
from app.db.models import Base
from app.ingestion.importer import commit_import, prepare_import
from app.ingestion.validator import ValidationError

IBAN = "ES12 2100 0000 0000 0000 0000"


def _row(fecha, ingreso=None, gasto=None, saldo_pos=None, saldo_neg=None,
         comun="044", issuer="", details=(), iban=IBAN):
    row = [None] * 24
    row[1] = iban
    row[3] = "EUR"
    row[4] = fecha
    row[5] = fecha
    row[6] = ingreso
    row[7] = gasto
    row[8] = saldo_pos
    row[9] = saldo_neg
    row[10] = comun
    row[11] = "000"
    row[12] = "000000000000"
    row[14] = issuer
    for offset, text in enumerate(details):
        row[15 + offset] = text
    return row


def _write_export(path, rows):
    wb = Workbook()
    ws = wb.active
    ws.append([None] * 24)
    ws.append([None, "MOVIMIENTOS DESDE 01/09/2026 HASTA 30/09/2026"] + [None] * 22)
    ws.append([None] * 24)
    # The real export labels every column, so the sheet is 24 columns wide
    # even when the trailing detail cells of every movement are empty.
    header = [f"Col {i}" for i in range(24)]
    header[4], header[5], header[6], header[7] = "F. Operación", "F. Valor", "Ingreso (+)", "Gasto (-)"
    ws.append(header)
    for row in rows:
        ws.append(row)
    wb.save(path)


EXPORT_ROWS = [
    _row("01/09/2026", gasto=45.3, saldo_pos=1954.7,
         issuer="Fecha de operación: 01-09-2026   IBERDROLA CLIENTES", details=("RECIBO LUZ",)),
    _row("02/09/2026", ingreso=1500.0, saldo_pos=3454.7, comun="012",
         issuer="MANPOWER TEAM E.T.T.", details=("NOMINA", "SEPTIEMBRE")),
    _row("03/09/2026", gasto=12.35, saldo_pos=3442.35, issuer="PANADERIA LOLA"),
    _row("04/09/2026", gasto=3500.0, saldo_neg=-57.65, issuer="TRANSFERENCIA A AHORRO"),
    _row("05/09/2026", gasto=0.0, saldo_neg=-57.65, comun="099", issuer="AJUSTE"),
]


@pytest.fixture
def session():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sess = sessionmaker(bind=engine)()
    yield sess
    sess.close()
    engine.dispose()


@pytest.fixture
def family(session):
    user = create_user(session, "maria", "secreto1")
    account = Account(user_id=user.id, account_name="CaixaBank Principal")
    session.add(account)
    session.commit()
    seed_default_categories(session, user.id)
    seed_default_rules(session, user.id)
    return user.id, account.id


def _import(path, session, user_id, account_id, decisions=None):
    """Mirror ImportView: clean rows are imported, review rows only if approved."""
    preview = prepare_import(path, session, user_id, account_id)
    chosen = {idx: True for idx in preview.duplicate_result.clean}
    chosen.update(decisions or {})
    summary = commit_import(preview, session, user_id, account_id, chosen)
    if summary.imported_ids:
        apply_rules_and_commit(session, summary.imported_ids, user_id)
    return preview, summary


def test_first_import_persists_categorizes_and_audits(tmp_path, session, family):
    user_id, account_id = family
    path = tmp_path / "movimientos.xlsx"
    _write_export(path, EXPORT_ROWS)

    preview, summary = _import(path, session, user_id, account_id)

    assert preview.validation_result.iban == IBAN
    assert [w.rule for w in preview.validation_result.warnings] == ["V-08"]
    assert summary.rows_imported == 5 and summary.rows_skipped == 0
    assert summary.status == ImportStatus.completed

    txns = {t.issuer_name: t for t in session.query(Transaction).all()}
    luz = txns["IBERDROLA CLIENTES"]
    assert luz.amount == -45.3 and luz.type == TransactionType.expense
    assert luz.transaction_date == date(2026, 9, 1)
    assert luz.raw_details == "RECIBO LUZ"
    assert luz.source_file == "movimientos.xlsx"
    assert session.get(Category, luz.category_id).name == "Luz"
    assert luz.category_source == CATEGORY_SOURCE_RULE

    nomina = txns["MANPOWER TEAM E.T.T."]
    assert nomina.amount == 1500.0 and nomina.type == TransactionType.income
    category = session.get(Category, nomina.category_id)
    assert (category.name, category.parent.name) == ("Nomina Maria", "INGRESO")

    assert txns["TRANSFERENCIA A AHORRO"].running_balance == -57.65
    assert txns["PANADERIA LOLA"].category_id is None
    assert txns["AJUSTE"].type == TransactionType.unknown

    audit_ids = {a.record_id for a in session.query(AuditLog).all()}
    assert audit_ids == {t.id for t in txns.values()}


def test_reimporting_the_same_file_creates_no_duplicates(tmp_path, session, family):
    user_id, account_id = family
    path = tmp_path / "movimientos.xlsx"
    _write_export(path, EXPORT_ROWS)
    _import(path, session, user_id, account_id)

    preview, summary = _import(path, session, user_id, account_id)

    dup = preview.duplicate_result
    assert sorted(dup.auto_skip) == [0, 1, 2, 3]
    assert dup.needs_review == [4]          # 0,00 € row: balance can't tell
    assert summary.rows_imported == 0
    assert session.query(Transaction).count() == 5
    assert session.query(ImportBatch).count() == 2


def test_overlapping_export_imports_only_new_rows(tmp_path, session, family):
    user_id, account_id = family
    first, second = tmp_path / "agosto.xlsx", tmp_path / "septiembre.xlsx"
    _write_export(first, EXPORT_ROWS[:3])
    _write_export(second, EXPORT_ROWS[1:4])
    _import(first, session, user_id, account_id)

    _, summary = _import(second, session, user_id, account_id)

    assert summary.rows_imported == 1
    assert summary.rows_skipped == 2
    assert session.query(Transaction).count() == 4


def test_file_with_two_accounts_is_rejected_before_any_write(tmp_path, session, family):
    user_id, account_id = family
    path = tmp_path / "mezcla.xlsx"
    _write_export(path, [EXPORT_ROWS[0], _row("02/09/2026", gasto=1.0, saldo_pos=1.0,
                                             issuer="X", iban="ES99 0000")])
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        prepare_import(path, session, user_id, account_id)
    assert session.query(Transaction).count() == 0

```

## `tests/test_importer.py`

```python
"""
tests/test_importer.py
=======================
commit_import(): persisted fields, audit trail, and duplicate decisions.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from app.auth.auth_service import create_user
from app.db.models import Account, AuditLog, Base, Transaction
from app.ingestion.duplicate_detector import DuplicateCheckResult
from app.ingestion.importer import ImportPreview, commit_import
from app.ingestion.validator import ValidationResult


@pytest.fixture
def db_session():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    session = sessionmaker(bind=engine)()
    yield session
    session.close()
    engine.dispose()


def _preview(rows: list[dict], duplicates: DuplicateCheckResult) -> ImportPreview:
    defaults = {
        "transaction_date": date(2026, 9, 1),
        "value_date":       date(2026, 9, 1),
        "amount":           -20.0,
        "running_balance":  500.0,
        "concepto_comun":   "044",
        "concepto_propio":  "000",
        "issuer_name":      "MERCADONA",
        "concept":          "MERCADONA | TARJETA",
        "raw_col14":        "MERCADONA",
        "raw_details":      "TARJETA   1234",
        "currency":         "EUR",
        "iban_raw":         "ES00",
    }
    df = pd.DataFrame([{**defaults, **row} for row in rows])
    return ImportPreview(
        df=df,
        validation_result=ValidationResult(row_count=len(df), iban="ES00"),
        duplicate_result=duplicates,
        file_path=Path("C:/banco/movimientos.xls"),
    )


def test_commit_import_persists_raw_details_and_audit_record_id(db_session):
    user = create_user(db_session, "ana", "secreto1")
    account = Account(user_id=user.id, account_name="Cuenta")
    db_session.add(account)
    db_session.commit()

    preview = _preview([{}], DuplicateCheckResult(clean=[0]))
    summary = commit_import(preview, db_session, user.id, account.id, {})

    txn = db_session.get(Transaction, summary.imported_ids[0])
    assert txn.raw_details == "TARJETA   1234"
    assert txn.source_file == "movimientos.xls"
    audit = db_session.query(AuditLog).one()
    assert audit.record_id == txn.id


def test_commit_import_skips_unresolved_review_rows(db_session):
    user = create_user(db_session, "ana", "secreto1")
    account = Account(user_id=user.id, account_name="Cuenta")
    db_session.add(account)
    db_session.commit()

    duplicates = DuplicateCheckResult(clean=[0], needs_review=[1, 2])
    preview = _preview([{}, {"amount": 0.0}, {"amount": 0.0}], duplicates)
    summary = commit_import(preview, db_session, user.id, account.id, {2: True})

    assert summary.rows_imported == 2
    assert summary.rows_skipped == 1
    flagged = db_session.get(Transaction, summary.imported_ids[1])
    assert flagged.is_duplicate_flagged is True

```

## `tests/test_period_summary.py`

```python
"""
tests/test_period_summary.py
=============================
SQL-side aggregates behind the Gastos / Ingresos views.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from app.analytics.period_summary import (
    UNCATEGORIZED_LABEL,
    category_totals,
    period_totals,
    summarize_period,
)
from app.auth.auth_service import create_user
from app.db.models import (
    Account, Base, Category, CategoryLevel, Transaction, TransactionType,
)


@pytest.fixture
def db_session():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    session = sessionmaker(bind=engine)()
    yield session
    session.close()
    engine.dispose()


@pytest.fixture
def family(db_session):
    ana = create_user(db_session, "ana", "secreto1")
    otro = create_user(db_session, "otro", "secreto1")
    accounts = {}
    for user in (ana, otro):
        account = Account(user_id=user.id, account_name="Cuenta")
        db_session.add(account)
        db_session.flush()
        accounts[user.id] = account.id
    gasto = Category(user_id=ana.id, name="GASTO", level=CategoryLevel.type,
                     transaction_type=TransactionType.expense)
    db_session.add(gasto)
    db_session.flush()
    super_ = Category(user_id=ana.id, parent_id=gasto.id, name="Supermercados",
                      level=CategoryLevel.category)
    luz = Category(user_id=ana.id, parent_id=gasto.id, name="Luz",
                   level=CategoryLevel.category)
    db_session.add_all([super_, luz])
    db_session.commit()

    def add(user, day, amount, category=None, issuer="X"):
        db_session.add(Transaction(
            user_id=user.id, account_id=accounts[user.id],
            transaction_date=day, amount=amount,
            type=TransactionType.expense if amount < 0 else TransactionType.income,
            category_id=category.id if category else None, issuer_name=issuer,
        ))

    add(ana, date(2026, 8, 30), -10.10, super_, "MERCADONA")
    add(ana, date(2026, 9, 1), -20.20, super_, "LIDL")
    add(ana, date(2026, 9, 1), -30.00, luz, "IBERDROLA")
    add(ana, date(2026, 9, 15), -5.05, None, "BAR")
    add(ana, date(2026, 9, 15), 1500.00, None, "NOMINA")   # income, excluded
    add(otro, date(2026, 9, 1), -999.0, None, "OTHER USER")  # other user, excluded
    db_session.commit()
    return ana


def test_summarize_period_totals_and_kpis(db_session, family):
    summary = summarize_period(
        db_session, family.id, TransactionType.expense,
        date(2026, 8, 1), date(2026, 9, 30),
    )
    assert summary.total == 65.35
    assert summary.count == 4
    assert summary.max_amount == 30.0
    assert summary.categorized == 3
    assert summary.average == round(65.35 / 4, 2)


def test_summarize_period_groupings(db_session, family):
    summary = summarize_period(
        db_session, family.id, TransactionType.expense,
        date(2026, 8, 1), date(2026, 9, 30),
    )
    assert summary.by_category == [
        ("Supermercados", 30.3), ("Luz", 30.0), (UNCATEGORIZED_LABEL, 5.05),
    ]
    assert [month for month, _ in summary.by_month_category] == [
        date(2026, 8, 1), date(2026, 9, 1),
    ]
    assert summary.by_month_category[1][1][0] == ("Luz", 30.0)
    # Chronological across months (was sorted as "dd/mm" text before).
    assert [day for day, _ in summary.by_day] == [
        date(2026, 8, 30), date(2026, 9, 1), date(2026, 9, 15),
    ]
    assert summary.by_day[1] == (date(2026, 9, 1), 50.2)
    assert [row.description for row in summary.latest] == [
        "BAR", "IBERDROLA", "LIDL", "MERCADONA",
    ]
    assert summary.latest[0].category is None


def test_period_totals_and_category_totals_for_income(db_session, family):
    assert period_totals(
        db_session, family.id, TransactionType.income,
        date(2026, 9, 1), date(2026, 9, 30),
    ) == (1500.0, 1)
    assert category_totals(
        db_session, family.id, TransactionType.income,
        date(2026, 9, 1), date(2026, 9, 30),
    ) == [(UNCATEGORIZED_LABEL, 1500.0)]


def test_empty_period(db_session, family):
    summary = summarize_period(
        db_session, family.id, TransactionType.expense,
        date(2020, 1, 1), date(2020, 1, 31),
    )
    assert (summary.total, summary.count, summary.by_day, summary.latest) == (0.0, 0, [], [])
    assert summary.categorized_pct == 0.0

```

## `tests/test_release_tooling.py`

```python
"""
tests/test_release_tooling.py
==============================
Release pipeline guarantees:
  - ExportVersionHandler.export_version() runs without prompts and writes the
    full release notes into CHANGELOG.md (never loses older entries).
  - tools/release_tools.py collects every change since the last tag, refuses
    notes that miss a commit / area / schema migration, packages the whole
    one-dir build, and detects a GitHub Release that differs from the notes.
  - Invariants of this repository that a release relies on.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
import subprocess
import sys
import zipfile
from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

import tools.ExportVersionHandler as evh
import tools.release_tools as rt

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent

CHANGELOG_SEED = """# FAMILYFIN — Changelog

> Auto-generated by `tools/ExportVersionHandler.py`

---

**Snapshot:** `FAMILYFIN.R.0.3.0.md`  |  **Files:** 1  |  **Handler:** v1.0.0
**Date:** 2026-09-16 01:19  |  **Type:** Release
**Description:** Entrada anterior.
"""

NOTES = """### Resumen

Esta versión protege las categorías puestas a mano y hace copias de seguridad automáticas.

### Novedades

- Copias de seguridad automáticas.

### Áreas afectadas

- **Base de datos y migraciones**: `app/db/database.py`
"""


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# ExportVersionHandler
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@pytest.fixture
def evh_root(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    (tmp_path / "app").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / "app" / "config.py").write_text(
        'APP_NAME    = "FAMILYFIN"\nAPP_VERSION = "0.3.0"\nAPP_RELEASE_DATE = "2026-09-16"\n',
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    (tmp_path / "main.py").write_text("print('hola')\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / "CHANGELOG.md").write_text(CHANGELOG_SEED, encoding="utf-8")
    monkeypatch.setattr(evh, "ROOT", tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(evh, "VERSIONS_DIR", tmp_path / "versions")
    monkeypatch.setattr("builtins.input", lambda *a: pytest.fail("export_version prompted"))
    return tmp_path


def test_export_version_is_non_interactive_and_writes_full_notes(evh_root):
    summary, body = rt.split_notes(NOTES)
    evh.export_version(version="0.4.0", ver_type="R", also_prj=True,
                       description=summary, notes=body)

    config = (evh_root / "app" / "config.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert 'APP_VERSION = "0.4.0"' in config
    assert f'APP_RELEASE_DATE = "{date.today().isoformat()}"' in config
    assert (evh_root / "versions" / "FAMILYFIN.R.0.4.0.md").is_file()
    assert (evh_root / "versions" / "FAMILYFIN.R.0.4.0.prj").is_file()

    changelog = (evh_root / "CHANGELOG.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    entries = changelog.split("\n---\n")
    assert entries[0].startswith("# FAMILYFIN — Changelog")
    assert "`FAMILYFIN.R.0.4.0.md`" in entries[1]
    assert f"**Description:** {summary}" in entries[1]
    assert "- Copias de seguridad automáticas." in entries[1]
    assert "`FAMILYFIN.R.0.3.0.md`" in entries[2]   # older entry kept, below


def test_export_version_rejects_notes_with_separator_before_writing(evh_root):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        evh.export_version(version="0.4.0", ver_type="R", also_prj=False,
                           description="x", notes="- uno\n---\n- dos")
    assert 'APP_VERSION = "0.3.0"' in (evh_root / "app" / "config.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert (evh_root / "CHANGELOG.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8") == CHANGELOG_SEED


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# release_tools on a throwaway git repository
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _git(root: Path, *args: str) -> str:
    return subprocess.run(
        ["git", *args], cwd=root, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True
    ).stdout


DB_V1 = "SCHEMA_MIGRATIONS = (\n    (1, _migration_1_category_source),\n)\n"
DB_V3 = (
    "SCHEMA_MIGRATIONS = (\n    (1, _migration_1_category_source),\n"
    "    (2, _migration_2_indexes_and_cents),\n    (3, _migration_3_fix),\n)\n"
)


@pytest.fixture
def repo(tmp_path):
    root = tmp_path / "repo"
    (root / "app" / "db").mkdir(parents=True)
    (root / "app" / "gui").mkdir()
    (root / "docs" / "agent").mkdir(parents=True)
    _git(root, "init", "-q", "-b", "main")
    _git(root, "config", "user.email", "t@example.com")
    _git(root, "config", "user.name", "Test")
    _git(root, "config", "commit.gpgsign", "false")
    (root / "app" / "config.py").write_text('APP_VERSION = "0.3.0"\n', encoding="utf-8")
    (root / "app" / "db" / "database.py").write_text(DB_V1, encoding="utf-8")
    (root / "app" / "gui" / "view.py").write_text("x = 1\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (root / "docs" / "agent" / "agent_log.md").write_text(
        "## 2026-09-01 — Old work\n", encoding="utf-8")
    _git(root, "add", ".")
    _git(root, "commit", "-q", "-m", "chore(release): v0.3.0")
    _git(root, "tag", "-a", "v0.3.0", "-m", "v0.3.0")

    (root / "app" / "db" / "database.py").write_text(DB_V3, encoding="utf-8")
    _git(root, "commit", "-qam", "feat(db): migrations v2 and v3")
    (root / "app" / "gui" / "view.py").write_text("x = 2\n", encoding="utf-8")
    _git(root, "commit", "-qam", "fix: dashboard crash")
    (root / "app" / "config.py").write_text('APP_VERSION = "0.3.1"\n', encoding="utf-8")
    _git(root, "commit", "-qam", "chore(release): v0.3.1")
    with (root / "docs" / "agent" / "agent_log.md").open("a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        fh.write("\n## 2099-01-01 — New work\n")
    _git(root, "commit", "-qam", "docs: log")
    return root


def test_collect_changes_since_last_tag(repo):
    changes = rt.collect_changes(repo)

    assert changes.since_tag == "v0.3.0"
    subjects = [c.subject for c in changes.commits]
    assert subjects == ["docs: log", "fix: dashboard crash", "feat(db): migrations v2 and v3"]
    assert (changes.schema_from, changes.schema_to) == (1, 3)
    # config.py only had its version bumped → not a change of the "Interfaz" area
    assert set(changes.files_by_area) == {"db", "gui", "docs"}
    assert changes.files_by_area["gui"] == ["app/gui/view.py"]
    assert changes.log_entries == ["2099-01-01 — New work"]


def test_draft_contains_every_fact_and_is_not_publishable(repo):
    changes = rt.collect_changes(repo)
    draft = rt.draft_notes(changes, "0.4.0")

    for commit in changes.commits:
        assert commit.sha in draft
    assert rt.migration_sentence(1, 3) in draft
    for label in ("Base de datos y migraciones", "Interfaz", "Documentación"):
        assert label in draft
    assert rt.verify_notes(draft, changes)   # TODOs and comments left → refused


def test_verify_notes_accepts_complete_notes_and_names_what_is_missing(repo):
    changes = rt.collect_changes(repo)
    good = rt.draft_notes(changes, "0.4.0")
    good = re.sub(r"<!--.*?-->\n?", "", good, flags=re.DOTALL)
    good = good.replace(
        "TODO: 2-4 frases sobre qué cambia al actualizar a 0.4.0.",
        "La base de datos se repara sola y el panel principal ya no se cierra con error.",
    )
    good = re.sub(r"- TODO: (.*) \(`(\w+)`\)", r"- \1 (`\2`)", good)
    assert rt.verify_notes(good, changes) == []

    missing = good.replace(changes.commits[0].sha, "xxxxxxx").replace("**Interfaz**", "**UI**")
    missing = missing.replace(rt.migration_sentence(1, 3), "Se migra la base.")
    problems = "\n".join(rt.verify_notes(missing, changes))
    assert changes.commits[0].sha in problems
    assert "Interfaz" in problems
    assert "v1 a v3" in problems


def test_split_notes_separates_summary():
    summary, body = rt.split_notes(NOTES)
    assert summary.startswith("Esta versión protege")
    assert "### Resumen" not in body
    assert body.startswith("### Novedades")


def test_area_mapping_and_ignored_paths():
    assert rt.area_for("app/db/database.py").key == "db"
    assert rt.area_for("app/analytics/period_summary.py").key == "analytics"
    assert rt.area_for("tests/test_x.py").user_facing is False
    assert rt.area_for("CHANGELOG.md") is None
    assert rt.area_for("versions/FAMILYFIN.R.0.3.0.md") is None


def test_parse_version_orders_betas_before_final():
    assert rt.parse_version("0.4.0-beta.1") < rt.parse_version("0.4.0")
    assert rt.parse_version("0.4.0") > rt.parse_version("0.3.9")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        rt.parse_version("v0.4")


def _fake_build(root: Path, complete: bool = True) -> Path:
    folder = rt.build_dir("0.4.0", "R", root)
    (folder / "_internal" / "PySide6").mkdir(parents=True)
    (folder / "PROFIN.R.0.4.0.exe").write_bytes(b"MZ")
    (folder / "_internal" / "PySide6" / "Qt6Core.dll").write_bytes(b"dll")
    if complete:
        (folder.parent / "PROFIN.R.0.4.0.build-ok").write_text("{}", encoding="utf-8")
    return folder


def test_package_build_zips_exe_and_internal_folder(tmp_path):
    _fake_build(tmp_path)

    target = rt.package_build("0.4.0", "R", tmp_path)

    with zipfile.ZipFile(target) as archive:
        names = set(archive.namelist())
    assert "PROFIN.R.0.4.0/PROFIN.R.0.4.0.exe" in names
    assert "PROFIN.R.0.4.0/_internal/PySide6/Qt6Core.dll" in names


def test_package_build_refuses_a_build_that_did_not_finish(tmp_path):
    _fake_build(tmp_path, complete=False)   # e.g. PyInstaller crashed in COLLECT
    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError, match="build-ok"):
        rt.package_build("0.4.0", "R", tmp_path)


def test_package_build_refuses_exe_without_internal(tmp_path):
    folder = rt.build_dir("0.4.0", "R", tmp_path)
    folder.mkdir(parents=True)
    (folder / "PROFIN.R.0.4.0.exe").write_bytes(b"MZ")
    (folder.parent / "PROFIN.R.0.4.0.build-ok").write_text("{}", encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError):
        rt.package_build("0.4.0", "R", tmp_path)


def _released_repo(repo: Path, notes: str) -> None:
    """Simulate steps 4-6 of the skill on the throwaway repo."""
    summary, body = rt.split_notes(notes)
    (repo / "app" / "config.py").write_text(
        f'APP_VERSION = "0.4.0"\nAPP_RELEASE_DATE = "{date.today().isoformat()}"\n', encoding="utf-8")
    (repo / "versions").mkdir()
    for suffix in (".md", ".prj"):
        (repo / "versions" / f"FAMILYFIN.R.0.4.0{suffix}").write_text("x", encoding="utf-8")
    (repo / "CHANGELOG.md").write_text(
        "# FAMILYFIN — Changelog\n\n---\n\n**Snapshot:** `FAMILYFIN.R.0.4.0.md`\n"
        f"**Description:** {summary}\n\n{body}\n", encoding="utf-8")
    _git(repo, "add", ".")
    _git(repo, "commit", "-qm", "chore(release): v0.4.0")
    notes_file = repo.parent / "notes.md"
    notes_file.write_text(notes, encoding="utf-8")
    _git(repo, "tag", "-a", "v0.4.0", "--cleanup=verbatim", "-F", str(notes_file))
    zip_path = rt.build_zip("0.4.0", "R", repo)
    zip_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
    zip_path.write_bytes(b"PK")


def test_postflight_checks_changelog_tag_package_and_github_release(repo):
    _released_repo(repo, NOTES)
    assert rt.postflight("0.4.0", "R", NOTES, root=repo) == []

    release = {
        "tagName": "v0.4.0", "body": NOTES, "isDraft": False, "isPrerelease": False,
        "assets": [{"name": "PROFIN.R.0.4.0-windows.zip"}],
    }
    import json
    ok_runner = lambda args: json.dumps(release)
    assert rt.postflight("0.4.0", "R", NOTES, root=repo, published=True, runner=ok_runner) == []

    bad = {**release, "body": "Otra cosa", "assets": [{"name": "PROFIN.R.0.4.0.exe"}]}
    problems = rt.postflight("0.4.0", "R", NOTES, root=repo, published=True,
                             runner=lambda args: json.dumps(bad))
    assert any("texto de la release" in p for p in problems)
    assert any("windows.zip" in p for p in problems)


def test_postflight_detects_tag_message_stripped_of_headings(repo):
    _released_repo(repo, NOTES)
    _git(repo, "tag", "-d", "v0.4.0")
    notes_file = repo.parent / "notes.md"
    _git(repo, "tag", "-a", "v0.4.0", "-F", str(notes_file))   # default cleanup=strip
    problems = rt.postflight("0.4.0", "R", NOTES, root=repo)
    assert any("mensaje del tag" in p for p in problems)


def test_postflight_detects_changelog_without_notes(repo):
    _released_repo(repo, NOTES)
    (repo / "CHANGELOG.md").write_text(
        "# FAMILYFIN — Changelog\n\n---\n\n**Snapshot:** `FAMILYFIN.R.0.4.0.md`\n"
        "**Description:** (no description)\n", encoding="utf-8")
    problems = rt.postflight("0.4.0", "R", NOTES, root=repo)
    assert any("resumen" in p for p in problems)
    assert any("cuerpo" in p for p in problems)
```
